Āpastamba Dharmasūtra

Original BSS text input Y. Ikari; compared with KSS ed. by K. Kano; March 1992. 
Converted to Unicode plain text without hyphens, sandhi resolution, and analytical markers by Timothy Lubin, October 2021.

Text based on Bühler's edition (Bombay Sanskrit Series, Nos. LIV and L, 3rd ed 1932). Variant readings adopted in the edition of A. Chinnaswami (Kashi Sanskrit Series, No. 93, Benares 1932) [= K], are included in brackets, usually after the end of each sūtra. Differences of sandhi are ignored and clear misprints are corrected.

ĀpDhS 1.1.1.1  athātas sāmayācārikān dharmān vyākhyāsyāmaḥ | 
ĀpDhS 1.1.1.2  dharmajñasamayaḥ pramāṇam | 
ĀpDhS 1.1.1.3  vedāś ca |
ĀpDhS 1.1.1.4  catvāro varṇo brāhmaṇakṣatriyavaiśyaśūdrāḥ | 
ĀpDhS 1.1.1.5  teṣāṃ pūrvas pūrvo janmataḥ śreyān | 
ĀpDhS 1.1.1.6  aśūdrāṇām aduṣṭakarmaṇām upāyanaṃ vedādhyayanam agnyādheyaṃ phalavanti ca karmāṇi | 
ĀpDhS 1.1.1.7  śuśrūṣā śūdrasyetareṣāṃ varṇānām | 
ĀpDhS 1.1.1.8  pūrvasmin pūrvasmin varṇe niḥśreyasam bhūyaḥ | 
ĀpDhS 1.1.1.9  upanayanaṃ vidyārthasya śrutitaḥ saṃskāraḥ | 
ĀpDhS 1.1.1.10  sarvebhyo vedebhyaḥ sāvitry anūcyata iti hi brāhmaṇam | 
ĀpDhS 1.1.1.11  tamasas vai eṣa tamaḥ praviśati yam avidvān upanayate yaś cāvidvān iti hi brāhmanam |
ĀpDhS 1.1.1.12  tasminn abhijanavidyāsamudetam saṃskartāram |
ĀpDhS 1.1.1.13  tasmiṃś caiva vidyākarmāntam avipratipanne dharmebhyaḥ | 
ĀpDhS 1.1.1.14  yasmād dharmān ācinoti sa ācāryaḥ | 
ĀpDhS 1.1.1.15  tasmai na druhyet kadā cana | 
ĀpDhS 1.1.1.16  sa hi vidyātas taṃ janayati | 
ĀpDhS 1.1.1.17  tac chreṣṭhaṃ janma | 
ĀpDhS 1.1.1.18  śarīram eva mātāpitarau janayatas |
ĀpDhS 1.1.1.19  vasante brāhmaṇam upanayīta, grīṣme rājanyaṃ, śaradi vaiśyaṃ garbhāṣṭameṣu brāhmaṇaṃ garbhaikādaśeṣu rājanyaṃ garbhadvādaśeṣu vaiśyam |
ĀpDhS 1.1.1.20  atha kāmyāni | 
ĀpDhS 1.1.1.21-26  saptame brahmavarcasakāmam, aṣṭama āyuṣkāmam, navame tejaskāmam, daśame 'nnādyakāmam, ekādaśa' indriyakāmam, dvādaśe paśukāmam |
ĀpDhS 1.1.1.27  ā ṣoḍaśād brāhmaṇasyānātyaya, ā dvāviṃśāt kṣatriyasyā caturviṃśād vaiśyasya yathā vrateṣu samarthaḥ syātyāni vakṣyāmas |
ĀpDhS 1.1.1.28  atikrānte sāvitryāḥ kāla ṛtuṃ traividyakaṃ brahmacaryaṃ caret | [K omits: kāla]
ĀpDhS 1.1.1.29  athopanayanam | 
ĀpDhS 1.1.1.30  tataḥ saṃvatsaram udakopasparśanam | 
ĀpDhS 1.1.1.31  athādhyāpyaḥ | 
ĀpDhS 1.1.1.32  atha yasya pitā pitāmaha iti anupetau syātāṃ te brahmahasaṃstutāḥ | 
ĀpDhS 1.1.1.33  teṣām abhyāgamanaṃ bhojanaṃ vivāham iti ca varjayet |
ĀpDhS 1.1.1.34  teṣām icchatāṃ prāyaścittam | 
ĀpDhS 1.1.1.35  yathā prathame 'tikrama ṛtur evaṃ saṃvatsaraḥ | 
ĀpDhS 1.1.1.36  athopanayanaṃ tata udakopasparśanam | 

ĀpDhS 1.1.2.1  pratipūrusaṃ saṃkhyāya saṃvatsarān yāvanto 'nupetāḥ syuḥ | 
ĀpDhS 1.1.2.2  saptabhiḥ pāvamānībhir "yad anti yac ca dūraka" iti etābhir yajuspavitreṇa sāmapavitreṇāṅgiraseṇeti |
ĀpDhS 1.1.2.3  api vā vyāhṛtībhir eva | [K: vyāhṛtibhir]
ĀpDhS 1.1.2.4  athādhyāpyaḥ | 
ĀpDhS 1.1.2.5  atha yasya prapitāmahādi nānusmaryata upanayanaṃ te śmaśānasaṃstutāḥ | 
ĀpDhS 1.1.2.6  teṣām abhyāgamanaṃ bhojanaṃ vivāham iti ca varjayet teṣām icchatāṃ prāyaścittaṃ dvādaśavarṣāṇi traividyakaṃ brahmacaryaṃ cared | athopanayanaṃ tatas udakopasparśanaṃ pāvamānyādibhiḥ ||
ĀpDhS 1.1.2.7  atha gṛhamedhopadeśanam |
ĀpDhS 1.1.2.8  nādhyāpanam |
ĀpDhS 1.1.2.9  tato yo nirvartate tasya saṃskāro yathā prathame' atikrame | [K: nivartate] 
ĀpDhS 1.1.2.10  tata ūrdhvaṃ prakṛtivat |
ĀpDhS 1.1.2.11  upetasyācāryakule brahmacārivāsaḥ | 
ĀpDhS 1.1.2.12  aṣṭācatvāriṃśad varṣāṇi |
ĀpDhS 1.1.2.13-15  pādūnam, ardhena, tribhir vā | [*var: pāda ūnam]
ĀpDhS 1.1.2.16  dvādaśāvarārdhyam |
ĀpDhS 1.1.2.17-18  na brahmacāriṇo vidyārthasya paropavāso 'sti |
[ = K 17, K 18: atha brahmacaryavidhiḥ]
ĀpDhS 1.1.2.19  ācāryādhīnaḥ syād anyatra patanīyebhyaḥ |
ĀpDhS 1.1.2.20  hitakārī guror apratilomayan vācā |
ĀpDhS 1.1.2.21  adhāsanaśāyī |
ĀpDhS 1.1.2.22  nānudeśyaṃ bhuñjīta |
ĀpDhS 1.1.2.23  tathā kṣāralavaṇamadhumāṃsāni |
ĀpDhS 1.1.2.24  adivāsvāpī |
ĀpDhS 1.1.2.25  agandhasevī |
ĀpDhS 1.1.2.26  maithunaṃ na caret |
ĀpDhS 1.1.2.27  utsannaślāghaḥ |
ĀpDhS 1.1.2.28  aṅgāni na prakṣālayīta |
ĀpDhS 1.1.2.29  prakṣālayīta tv aśuciliptāni guror asaṃdarśe |
ĀpDhS 1.1.2.30  nāpsu ślāghamānaḥ snāyād yadi snāyād daṇḍavat plavet |
ĀpDhS 1.1.2.31-32  jaṭilaḥ, śikhājaṭas vā vāpayed itarān |
ĀpDhS 1.1.2.33  mauñjī mekhalā trivṛd brāhmaṇasya śaktiviṣaye dakṣiṇāvṛttānām |
ĀpDhS 1.1.2.34  jyā rājanyasya |
ĀpDhS 1.1.2.35  mauñjī vāyomiśrā |
ĀpDhS 1.1.2.36  āvīsūtraṃ vaiśyasya |
ĀpDhS 1.1.2.37  sairī tāmalī vety eke |
ĀpDhS 1.1.2.38  pālāśo daṇḍo brāhmaṇasya, naiyyagrodhaskandhajo 'vāṅgro rājanyasya, bādara audumbaro vā vaiśyasya, vārkṣo daṇḍa ity avarṇasaṃyogenaika upadiśanti |
ĀpDhS 1.1.2.39  vāsaḥ |
ĀpDhS 1.1.2.40  śāṇīkṣaumājināni |
ĀpDhS 1.1.2.41  kaṣāyaṃ caike vastram upadiśanti |

ĀpDhS 1.1.3.1  māñjiṣṭhaṃ rājanyasya |
ĀpDhS 1.1.3.2  hāridraṃ vaiśyasya |
ĀpDhS 1.1.3.3  hāriṇam aiṇeyaṃ vā kṛṣṇaṃ brāhmaṇasya |
ĀpDhS 1.1.3.4  kṛṣṇaṃ ced anupastīrṇāsanaśāyī syāt |
ĀpDhS 1.1.3.5  rauravaṃ rājanyasya |
ĀpDhS 1.1.3.6  bastājinaṃ vaiśyasya |
ĀpDhS 1.1.3.7-8  āvikaṃ sārvavarṇikam, kambalaś ca |
ĀpDhS 1.1.3.9  brahmavṛddhim icchann ajināny eva vasīta, kṣatravṛddhim icchan vastrāṇy evobhayavṛddhim icchann ubhayam iti hi brāhmaṇam |
ĀpDhS 1.1.3.10  ajinaṃ tv evottaraṃ dhārayet |
ĀpDhS 1.1.3.11  anṛttadarśī |
ĀpDhS 1.1.3.12  sabhāḥ samājāṃś cāgantā |
ĀpDhS 1.1.3.13  ajanavādaśīlaḥ |
ĀpDhS 1.1.3.14  rahasśīlaḥ |
ĀpDhS 1.1.3.15  guror udācāreṣv akartā svairikarmāṇi |
ĀpDhS 1.1.3.16  strībhir yāvadarthasaṃbhāṣī |
ĀpDhS 1.1.3.17-24  mṛduḥ, śāntaḥ, dāntaḥ, hrīmān, dṛḍhadhṛtiḥ, aglāṃsnuḥ, akrodhanaḥ, anasūyuḥ |
ĀpDhS 1.1.3.25  sarvaṃ lābham āharan gurave sāyaṃ prātar amantreṇa bhikṣācaryaṃ cared, bhikṣamāṇo 'nyatrāpapātrebhyo 'bhiśastāc ca | [K: amatreṇa]
ĀpDhS 1.1.3.26a  strīṇāṃ pratyācakṣāṇānāṃ samāhito brahmacārīṣṭaṃ dattaṃ hutaṃ prajāṃ paśūn brahmavarcasam annādyaṃ vṛṅkte |
ĀpDhS 1.1.3.26b  tasmād u ha vai brahmacārisamghaṃ carantaṃ na pratyācakṣīta, api haiṣv evamvidha evaṃvrataḥ syād iti hi brāhmaṇam |
ĀpDhS 1.1.3.27  nānumānena bhaikṣam ucchiṣṭaṃ dṛṣṭaśrutābhyāṃ tu |
ĀpDhS 1.1.3.28  bhavatpūrvayā brāhmaṇo bhikṣet | [K: bhikṣeta]
ĀpDhS 1.1.3.29  bhavatmadhyayā rājanyaḥ |
ĀpDhS 1.1.3.30  bhavatantyayā vaiśyaḥ |
ĀpDhS 1.1.3.31  tat samāhṛtyopanidhāyācāryāya prabrūyāt |
ĀpDhS 1.1.3.32  tena pradiṣṭaṃ bhuñjīta |
ĀpDhS 1.1.3.33  vipravāse guror ācāryakulāya |
ĀpDhS 1.1.3.34  tair vipravāse 'nyebhyo 'pi śrotriyebhyaḥ |
ĀpDhS 1.1.3.35  nātmaprayojanaś caret |
ĀpDhS 1.1.3.36  bhuktvā svayam amatraṃ prakṣālayīta |
ĀpDhS 1.1.3.37  na cocchiṣṭaṃ kuryāt |
ĀpDhS 1.1.3.38  aśaktau bhūmau nikhanet |
ĀpDhS 1.1.3.39  apsu vā praveśayet |
ĀpDhS 1.1.3.40  āryāya vā paryavadadhyāt |
ĀpDhS 1.1.3.41  antardhine vā śūdrāya |
ĀpDhS 1.1.3.42  proṣito bhaikṣād agnau kṛtvā bhuñjīta |
ĀpDhS 1.1.3.43  bhaikṣaṃ haviṣā saṃstutaṃ tatrācāryo devatārthe |
ĀpDhS 1.1.3.44  āhavanīyārthe ca |
ĀpDhS 1.1.3.45  taṃ bhojayitvā |

ĀpDhS 1.1.4.1  yad ucchiṣṭaṃ prāśñāti |
ĀpDhS 1.1.4.2  havirucchiṣṭam eva tat |
ĀpDhS 1.1.4.3  yad anyāni dravyāṇi yathālābham upaharati dakṣiṇā eva tāḥ |
ĀpDhS 1.1.4.4  sa eṣa brahmacāriṇo yajño nityapratataḥ |
ĀpDhS 1.1.4.5  na cāsmai |
ĀpDhS 1.1.4.6  yathā kṣāralavaṇamadhumāṃsānīti |
ĀpDhS 1.1.4.7  etenānye niyamā vyākhyātāḥ |
ĀpDhS 1.1.4.8  śrutir hi balīyasy ānumānikād ācārāt |
ĀpDhS 1.1.4.9  dṛśyate cāpi pravṛttikāraṇam |
ĀpDhS 1.1.4.10  prītir hy upalabhyate |
ĀpDhS 1.1.4.11  pitur jyeṣṭhasya ca bhrātur ucchiṣṭaṃ bhoktavyam |
ĀpDhS 1.1.4.12  dharmavipratipattāv abhojyam |
ĀpDhS 1.1.4.13  sāyaṃ prātar udakumbham āharet |
ĀpDhS 1.1.4.14  sadāraṇyād edhān āhṛtya adho nidadhyāt |
ĀpDhS 1.1.4.15  nāstamite samiddhāro gacchet |
ĀpDhS 1.1.4.16  agnim iddhvā parisamūhya samidha ādadhyāt sāyaṃ prātar yathopadeśam |
ĀpDhS 1.1.4.17  sāyam evāgnipūjety eke |
ĀpDhS 1.1.4.18  samiddham agniṃ pāṇinā parisamūhen na samūhanyā |
ĀpDhS 1.1.4.19  prāk tu yāthākāmī |
ĀpDhS 1.1.4.20  nāgnyudakaśeṣeṇa vṛthākarmāṇi kurvītācāmed vā |
ĀpDhS 1.1.4.21  pāṇisaṃkṣubdhenodakenaikapāṇyāvarjitena ca nācāmet |
ĀpDhS 1.1.4.22  svapnaṃ ca varjayet |
ĀpDhS 1.1.4.23  athāharahar ācāryaṃ gopāyed dharmārthayuktaiḥ karmabhiḥ |
ĀpDhS 1.1.4.24  sa guptvā saṃviśan brūyād dharmagopāyam ājūgupam aham iti |
ĀpDhS 1.1.4.25  pramādād ācāryasya buddhipūrvaṃ vā niyamātikramaṃ rahasi bodhayet |
ĀpDhS 1.1.4.26-27  anivṛttau svayaṃ karmāṇy ārabheta, nivartayed vā |
ĀpDhS 1.1.4.28  atha yaḥ pūrvotthāyī jaghanyasaṃveśī tam āhur na svapitīti |
ĀpDhS 1.1.4.29  sa ya evaṃ praṇihitātmā brahmacāry atraivāsya sarvāṇi karmāṇi phalavanty avāptāni bhavanti yāny api gṛhamedhe |

ĀpDhS 1.2.5.1  niyameṣu tapasśabdaḥ |
ĀpDhS 1.2.5.2  tadatikrame vidyākarma niḥsravati brahma sahāpatyād etasmāt |
ĀpDhS 1.2.5.3  kartapatyam* anāyuṣyaṃ ca | [*var gartapatyam]
ĀpDhS 1.2.5.4  tasmād ṛṣayo 'vareṣu na jāyante niyamātikramāt |
ĀpDhS 1.2.5.5-6  śrutaṛṣayas tu bhavanti kecit karmaphalaśeṣeṇa punaḥsaṃbhave, yathā śvetaketuḥ |
ĀpDhS 1.2.5.7  yat kiṃ ca samāhito brahmāpy ācāryād upayuṅkte brahmavad eva tasmin phalaṃ bhavati |
ĀpDhS 1.2.5.8  atho yat kiñ ca manasā vācā cakṣuṣā vā saṅkalpan dhyāyaty āhaabhivipaśyati vā tathaiva tad bhavatīty upadiśanti | [K: saṅkalpayan]
ĀpDhS 1.2.5.9  guruprasādanīyāni karmāṇi svastyayanam adhyayanasaṃvṛttir iti |
ĀpDhS 1.2.5.10  ato 'nyāni nivartante brahmacāriṇaḥ karmāṇi |
ĀpDhS 1.2.5.11  svādhyāyadhṛg dharmarucis tapsvy ṛjur mṛduḥ sidhyati brahmacārī | [K: ajur]
ĀpDhS 1.2.5.12  sadā mahāntam apararātram utthāya guros tiṣṭhan prātarabhivādam abhivādayītaasāv ahaṃ bho iti | [K: abhivādanam]
ĀpDhS 1.2.5.13  samānagrāme ca vasatām anyeṣām api vṛddhatarāṇāṃ prāk prātarāśāt |
ĀpDhS 1.2.5.14  proṣya ca samāgame |
ĀpDhS 1.2.5.15  svargam āyuś caīpsan |
ĀpDhS 1.2.5.16  dakṣiṇam bāhuṃ śrotrasamaṃ prasārya brāhmaṇo 'bhivādayītoraḥsamaṃ rājanyo madhyasamaṃ vaiśyo nīcaiḥ śūdraḥ prañjalim |
ĀpDhS 1.2.5.17  plāvanaṃ ca nāmno 'bhivādanapratyabhivādane ca pūrveṣāṃ varṇānām |
ĀpDhS 1.2.5.18  udite tv āditya ācāryeṇa sametyaupasaṅgrahaṇam |
ĀpDhS 1.2.5.19  sadaivābhivādanam |
ĀpDhS 1.2.5.20  upasaṃgrāhya ācārya ity eke |
ĀpDhS 1.2.5.21  dakṣiṇena pāṇinā daksiṇaṃ pādam adhastād abhy adhimṛśya sakuṣṭhikam upasaṃgṛhṇīyāt |
ĀpDhS 1.2.5.22  ubhābhyām evobhāv abhipīḍayata upasaṃgrāhyāv ity eke |
ĀpDhS 1.2.5.23  sarvāhṇaṃ suyukto 'dhyayanād anantaro 'dhyāye |
ĀpDhS 1.2.5.24  tathā gurukarmasu |
ĀpDhS 1.2.5.25  manasā cānadhyāye |
ĀpDhS 1.2.5.26  āhūtādhyāyī ca syāt | 

ĀpDhS 1.2.6.1  sadā niśāyāṃ guruṃ saṃveśayet tasya pādau prakṣālya saṃvāhya |
ĀpDhS 1.2.6.2  anujñātaḥ saṃviśet |
ĀpDhS 1.2.6.3  na cainam abhiprasārayīta |
ĀpDhS 1.2.6.4  na khaṭvāyāṃ sato 'bhiprasāraṇam astīty eke |
ĀpDhS 1.2.6.5  na cāsya sakāśe saṃviṣṭo bhāṣet |
ĀpDhS 1.2.6.6-7  abhibhāṣitas tv āsīnaḥ pratibrūyāt, anūtthāya tiṣṭhantam |
ĀpDhS 1.2.6.8-9  gacchantam anugacchet, dhāvantam anudhāvet |
ĀpDhS 1.2.6.10  na sopānahveṣṭitaśirā avahitapāṇir vā sīdet | 
ĀpDhS 1.2.6.11  adhvāpannas tu karmayukto vā sīdet |
ĀpDhS 1.2.6.12  na ced upasīdet |
ĀpDhS 1.2.6.13  devam ivācāryam upāsītaavikathayann avimanā vācaṃ śuśrūṣamāṇo 'sya | 
ĀpDhS 1.2.6.14  anupasthakṛtaḥ |
ĀpDhS 1.2.6.15-17  anuvāti vītaḥ, apratiṣṭabdhaḥ pāṇinā, anapaśrito 'nyatra | [K: anuvāti vāte]
ĀpDhS 1.2.6.18  yajñopavītī dvivastraḥ |
ĀpDhS 1.2.6.19  adhonivītas tv ekavastraḥ |
ĀpDhS 1.2.6.20  abhimukho 'nabhimukham |
ĀpDhS 1.2.6.21  anāsanno 'natidūre | [K: anatidūre ca]
ĀpDhS 1.2.6.22  yāvadāsīno bāhubhyām prāpnuyāt |
ĀpDhS 1.2.6.23  aprativātam |
ĀpDhS 1.2.6.24-25  ekādhyāyī dakṣiṇaṃ bāhuṃ praty upasīdet, yathāvakāśaṃ bahavaḥ |
ĀpDhS 1.2.6.26  tiṣṭhati ca nāsītaanāsanayogavihite |
ĀpDhS 1.2.6.27  āsīne ca na saṃviśet |
ĀpDhS 1.2.6.28  ceṣṭati ca cikīrṣantacchaktiviṣaye |
ĀpDhS 1.2.6.29  na cāsya sakāśe 'nvaksthāninam upasaṃgṛhṇīyāt | [K: sthānina]
ĀpDhS 1.2.6.30  gotreṇa vā kīrtayet |
ĀpDhS 1.2.6.31  na cainaṃ praty uttiṣṭhed anūttiṣṭhed vā | [K: anuttiṣṭhed]
ĀpDhS 1.2.6.32  api cet tasya guruḥ syāt | [ 31+32  = K 31]
ĀpDhS 1.2.6.33  deśāt tv āsanāc ca saṃsarpet | [ = K 32]
ĀpDhS 1.2.6.34  nāmnā tadantevāsinaṃ gurum apy ātmana ity eke | [ = K 33]
ĀpDhS 1.2.6.35  yasmintv anācāryasaṃbandhād gauravaṃ vṛttis tasminn anvaksthānīye 'py ācāryasya | [ = K 34]

ĀpDhS 1.4.12.1  tapaḥ svādhyāya iti brāhmaṇam |
ĀpDhS 1.4.12.2  tatra śrūyate | sa yadi tiṣṭhann āsīnaḥ śayāno vā svādhyāyam adhīte tapa eva tat tapyate tapo hi svādhyāya iti |
ĀpDhS 1.4.12.3  athāpi vājasaneyibrāhmaṇam | brahmayajño ha vā eṣa yat svādhyāyas tasyaite vaṣaṭkārā yat stanayati yad vidyotate yad avasphūrjati yad vāto vāyati | tasmāt stanayati vidyotamāne 'vasphūrjati vāte vā vāyaty adīyītaiva vaṣaṭkārāṇām acchambaṭkārāyeti | 
ĀpDhS 1.4.12.4  tasya śākhāntare vākyasamāptiḥ |
ĀpDhS 1.4.12.5  atha yadi vāto vā vāyāt stanayed vā vidyoteta vāvasphūrjed vaikāṃ vāṛcam ekaṃ vā yajur ekaṃ vā sāmābhivyāhared bhūr bhuvaḥ suvaḥ satyaṃ tapaḥ śraddhāyāṃ juhomīti vaitat | teno haivaasyaitad āḥ svādhyāya upātto bhavati | [K 1:vā vāto instead of vāto vā, 2:tenaūhaiva instead of teno haiva]
ĀpDhS 1.4.12.6  evaṃ saty āryasamayenāvipratiṣiddham |
ĀpDhS 1.4.12.7  adhyāyānadhyāyaṃ hy upadiśanti | tad anarthakaṃ syād vājasaneyibrāhmaṇaṃ ced avekṣeta |
ĀpDhS 1.4.12.8  āryasamayo hy agṛhyamānakāraṇaḥ |
ĀpDhS 1.4.12.9  vidyāṃ praty anadhyāyaḥ śrūyate na karmayoge mantrāṇām |

ĀpDhS 1.4.13.1  devebhyaḥ svāhākāra ā kāṣṭhāt, pitṛbhyaḥ svadhākāra odapātrāt svādhyāya iti |
ĀpDhS 1.4.13.2.3  pūjā varṇajyāyasāṃ kāryā, vṛddhatarāṇāṃ ca |
ĀpDhS 1.4.13.4  hṛṣṭo darpati dṛpto dharmam atikrāmati dharmātikrame khalu punar narakaḥ |
ĀpDhS 1.4.13.5  na samāvṛtte samādeśo vidyate |
ĀpDhS 1.4.13.6  oṃkāraḥ svargadvāraṃ tasmād brahmādhyeṣyamāṇa etadādi pratipadyeta |
ĀpDhS 1.4.13.7  vikathāṃ cānyāṃ kṛtvaivaṃ laukikyā vācā vyāvartate brahma |
ĀpDhS 1.4.13.8  yajñeṣu caitadādayaḥ prasavāḥ |
ĀpDhS 1.4.13.9  loke ca bhūtikarmasv etadādīny eva vākyāni syur yathā puṇyāhaṃ svastiṛddhim iti |
ĀpDhS 1.4.13.10  nāsamayena kṛcchraṃ kurvīta triḥśrāvaṇaṃ triḥsahavacanam iti parihāpya |
ĀpDhS 1.4.13.11  avicikitsā yāvad brahma nigantavyam iti hārītaḥ |
ĀpDhS 1.4.13.12  na bahirvede gatir vidyate |
ĀpDhS 1.4.13.13  samādiṣṭam adhyāpayantaṃ yāvad adhyayanam upasaṃgṛhṇīyāt |
ĀpDhS 1.4.13.14  nityam arhantam ity eke |
ĀpDhS 1.4.13.15  na gatir vidyate |
ĀpDhS 1.4.13.16  vṛddhānāṃ tu |
ĀpDhS 1.4.13.17  brahmaṇi mitho viniyoge na gatir vidyate |
ĀpDhS 1.4.13.18  brahma vardhata ity upadiśanti |
ĀpDhS 1.4.13.19  niveśe vṛtte saṃvatsare saṃvatsare dvau dvau māsau samāhita ācāryakule vased bhūyaḥ śrutam icchann iti śvetaketuḥ |

ĀpDhS 1.4.13.21  tac chāstrair vipratiṣiddham |
ĀpDhS 1.4.13.22  niveśe hi vṛtte naiyamikāni śrūyante |

ĀpDhS 1.5.15.1  upāsane gurūṇāṃ vṛddhānām atithīnāṃ home japyakarmaṇi bhojana ācamane svādhyāye ca yajñopavītī syāt |

ĀpDhS 1.7.20.1  nemaṃ laukikam arthaṃ puraskṛtya dharmāṃś caret |

ĀpDhS 1.8.22.1  adhyātmikān yogān anutiṣṭhen nyāyasaṃhitān anaiścārikān |
ĀpDhS 1.8.22.2  ātmalābhān na paraṃ vidyate |
ĀpDhS 1.8.22.3  tatrātmalābhīyānślokān udāhariṣyāmaḥ |
ĀpDhS 1.8.22.4  {Ś}? pūḥ prāṇinaḥ sarva eva guhāśayasya | ahanyamānasya vikalmaṣasya | acalaṃ calaniketaṃ ye 'nutiṣṭhanti te 'mṛtāḥ |
ĀpDhS 1.8.22.5  {Ś} yad idam id ihed iha loke viṣayam ucyate | vidhūya kavir etad anutiṣṭhed guhāśayam |
ĀpDhS 1.8.22.6  ātmann evāham alabdhvaitad dhitaṃ sevasva nāhitam | {Ś} athānyeṣu pratīcchāmi sādhuṣṭhānam anapekṣayā | mahāntaṃ tejasas kāyaṃ sarvatra nihitaṃ prabhum | 
ĀpDhS 1.8.22.7  {Ś}? sarvabhūteṣu yo nityo vipaścid amṛto dhruvaḥ | anaṅgo 'śabdo 'śarīro 'sparśaś ca mahānśuciḥ | sa sarvaṃ paramā kāṣṭhā sa vaiṣuvataṃ sa vai vaibhājanaṃ puram |
ĀpDhS 1.8.22.8  {Ś} taṃ yo 'nutiṣṭhet sarvatra prādhvaṃ cāsya sadācaret | durdarśaṃ nipuṇaṃ yukto yaḥ paśyet sa modeta viṣṭape ||

ĀpDhS 1.8.23.1  {Ś} ātman paśyan sarvabhūtāni na muhyec cintayan kaviḥ | ātmānaṃ caiva sarvatra yaḥ paśyet sa vai brahmā nākapṛṣṭhe virājati ||
ĀpDhS 1.8.23.2  {Ś}? nipuṇo 'ṇīyān bisorṇāyā yaḥ sarvam āvṛtya tiṣṭhati | varṣīyāṃś ca pṛthivyā dhruvaḥ sarvam ārabhya tiṣṭhati | sa indriyair jagato 'sya jñānād anyo 'nanyasya jñeyāt parameṣṭhī vibhājaḥ | tasmā kāyāḥ prabhavanti sarve sa mūlaṃ śāśvatikaḥ sa nityaḥ ||
ĀpDhS 1.8.23.3  {Ś}? doṣāṇāṃ tu vinirghāto yogamūla iha jīvite | nirhṛtya bhūtadāhīyān kṣemaṃ gacchati paṇḍitaḥ ||
ĀpDhS 1.8.23.4  atha bhūtadāhīyān doṣān udāhariṣyāmaḥ | 
ĀpDhS 1.8.23.5  krodho harṣo roṣo lobho moho dambho droho mṛṣodyam atyāśaparīvādāvasūyā kāmamanyū anātmyam ayogas teṣāṃ yogamūlo nirghātaḥ |
ĀpDhS 1.8.23.6  akrodho 'harṣo 'roṣo 'lobho 'moho 'dambho 'drohaḥ satyavacanam anatyāśo 'paiśunam anasūyā saṃvibhāgas tyāga ārjavaṃ mārdavaṃ śamo damaḥ sarvabhūtair avirodho yoga āryam ānṛśaṃsaṃ tuṣṭir iti sarvāśramāṇāṃ samayapadāni tāny anutiṣṭhan vidhinā sārvagāmī bhavati |

ĀpDhS 1.11.30.14  divā ca śirasaḥ prāvaraṇaṃ varjayen mūtrapurīṣayoḥ karma parihāpya |
ĀpDhS 1.11.30.15  śiras tu prāvṛtya mūtrapurīṣe kuryād bhūmyāṃ kiṃcid antardhāya |
ĀpDhS 1.11.30.16  chāyāyām mūtrapurīṣayoḥ karma varjayet |
ĀpDhS 1.11.30.17  svāṃ tu chāyām avamehet |
ĀpDhS 1.11.30.18  na sopānahmūtrapurīṣe kuryāt kṛṣṭe pathy apsu ca | [ = K18+19+20+21]

ĀpDhS 1.11.30.20  agnim ādityam apo brāhmaṇaṃ gā devatāś cābhimukho mūtrapurīṣayoḥ karma varjayet | [ = K 23]
ĀpDhS 1.11.30.21  aśmānaṃ loṣṭham ārdrān oṣadhivanaspatīn ūrdhvān ācchidya mūtrapurīṣayoḥ śundhane varjayet | [ = K 24]
ĀpDhS 1.11.30.22  agnim apo brāhmaṇaṃ gā devatā dvāraṃ pratīvātam ca śaktiviṣaye nābhiprasārayīta | [ = K 25, K 1ìnserts ādityam, 2:devatādvāraṃ]
ĀpDhS 1.11.30.23  athāpy udāharanti |

ĀpDhS 1.11.31.1  {Ś} prāṅmukho 'nnāni bhuñjīta uccared dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ |

ĀpDhS 1.11.31.3  ārāc cāvasathān mūtrapurīṣe kuryād dakṣiṇāṃ diśaṃ dakṣiṇāparāṃ vā | [ = K 2, K 3: astamite ca bahirgrāmād ārād āvasathād vā mūtrapurīṣayoḥ karma varjayet |]
ĀpDhS 1.11.31.4  devatābhidhānaṃ cāprayataḥ |

ĀpDhS 1.11.31.23  krodhādīṃś ca bhūtadāhīyān doṣān varjayet || [ = K 27]

ĀpDhS 1.11.32.1  pravacanayukto varṣāśaradaṃ maithunaṃ varjayet |

ĀpDhS 2.1.2.1  api vā lepān prakṣālyācamya prokṣaṇam aṅgānām |

ĀpDhS 2.3.6.1  jātyācārasaṃśaye dharmārtham āgatam agnim upasamādhāya jātim ācāraṃ ca pṛcchet |
ĀpDhS 2.3.6.2  sādhutāṃ cet pratijānīte 'gnir upadraṣṭā vāyur upaśrotādityo 'nukhyātā sādhutāṃ pratijānīte sādhvasmā astu vitatha eṣa enasa ity uktvā śāstuṃ pratipadyeta | [K 1  insets: cet, 2: pratipadyate]
ĀpDhS 2.3.6.3  agnir iva jvalann atithir abhyāgacchati | 
ĀpDhS 2.3.6.4  dharmeṇa vedānām ekaikāṃ śākhām adhītya śrotriyo bhavati |
ĀpDhS 2.3.6.5  svadharmayuktaṃ kuṭumbinam abhyāgacchati dharmapuraskāro nānyaprayojanaḥ so 'tithir bhavati |
ĀpDhS 2.3.6.6  tasya pūjāyāṃ śāntiḥ svargaś ca |
ĀpDhS 2.3.6.7  tam abhimukho 'bhyāgamya yathāvayaḥ sametya tasyāsanam āhārayet |

ĀpDhS 2.3.7.1  sa eṣa prājāpatyaḥ kuṭumbino yajño nityapratataḥ | [K: prajāpatyaḥ]

ĀpDhS 2.3.7.3  ūrjaṃ puṣṭiṃ prajāṃ paśūn iṣṭāpūrtam iti gṛhāṇām aśnāti yaḥ pūrvo 'tither aśnāti |
ĀpDhS 2.3.7.4  payopasecanam annam agniṣṭomasaṃmitaṃ sarpiṣokthyasaṃmitaṃ madhunātirātrasaṃmitaṃ māṃsena dvādaśāhasaṃmitam udakena prajāvṛddhir āyuṣaś ca |

ĀpDhS 2.3.7.10  yad upāvartate so 'vabhṛthaḥ |
ĀpDhS 2.3.7.11  iti hi brāhmaṇam | [K omits: hi]

ĀpDhS 2.3.7.14  purāgnihotrasya homād upāṃśu japet | vrātya yathā te manas tathāstv iti | vrātya yathā te vaśas tathāstuiti | vrātya yathā te priyaṃ tathāstuiti | vrātya yathā te nikāmas tathāstuiti ||
ĀpDhS 2.3.7.15  yasyauddhṛteṣv ahuteṣv agniṣv atithir abhyāgacchet svayam enam abhyudetya brūyāt vrātya atisṛja hoṣyāmi | ity atisṛṣṭena hotavyam | anatisṛṣṭaś cejjuhuyād doṣam brāhmaṇam āha ||

ĀpDhS 2.7.16.1  saha devamanuṣyā asmiṃl loke purā babhūvuḥ | atha devāḥ karmabhir divaṃ jagmur ahīyanta manuṣyāḥ | teṣāṃ ye tathā karmāṇy ārabhante saha devair brahmaṇā cāmuṣmiṃl loke bhavanti | athaitan manuḥ śrāddhaśabdṃ karma provāca |

ĀpDhS 2.7.17.1  khaḍgopastaraṇe khaḍgamāṃsenānantyaṃ kālam |

ĀpDhS 2.9.22.1  tasyāraṇyam ācchādanaṃ vihitam |

ĀpDhS 2.9.23.1  bhūyāṃsaṃ vā niyamam icchann anvaham eva pātreṇa sāyaṃ prātar artham āharet | 
ĀpDhS 2.9.23.2  tato mūlaiḥ phalaiḥ parṇais tṛṇair iti vartayaṃś cared antataḥ pravṛttāni tato 'po vāyum ākāśam ity abhiniśrayet teṣām uttara uttaraḥ saṃyogaḥ phlato viśiṣṭaḥ | [K inserts |]
ĀpDhS 2.9.23.3  atha purāṇe ślokāv udāharanti |
ĀpDhS 2.9.23.4  {Ś} aṣṭāśītisahasrāṇi ye prajām īśiraṛṣayaḥ | dakṣiṇenāryamṇaḥ panthānaṃ te śmaśānāni bhejire || ( 3+4  = K 3]
ĀpDhS 2.9.23.5  {Ś} aṣṭāśītisahasrāṇi ye prajāṃ naīṣiraṛṣayaḥ | uttareṇāryamṇaḥ panthānaṃ te 'mṛtatvaṃ hi kalpate || ( = K 4]
ĀpDhS 2.9.23.6  ity ūrdhvaretasāṃ praśaṃsā | [ = K 5]
ĀpDhS 2.9.23.7  athāpi saṃkalpasiddhayo bhavanti | [ = K 6]
ĀpDhS 2.9.23.8  yathā varṣaṃ prajādānaṃ dūre darśanaṃ manojavatā yac cānyad evaṃ yuktam | [ = K 7, prajā dānaṃ]
ĀpDhS 2.9.23.9  tasmāc chrutitaḥ pratyakṣaphalatvāc ca viśiṣṭān āśramān etān eke bruvate | [ = K 8]
ĀpDhS 2.9.23.10  traividyavṛddhānāṃ tu vedāḥ pramāṇam iti niṣṭhā tatra yāni śrūyante vrīhiyavapaśvājyapayaḥkapālapatnīsaṃbandhāny uccair nīcaiḥ kāryam iti tair viruddha ācāro 'pramāṇam iti manyante | [ = K 11]
ĀpDhS 2.9.23.11  yat tu śmaśānam ucyate nānākarmaṇām eṣo 'nte puruṣasaṃskāro vidhīyate | [ = K 12]
ĀpDhS 2.9.23.12  tataḥ param anantyaṃ phalaṃ svargyaśabdaṃ śrūyate ||

ĀpDhS 2.9.24.1  athāpy asya prajātim amṛtam āmnāya āha | prajām anu prajāyase tad u te martyāmṛtam iti | [K]
ĀpDhS 2.9.24.2  athāpi sa evāyaṃ virūḍhaḥ pṛthak pratyakṣeṇaupalabhyate dṛśyate cāpi sārūpyaṃ dehatvam evānyat |
ĀpDhS 2.9.24.3-6  te śiṣṭeṣu karmasu vartamānāḥ pūrveṣāṃ sāṃparāyeṇa kīrtiṃ svargaṃ ca vardhayanti | evam avaro 'varaḥ pareṣām | ā bhūtasaṃplavāt te svargajitaḥ | punaḥ sarge bījārthā bhavantīti bhaviṣyatpurāṇe |
ĀpDhS 2.9.24.7-8  athāpi prajāpater vacanam | {Ś} trayīṃ vidyāṃ brahmacaryaṃ prajātiṃ śraddhāṃ tapo yajñam anupradānam | ya etāni kurvate tair it saha smo rajo bhūtvā dhvaṃsate 'nyat praśaṃsann iti ||
ĀpDhS 2.9.24.9  tatra ye pāpakṛtas ta eva dhvaṃsanti yathā parṇaṃ vanaspater na parān hiṃsanti |
ĀpDhS 2.9.24.10  nāsyāsmiṃl loke karmabhiḥ saṃbandho vidyate tathā parasmin karmaphalaiḥ |
ĀpDhS 2.9.24.11  tad etena veditavyam |
ĀpDhS 2.9.24.12  prajāpater ṛṣīṇām iti sargo 'yam |
ĀpDhS 2.9.24.13  tatra ye puṇyakṛtas teṣāṃ prakṛtayaḥ parā jvalantya upalabhyante |
ĀpDhS 2.9.24.14  syāt tu karmāvayavena tapasā vā kaścit saśarīro 'ntavantaṃ lokaṃ jayati saṃkalpasiddhiś ca syān na tu taj jyaiṣṭhyam āśramāṇām |

ĀpDhS 2.9.25.1  vyākhyātāḥ sarvavarṇānāṃ sādhāraṇavaiśeṣikā dharmāḥ | rājñas tu viśeṣād vakṣyāmaḥ | [ = K omits: | : dharmā rājñas]
ĀpDhS 2.9.25.2  dakṣiṇādvāraṃ veśma puraṃ ca māpayet |
ĀpDhS 2.9.25.3  antarasyāṃ puri veśma |
ĀpDhS 2.9.25.4  tasya purastād āvasathas tad āmantraṇam ity ācakṣate |
ĀpDhS 2.9.25.5  dakṣiṇena puraṃ sabhā dakṣiṇodagdvārā yathobhayaṃ saṃdṛśyeta bahir antaraṃ ceti |
ĀpDhS 2.9.25.6  sarveṣv evājasrā agnayaḥ syuḥ |
ĀpDhS 2.9.25.7  agnipūjā ca nityā yathā gṛhamedhe |
ĀpDhS 2.9.25.8  āvasathe śrotriyāvarārdhyān atithīn vāsayet |
ĀpDhS 2.9.25.9  teṣāṃ yathāguṇam āvasathāḥ śayyānnapānaṃ ca videyam |
ĀpDhS 2.9.25.10  gurūn amātyāṃś ca nātijīvet |
ĀpDhS 2.9.25.11  na cāsya viṣaye kṣudhā rogeṇa himātapābhyāṃ vāvasīded abhāvād buddhipūrvaṃ vā kaścit |
ĀpDhS 2.9.25.12  sabhāyā madhye 'dhidevanam uddhatyāvokṣyākṣān nivaped yugmān vaibhītakān yathārthān |
ĀpDhS 2.9.25.13  āryāḥ śucayaḥ satyaśīlā dīvitāraḥ syuḥ |
ĀpDhS 2.9.25.14  āyudhagrahaṇaṃ nṛttagītavāditrāṇīti rājādhīnebhyo 'nyatra na vidyeran |
ĀpDhS 2.9.25.15  kṣemakṛd rājā yasya viṣaye grāme 'raṇye vā taskarabhayaṃ na vidyate ||

ĀpDhS 2.10.26.1  bhṛtyānām anuparodhena kṣetraṃ vittaṃ ca dadad brāhmaṇebhyo yathārham anantāṃl lokān abhijayati | 
ĀpDhS 2.10.26.2  brāhmaṇasvāny apajigīṣamāṇo rājā yo hanyate tam āhur ātmayūpo yajño 'nantadakṣiṇa iti |
ĀpDhS 2.10.26.3  etenānye śūrā vyākhyātāḥ prayojane yudhyamānās tanutyajaḥ |
ĀpDhS 2.10.26.4  grāmeṣu nagareṣu cāryāñ chucīn satyaśīlān prajāguptaye nidadhyāt |
ĀpDhS 2.10.26.5  teṣāṃ puruṣās tathāguṇā eva syuḥ |
ĀpDhS 2.10.26.6  sarvato yojanaṃ nagaraṃ taskarebhyo rakṣyam |
ĀpDhS 2.10.26.7  krośo grāmebhyaḥ |
ĀpDhS 2.10.26.8  tatra yatmuṣyate tais tat pratidāpyam |
ĀpDhS 2.10.26.9  dhārmyaṃ śulkam avahārayet |
ĀpDhS 2.10.26.10  akaraḥ śrotriyaḥ |
ĀpDhS 2.10.26.11  sarvavarṇānāṃ ca striyaḥ |
ĀpDhS 2.10.26.12  kumārāś ca prāg vyañjanebhyaḥ |
ĀpDhS 2.10.26.13  ye ca vidyārthā vasanti |
ĀpDhS 2.10.26.14  tapasvinaś ca ye dharmaparāḥ |
ĀpDhS 2.10.26.15  śūdraś ca pādāvanektā |
ĀpDhS 2.10.26.16  andhamūkabadhirarogaviṣṭāś ca
ĀpDhS 2.10.26.17  ye vyarthā dravyaparigrahaiḥ |
ĀpDhS 2.10.26.18  abuddhipūrvam alaṃkṛto yuvā paradāram anupraviśan kumārīṃ vā vācā bādhyaḥ |
ĀpDhS 2.10.26.19  buddhi,pūrvaṃ tu duṣṭabhāvo daṇḍyaḥ |
ĀpDhS 2.10.26.20  saṃnipāte vṛtte śiśnacchedanaṃ savṛṣaṇasya | 
ĀpDhS 2.10.26.21  kumāryāṃ tu svāny ādāya nāśyaḥ |
ĀpDhS 2.10.26.22  atha bhṛtye rājñā |
ĀpDhS 2.10.26.23  rakṣye cāta ūrdhvaṃ maithunāt |
ĀpDhS 2.10.26.24  nirveṣābhyupāye tu svāmibhyo 'vasṛjet ||

ĀpDhS 2.10.27.1  carite yathāpuraṃ dharmādhi saṃbandhaḥ |
ĀpDhS 2.10.27.2  sagotrasthānīyāṃ na parebhyaḥ samācakṣīta |
ĀpDhS 2.10.27.3  kulāya hi strī pradīyata ity upadiśanti |
ĀpDhS 2.10.27.4  tad indriyadaurbalyād vipratipannam |
ĀpDhS 2.10.27.5  aviśiṣṭaṃ hi paratvaṃ pāṇeḥ |
ĀpDhS 2.10.27.6  tadvyatikrame khalu punar ubhayor narakaḥ |
ĀpDhS 2.10.27.7  niyamārambhaṇo hi varṣīyān abhyudaya evam ārambhaṇād apatyāt |
ĀpDhS 2.10.27.8  nāśya āryaḥ śūdrāyām | 
ĀpDhS 2.10.27.9  vadhyaḥ śūdra āryāyām |
ĀpDhS 2.10.27.10  dāraṃ cāsya karśayet |
ĀpDhS 2.10.27.11  savarṇāyām anyapūrvāyāṃ sakṛt saṃnipāte pādaḥ patatīty upadiśanti |
ĀpDhS 2.10.27.12  evam abhyāse pādaḥ pādaḥ |
ĀpDhS 2.10.27.13  caturthe sarvam |
ĀpDhS 2.10.27.14  jihvācchedanaṃ śūdrasyāryaṃ dhārmikam ākrośataḥ |
ĀpDhS 2.10.27.15  vāci pathi śayyāyām āsana iti samībhavato daṇḍatāḍanam | [K bāci]
ĀpDhS 2.10.27.16  puruṣavadhe steye bhūmyādāna iti svāny ādāya vadhyaḥ |
ĀpDhS 2.10.27.17  cakṣunirodhas tv eteṣu brāhmaṇasya |
ĀpDhS 2.10.27.18  niyamātikramaṇam anyaṃ vā rahasi bandhayet | 
ĀpDhS 2.10.27.19-20  ā samāpatteḥ | asamāpattau nāśyaḥ |
ĀpDhS 2.10.27.21  ācārya ṛtvik snātako rājeti trāṇaṃ syur anyatra vadhyāt ||

ĀpDhS 2.11.28.1  kṣetraṃ parigṛhyotthānābhāvāt phalābhāve yaḥ samṛddhaḥ sa bhāvi tad apahāryaḥ |
ĀpDhS 2.11.28.2.3  avaśinaḥ kīnāśasya karmanyāse daṇḍatāḍanam | tathā paśupasya | 
ĀpDhS 2.11.28.4  avarodhanaṃ cāsya paśūnām |
ĀpDhS 2.11.28.5  hitvā vrajam ādinaḥ karśayet paśūn nātipātayet | [ = K 5+6]
ĀpDhS 2.11.28.6  avarudhya paśūn māraṇe nāśane vā svāmibhyo 'vasṛjet | [ = K 7]
ĀpDhS 2.11.28.7  pramādād araṇye paśūn utsṛṣtān dṛṣṭvā grāmam ānīya svāmibhyo 'vasṛjet | [ = K 8]
ĀpDhS 2.11.28.8-9  punaḥ pramāde sakṛd avarudhya | tata ūrdhvaṃ na sūrkṣet | [ = K 9+10]
ĀpDhS 2.11.28.10  paraparigraham avidvān ādadāna edhodake mūle puṣpe phale gandhe grāse śāka iti vācā bādhyaḥ | [ = K 11]
ĀpDhS 2.11.28.11  viduṣo vāsasaḥ parimoṣaṇam | [ = K 12]
ĀpDhS 2.11.28.12  adaṇḍyaḥ kāmakṛte tathā prāṇasaṃśaye bhojanam ādadānaḥ | [ = K 13]
ĀpDhS 2.11.28.13  prāptanimitte daṇḍākarmaṇi rājānam enaḥ spṛśati || [ = K 14]

ĀpDhS 2.11.29.1  prayojayitā mantā karteti svarganarakaphaleṣu karmasu bhāginaḥ | [*karmeti]
ĀpDhS 2.11.29.2  yo bhūya ārabhate tasmin phalaviśeṣaḥ |
ĀpDhS 2.11.29.3  kuṭumbinau dhanasyaīśate |
ĀpDhS 2.11.29.4  tayor anumate 'nye |
ĀpDhS 2.11.29.5  vivāde vidyābhijanasaṃpannā vṛddhā medhāvino dharmeṣv avinipātinaḥ |
ĀpDhS 2.11.29.6  saṃdehe liṅgato daiveneti vicitya |
ĀpDhS 2.11.29.7  puṇyāhe prātar agnāv iddhe' apāmante rājavaty ubhayataḥ samākhyāpya sarvānumate mukhyaḥ satyaṃ praśnaṃ brūyāt |
ĀpDhS 2.11.29.8  anṛte rājā daṇḍaṃ praṇayet |
ĀpDhS 2.11.29.9  narakaś cātrādhikaḥ sāṃparāye |
ĀpDhS 2.11.29.10  satye svargaḥ sarvabhūtapraśaṃsā ca |
ĀpDhS 2.11.29.11  sā niṣṭhā yā vidyā strīṣu śūdreṣu ca |
ĀpDhS 2.11.29.12  ātharvaṇasya vedasya śeṣa ity upadiśanti |
ĀpDhS 2.11.29.13  kṛcchrā dharmasamāptiḥ samāmnātena | lakṣaṇakarmaṇāt tu samāpyate | [K 1: samāmnānena, 2: omits: |, 3: karmaṇā]
ĀpDhS 2.11.29.14  tatra lakṣaṇam | sarvajanapadeṣv ekāntasamāhitam āryāṇāṃ vṛttaṃ samyagvinītānāṃ vṛddhānām ātmavatām alolupānām adāmbhikānāṃ vṛttasādṛśyaṃ bhajeta | evam ubhau lokāv abhijayati | [ = K 14+15, omit: |]
ĀpDhS 2.11.29.15  strībhyaḥ sarvavarṇebhyaś ca dharmaśeṣān pratīyād ity eka ity eke || 

|| samāptaṃ cedam āpastambīyadharmasūtram ||

Gautama Dharmasūtra


Originally typed and analyzed by Nobuyuki Watase.
Converted to Unicode plain text without hyphens, sandhi resolution, and analytical markers by Timothy Lubin, October 2021.

Based on the edition of Ganeśa Śāstrin Gokhale, Pune 1910, Anandasrama Sanskrit Series, 61;
with variants from: The Institutes of Gautama, edition by A.F. Stenzler, London 1876.

Structure of references:
GDhS-A n,nn = Anandaśrama ed.: praśna,adhyāya.sūtra
GDhS-St nn = Stenzler's ed.: chapter.sūtra
If only one of these two references is given, the respective sutra is lacking in the other edition.
{VAR_St: } = variant reading in Stenzler's edition

NOTE:
In this GRETIL version, all variants have been extracted from the running text
and transferred to a separate line below the respective sutra

GDhS-A 1,1.47 / GDhS-St 1.44: mūtrapurīṣasnehavisraṃsanābhyavahārasaṃyogeṣu ca |

GDhS-A 1,1.48 / GDhS-St 1.45: yatra cāmnāyo vidadhyāt |
GDhS-A 1,1.49a | GDhS-St 1.46: pāṇinā savyam upasaṅgṛhya anaṅguṣṭham adhīhi bho ity āmantrayed guruṃ |

GDhS-A 1,1.49c | GDhS-St 1.48: prāṇopasparśanaṃ darbhaiḥ |

{VAR_St: -tūleṣu}
GDhS-A 1,1.52 / GDhS-St 1.51: oṃ pūrvā vyāhṛtayaḥ pañca satyāntāḥ |
GDhS-A 1,1.53 / GDhS-St 1.52: guroḥ pādopasaṃgrahaṇaṃ prātaḥ |
GDhS-A 1,1.54 / GDhS-St 1.53: brahmānuvacane cādyantayoḥ |

GDhS-A 3,10.46 / GDhS-St 28.48: anājñāte daśāvaraiḥ śiṣṭair ūhavidbhir alubdhaiḥ praśastaṃ kāryam |
{VAR_St: ūhavadbhir}
GDhS-A 3,10.47 / GDhS-St 28.49: catvāraś caturṇāṃ pāragā vedānāṃ prāg uttamāttraya āśramiṇaḥ pṛthag dharmavidas traya etān daśāvarān pariṣad ity ācakṣate |
{VAR_St: traya ity etān}
GDhS-A 3,10.48 / GDhS-St 28.50: asambhave tv eteṣāṃ śrotriyo vedavicchiṣṭovipratipattau yad āha |
GDhS-A 3,10.49 / GDhS-St 28.51: yato 'yam aprabhavo bhūtānāṃ hiṃsānugrahayogeṣu |
GDhS-A 3,10.50 / GDhS-St 28.52: dharmiṇāṃ viśeṣeṇa svargaṃ lokaṃ dharmavid āpnotijñānābhiniveśābhyām |
GDhS-A 3,10.51  iti dharmo dharmaḥ |


%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

Baudhāyana Dharmasūtra

Originally typed and analyzed by Masato Fujii & Mieko Kajihara; proofread by Toru Yagi; revised version 1  completed on May 20, 1992.
Converted to Unicode plain text without hyphens, sandhi resolution, and analytical markers by Timothy Lubin, October 2021.

Text based on editions of:
[H] E Hultzsch [ed]: Das Baudhayana-Dharmasutra Zweite, verbesserte
Auflage [Abhandlungen fur die Kunde des Morgenlandes, 16] Leipzig 1922.
[K] Umesa Chandra Pandeya [ed]: The Baudhāyana-Dharmasutra with the 'Vivaraṇa' Commentary by Śri Govinda Svami and Critical Notes by M. M. A. Chinnaswami Sastri [The Kashi Sanskrit Series, 104], Varanasi 1972.


|| atha baudhāyanadharmasūtram ||

BDhS 1.1.1.1  upadiṣṭo dharmaḥ prativedam ||
BDhS 1.1.1.2  tasyānu vyākhyāsyāmaḥ ||
BDhS 1.1.1.3  smārto dvitīyaḥ ||
BDhS 1.1.1.4  tṛtīyaḥ śiṣṭāgamaḥ ||
BDhS 1.1.1.5  śiṣṭāḥ khalu vigatamatsarā nirahaṃkārāḥ kumbhīdhānyā alolupā dambhadarpalobhamohakrodhavivarjitāḥ ||
BDhS 1.1.1.6ab  dharmeṇādhigato yeṣāṃ vedaḥ saparibṛṃhaṇaḥ |
BDhS 1.1.1.6cd  śiṣṭās tadanumānajñāḥ śrutipratyakṣahetavaḥ || iti || [cf. Va 6.43; M 12.109]
BDhS 1.1.1.7  tadabhāve daśāvarā pariṣat ||
BDhS 1.1.1.8  athāpy udāharanti |
BDhS 1.1.1.8ab  cāturvaidyaṃ vikalpī ca aṅgavid dharmapāṭhakaḥ |
BDhS 1.1.1.8cd  āśramasthās trayo viprāḥ parṣad eṣā daśāvarā ||
BDhS 1.1.1.9ab  pañca vā syus trayo vā syur eko vā syād aninditaḥ |
BDhS 1.1.1.9cd  prativaktā tu dharmasya netare tu sahasraśaḥ ||
BDhS 1.1.1.10ab  yathā dārumayo hastī yathā carmamayo mṛgaḥ |
BDhS 1.1.1.10cd  brāhmaṇaś cānadhīyānas trayas te nāmadhārakāḥ ||
BDhS 1.1.1.11ab  yad vadanti tamomūḍhā mūrkhā dharmam ajānataḥ |
BDhS 1.1.1.11cd  tat pāpaṃ śatadhā bhūtvā vaktṝn samadhigacchati ||
BDhS 1.1.1.12ab  bahudvārasya dharmasya sūkṣmā duranugā gatiḥ |
BDhS 1.1.1.12cd  tasmān na vācyo hy ekena bahujñenāpi saṃśaye ||
BDhS 1.1.1.13ab  dharmaśāstrarathārūḍhā vedakhaḍgadharā dvijāḥ |
BDhS 1.1.1.13cd  krīḍārtham api yad brūyuḥ sa dharmaḥ paramaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
BDhS 1.1.1.14ab  yathāśmani sthitaṃ toyaṃ mārutārkau praṇāśayet |
BDhS 1.1.1.14cd  tadvat kartari yat pāpaṃ jalavat saṃpralīyate ||
BDhS 1.1.1.15ab  śarīraṃ balam āyuś ca vayaḥ kālaṃ ca karma ca |
BDhS 1.1.1.15cd  samīkṣya dharmavid buddhyā prāyaścittāni nirdiśet ||
BDhS 1.1.1.16ab  avratānām amantrāṇāṃ jātimātropajīvinām |
BDhS 1.1.1.16cd  sahasraśaḥ sametānāṃ pariṣattvaṃ na vidyate || iti ||
BDhS 1.1.2.1  pañcadhā vipratipattir dakṣiṇatas tathottarataḥ ||
BDhS 1.1.2.2  yāni dakṣiṇatas tāni vyākhyāsyāmaḥ ||
BDhS 1.1.2.3  yathaitad anupetena saha bhojanaṃ striyā saha bhojanaṃparyuṣitabhojanaṃ mātulapitṛsvasṛduhitṛgamanam iti ||
BDhS 1.1.2.4  athottarata ūrṇāvikrayaḥ sīdhupānam ubhayatodadbhirvyavahāra āyudhīyakaṃ samudrasaṃyānam iti ||
BDhS 1.1.2.5  itarad itarasmin kurvan duṣyatītarad itarasmin ||
BDhS 1.1.2.6  tatra tatra deśaprāmāṇyam eva syāt ||
BDhS 1.1.2.7  mithyaitad iti gautamaḥ ||
BDhS 1.1.2.8  ubhayaṃ caiva | nādriyeta śiṣṭasmṛtivirodhadarśanāt ||
BDhS 1.1.2.9  prāg ādarśāt pratyak kanakhalād dakṣiṇenahimavantam udak pāriyātram etad āryāvartam | tasmin ya ācāraḥ sapramāṇam || [K: kālakavanād]
BDhS 1.1.2.10  gaṅgāyamunayor antaram ity eke ||
BDhS 1.1.2.11  athāpy atra bhāllavino gāthām udāharanti ||
BDhS 1.1.2.12ab  paścāt sindhur vidharaṇī sūryasyodayanaṃ puraḥ |
BDhS 1.1.2.12cd  yāvat kṛṣṇā vidhāvanti tāvad dhi brahmavarcasam || iti ||
BDhS 1.1.2.13ab  avantayo 'ṅgamagadhāḥ surāṣṭrā dakṣiṇāpathāḥ |
BDhS 1.1.2.13cd  upāvṛdsindhusauvīrā ete saṃkīrṇayonayaḥ ||
BDhS 1.1.2.14  āraṭṭān kāraskarān puṇḍrān sauvīrān vaṅgān kaliṅgānprānūnān iti ca gatvā punastomena yajeta sarvapṛṣṭhayā vā ||
BDhS 1.1.2.15-1  athāpy udāharanti |
BDhS 1.1.2.15-2ab  padbhyāṃ sa kurute pāpaṃ yaḥ kaliṅgān prapadyate |
BDhS 1.1.2.15-2cd  ṛṣayo niṣkṛtiṃ tasya prāhur vaiśvānaraṃ haviḥ ||
BDhS 1.1.2.16ab  bahūnām api doṣāṇāṃ kṛtānāṃ doṣanirṇaye |
BDhS 1.1.2.16cd  pavitreṣṭiṃ praśaṃsanti sā hi pāvanamuttamam || iti || [N.B.: gender disagreement]
BDhS 1.1.2.17-1  athāpy udāharanti |
BDhS 1.1.2.17-2ab  vaiśvānarīṃ vrātapatīṃ pavitreṣṭiṃ tathaiva ca |
BDhS 1.1.2.17-2cd  ṛtav ṛtau prayuñjānaḥ pāpebhyo vipramucyate || pāpebhyovipramucyata iti ||
BDhS 1.2.3.1  aṣṭācatvāriṃśad varṣāṇi paurāṇaṃ vedabrahmacaryam ||
BDhS 1.2.3.2  caturviṃśatiṃ dvādaśa vā prativedam ||
BDhS 1.2.3.3  saṃvatsarāvamaṃ vā pratikāṇḍam ||
BDhS 1.2.3.4  grahaṇāntaṃ vā jīvitasyāsthiratvāt ||
BDhS 1.2.3.5  kṛṣṇakeśo 'gnīn ādadhīteti śrutiḥ ||
BDhS 1.2.3.6ab  nāsya karma niyacchanti kiṃcid ā mauñjibandhanāt |
BDhS 1.2.3.6cd  vṛttyā śūdrasamo hy eṣa yāvad vedena jāyate || iti ||
BDhS 1.2.3.7  garbhādiḥ saṃkhyā varṣāṇām | tadaṣṭameṣu brāhmaṇamupanayīta ||
BDhS 1.2.3.8  tryadhikeṣu rājanyam ||
BDhS 1.2.3.9  tasmād ekādhikeṣu vaiśyam ||
BDhS 1.2.3.10  vasanto grīṣmaḥ śarad ity ṛtavo varṇānupūrvyeṇa ||
BDhS 1.2.3.11  gāyatrītriṣṭubjagatībhir yathākramam ||
BDhS 1.2.3.12  ā ṣoḍaśād ā dvāviṃśād ā caturviṃśād anātyaya eṣāṃ krameṇa ||
BDhS 1.2.3.13  mauñjī dhanurjyā śāṇīti mekhalāḥ ||
BDhS 1.2.3.14  kṛṣṇarurubastājināny ajināni ||
BDhS 1.2.3.15  mūrdhalalāṭanāsāgrapramāṇā yājñikasya vṛkṣasya daṇḍāḥ ||
BDhS 1.2.3.16  bhavatpūrvāṃ bhikṣāmadhyāṃ yācñāntāṃ bhikṣāṃ caret saptākṣarāṃ kṣāṃ ca hiṃ ca na vardhayet ||
BDhS 1.2.3.17  bhavatpūrvāṃ brāhmaṇo bhikṣeta bhavanmadhyāṃ rājanyobhavadantāṃ vaiśyaḥ sarveṣu varṇeṣu ||
BDhS 1.2.3.18  te brāhmaṇādyāḥ svakarmasthāḥ ||
BDhS 1.2.3.19  sadāraṇyāt samidha āhṛtyādadhyāt ||
BDhS 1.2.3.20  satyavādī hrīmān anahaṃkāraḥ ||
BDhS 1.2.3.21  pūrvotthāyī jaghanyasaṃveśī ||
BDhS 1.2.3.22  sarvatrāpratihataguruvākyo 'nyatra pātakāt ||
BDhS 1.2.3.23  yāvadarthasaṃbhāṣī strībhiḥ ||
BDhS 1.2.3.24  nṛttagītavāditragandhamālyopānakchattradhāraṇāñjanābhyañjanavarjī ||
BDhS 1.2.3.25  dakṣiṇaṃ dakṣiṇena savyaṃ savyena copasaṃgṛhṇīyāt ||
BDhS 1.2.3.26  dīrgham āyuḥ svargaṃ cepsan kāmam anyasmai sādhuvṛttāyaguruṇānujñātaḥ ||
BDhS 1.2.3.27  asāv ahaṃ bho iti śrotre saṃspṛśya manaḥsamādhānārtham ||
BDhS 1.2.3.28  adhastāj jānvor ā padbhyām ||
BDhS 1.2.3.29  nāsīno nāsīnāya na śayāno na śayānāya nāprayatonāprayatāya ||
BDhS 1.2.3.30  śaktiviṣaye muhūrtam api nāprayataḥ syāt ||
BDhS 1.2.3.31  samiddhāry udakumbhapuṣpānnahasto nābhivādayed yaccānyad apy evaṃyuktam ||
BDhS 1.2.3.32  na samavāye 'bhivādayed atyantaśaḥ ||
BDhS 1.2.3.33  bhrātṛpatnīnāṃ yuvatīnāṃ ca guruptnīnāṃ jātavīryaḥ ||
BDhS 1.2.3.34  nauśilāphalakakuñjaraprāsādakaṭeṣu cakravatsu cādoṣamsahāsanam ||
BDhS 1.2.3.35  prasādhanotsādanasnāpanocchiṣṭabojanānīti guroḥ || [K: ucchādana]
BDhS 1.2.3.36  ucchiṣṭavarjanaṃ tatputre 'nūcāne vā || [K: varjaṃ]
BDhS 1.2.3.37  prasādhanotsādanasnāpanavarjanaṃ ca tatpatnyām || [K: ucchādana ... varjaṃ]
BDhS 1.2.3.38  dhāvantam anudhāved gacchantam anugacchet tiṣṭhantamanutiṣṭhet ||
BDhS 1.2.3.39  nāpsu ślāghamānaḥsnāyāt || [K: ślaghamānaḥ] 
BDhS 1.2.3.40  daṇḍa iva plavet ||
BDhS 1.2.3.41  abrāhmaṇād adhyayanam āpadi ||
BDhS 1.2.3.42  śuśrūṣānuvrajyā ca yāvadadhyayanam ||
BDhS 1.2.3.43  tayos tad eva pāvanam ||
BDhS 1.2.3.44  bhrātṛputraśiṣyeṣu caivam ||
BDhS 1.2.3.45  ṛtvijśvaśurapitṛvyamātulānāṃ tu yavīyasāṃpratyutthāyābhibhāṣaṇam ||
BDhS 1.2.3.46  pratyabhivāda iti kātyaḥ ||
BDhS 1.2.3.47  śiśāv āṅgirase darśanāt ||
BDhS 1.2.4.1ab  dharmārthau yatra na syātāṃ śuśrūṣā vāpi tadvidhā |
BDhS 1.2.4.1cd  vidyayā saha martavyaṃ na cainām ūṣare vapet || [cf. M 2.112]
BDhS 1.2.4.2ab  agnir iva kakṣaṃ dahati brahma pṛṣṭam anādṛtam |
BDhS 1.2.4.2cd  tasmād vai śakyaṃ na brūyād brahma mānam akurvatām || iti ||
BDhS 1.2.4.3  evāsmai [K: atraivāsmai] vaco vedayante ||
BDhS 1.2.4.4  brahma vai mṛtyave prajāḥ prāyacchat | tasmai brahmacāriṇameva na prāyacchat | so 'bravīd astu mahyam apy etasmin bhāga iti | yām eva rātriṃ samidhaṃ nāharātaīti ||
BDhS 1.2.4.5  tasmād brahmacārī yāṃ rātriṃ samidhaṃ nāharaty āyuṣaeva tām avadāya vasati | tasmād brahmacārī samidham āharen nedāyuṣo 'vadāya vasānīti ||
BDhS 1.2.4.6  dīrgasattraṃ vay eṣa upaiti yo brahmacaryam upaiti | sa yāmupayan samidham ādadhāti sā prāyaṇīyātha yāṃ snāsyansodayanīyātha yā antareṇa sattryā evāsya tāḥ ||
BDhS 1.2.4.7-1  brāhmaṇo vai brahmacaryam upayaṃś caturdhā bhūtānipraviśaty agniṃ padā mṛtyuṃ padācāryaṃ padātmany evāsyacaturthaḥ pādaḥ pariśiṣyate |
BDhS 1.2.4.7-2  sa yad agnau samidham ādadhāti ya evāsyāgnau pādas tameva tena parikrīṇāti taṃ saṃskṛtyātman dhatte sa enam āviśati |
BDhS 1.2.4.7-3  atha yad ātmānaṃ daridrīkrityāhrīr bhūtvā bhikṣatebrahmacaryaṃ carati ya evāsya mṛtyau pādas tam eva tena parikrīṇātitaṃ saṃskṛtyātman dhatte sa enam āviśati |
BDhS 1.2.4.7-4  atha yad ācāryavacaḥ karoti ya evāsyācārye pādas tameva tena parikrīṇāti taṃ saṃskṛtyātman dhatte sa enam āviśati |
BDhS 1.2.4.7-5  atha yat svādhyāyam adhīte ya evāsyātmani pādas tameva tena parikrīṇāti taṃ saṃskṛtyātman dhatte sa enam āviśati |
BDhS 1.2.4.7-6  na ha vai snātvā bhikṣeta | api ha vai snātvā bhikṣāṃcaraty api jñātīnām aśanāyāpi pitṝṇām anyābhyaḥ kriyābhyaḥ |
BDhS 1.2.4.7-7  sa yad anyāṃ bhikṣitavyāṃ na vindetāpi svāmevācāryajāyāṃ bhikṣetātho svāṃ mātaram |
BDhS 1.2.4.7-8  nainaṃ saptamy abhikṣitātīyāt |
BDhS 1.2.4.7-9ab  bhaikṣasyācaraṇe doṣaḥ pāvakasyāsamindhane |
BDhS 1.2.4.7-9cd  saptarātram akṛtvaitad avakīrṇivrataṃ caret ||
BDhS 1.2.4.7-10  tam evaṃ vidvāṃsam evaṃ carantaṃ sarve vedā āviśanti ||
BDhS 1.2.4.8  yathā ha vā agniḥ samiddho rocata evaṃ ha vay eṣa snātvārocate ya evaṃ vidvān brahmacaryaṃ caratīti brāhmaṇam | itibrāhmaṇam ||
BDhS 1.3.5.1  atha snātakasya ||
BDhS 1.3.5.2  antarvāsa uttarīyam ||
BDhS 1.3.5.3  vaiṇavaṃ daṇḍaṃ dhārayet ||
BDhS 1.3.5.4  sodakaṃ ca kamaṇḍalum ||
BDhS 1.3.5.5  dviyajñopavītī ||
BDhS 1.3.5.6  uṣṇīṣam ajinam uttarīyam upānahau chattraṃ copāsanaṃdarśapūrṇamāsau ||
BDhS 1.3.5.7  parvasu ca keśaśmaśrulomanakhavāpanam ||
BDhS 1.3.5.8  tasya vṛttiḥ ||
BDhS 1.3.5.9  brāhmaṇarājanyavaiśyarathakāreṣv āmaṃ lipseta ||
BDhS 1.3.5.10  bhaikṣaṃ vā ||
BDhS 1.3.5.11  vāgyatas tiṣṭhet ||
BDhS 1.3.5.12  sarvāṇi cāsya devapitṛsaṃyuktāni pākayajñasaṃsthānibhūtikarmāni kurvīteti ||
BDhS 1.3.5.13  etena vidhinā prajāpateḥ parameṣṭhinaḥ paramaṛṣayaḥ paramāṃkāṣṭhāṃ gacchantīti baudhāyanaḥ ||
BDhS 1.4.6.1  atha kamaṇḍalucaryām upadiśanti ||
BDhS 1.4.6.2-1ab  chāgasya dakṣiṇe karṇe pāṇau viprasya dakṣiṇe |
BDhS 1.4.6.2-1cd  apsu caiva kuśastambe pāvakaḥ paripaṭhyate ||
BDhS 1.4.6.2-2  tasmāc chaucaṃ kṛtvā pāṇinā parimṛjīta paryagnikaraṇaṃ hitat | uddīpyasva jātaveda iti punardāhād viśiṣyate ||
BDhS 1.4.6.3  tatrāpi kiṃcit saṃspṛṣṭaṃ manasi manyeta [K: manyate] kuśairvā tṛṇair vā prajvālya pradakṣiṇaṃ paridahanam ||
BDhS 1.4.6.4  ata ūrdhvaṃ śvavāyasaprabhṛtyupahatānām agnivarṇa ityupadiśanti ||
BDhS 1.4.6.5  mūtrapurīṣalohita[K: rohita]retaḥprabhṛtyupahatānāmutsargaḥ ||
BDhS 1.4.6.6  bhagne kamaṇḍalau vyāhṛtibhiḥ śataṃ juhuyāj japed vā ||
BDhS 1.4.6.7-1  bhūmir bhūmim agān mātā mātaram apy agāt | bhūyāsmaputraiḥ paśubhir yo no dveṣṭi sa bhidyatām iti || [= ĀśvŚS 3.14.12, ApŚS 30.20.9, ĀpMP 2.15.17; cf. ŚaḍvBr 1.6.20, KauśŚS 30.20.9, KauśS 136.2]
BDhS 1.4.6.7-2  kapālāni saṃhṛtyāpsu prakṣipya sāvitrīṃ daśāvarāṃkṛtvā punar evānyaṃ gṛhṇīyāt ||
BDhS 1.4.6.8  varuṇam āśritya | etat te varuṇa punar eva mām om iti | akṣaraṃ dhyāyet ||
BDhS 1.4.6.9ab  śūdrād gṛhya śataṃ kuryād vaiśyād ardhaśataṃ smṛtam |
BDhS 1.4.6.9cd  kṣatriyāt pañcaviṃśat tu brāhmaṇād daśa kīrtitāḥ ||
BDhS 1.4.6.10  astamita āditya udakaṃ gṛhṇīyān na gṛhṇīyād itimīmāṃsante brahmavādinaḥ ||
BDhS 1.4.6.11  gṛhṇīyād ity etad aparam ||
BDhS 1.4.6.12  yāvad udakaṃ gṛhṇīyāt tāvat prāṇam [K: prāṇān] āyacchet ||
BDhS 1.4.6.13  agnir ha vai hy udakaṃ gṛhṇāti ||
BDhS 1.4.6.14  kamaṇḍalūdakenābhiṣiktapāṇipādo yāvad ārdraṃ tāvadaśuciḥ pareṣām | ātmānam eva pūtam karoti | nānyat karmakurvīteti vijñāyate ||
BDhS 1.4.6.15  api vā pratiśaucam ā maṇibandhāc chucir iti baudhāyanaḥ ||
BDhS 1.4.6.16  athāpy udāharanti ||
BDhS 1.4.7.1-1ab  kamaṇḍalur dvijātīnāṃ śaucārthaṃ vihitaḥ purā |
BDhS 1.4.7.1-1cd  brahmaṇā munimukhyaiś ca tasmāt taṃ dhārayet sadā ||
BDhS 1.4.7.1-2ab  tataḥ śaucaṃ tataḥ pānaṃ saṃdhyopāsanam eva ca |
BDhS 1.4.7.1-2cd  nirviśaṅkena kartavyaṃ yadīcchec chreya ātmanaḥ ||
BDhS 1.4.7.2  kuryāc chuddhena manasā na cittaṃ dūṣayed budhaḥ | sahakamaṇḍalunotpannaḥ svayaṃbhūs tasmāt kamaṇḍalunā caret ||
BDhS 1.4.7.3  mūtrapurīṣe kurvan dakṣiṇe haste gṛhṇāti savya ācamanīyam | etat sidhyati sādhūnām ||

BDhS 2.3.6.40cd  bahirjānu na kāryāṇi tadvad ācamanaṃ smṛtam ||
BDhS 2.3.6.41ab  anne śritāni bhūtāni annaṃ prāṇam iti śrutiḥ |
BDhS 2.3.6.41cd  tasmād annaṃ pradātavyam annaṃ hi paramaṃ haviḥ ||
BDhS 2.3.6.42-1ab  hutena śāmyate pāpaṃ hutam annena śāmyati |
BDhS 2.3.6.42-1cd  annaṃ dakṣiṇayā śāntim upayātīti na śrutiḥ [K: naśśrutir iti] |
BDhS 2.3.6.42-2  upayātīti naḥ śrutir iti || [K omits]
BDhS 2.4.7.1  athātaḥ saṃdhyopāsanavidhiṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ ||
BDhS 2.4.7.2  tīrthaṃ gatvāprayato 'bhiṣiktaḥ prayato vānabhiṣiktaḥprakṣālitapādapāṇir apa ācamya surabhimatyābliṅgābhirvāruṇībhir hiraṇyavarṇābhiḥ pāvamānībhir vyāhṛtibhir anyaiś capavitrair ātmānaṃ prokṣya prayato bhavati ||
BDhS 2.4.7.3-1  athāpy udāharanti |
BDhS 2.4.7.3-2ab  apovagāhanaṃ snānaṃ vihitaṃ sārvavarṇikam |
BDhS 2.4.7.3-2cd  mantravatprokṣaṇaṃ cāpi dvijātīnāṃ viśiṣyate || iti ||
BDhS 2.4.7.4  sarvakarmaṇāṃ caivārambheṣu prāk saṃdhyopāsanakālāccaitenaiva pavitrasamūhenātmānaṃ prokṣya prayato bhavati ||
BDhS 2.4.7.5  athāpy udāharanti | darbheṣv āsīno darbhān dhārayamāṇaḥsodakena pāṇinā pratyaṅmukhaḥ sāvitrīṃ sahasrakṛtva āvartayet ||
BDhS 2.4.7.6  prāṇāyāmaśo vā śatakṛtvaḥ ||
BDhS 2.4.7.7  ubhayataḥpraṇavāṃ sasaptavyāhṛtikāṃ manasā vā daśakṛtvaḥ ||
BDhS 2.4.7.8  tribhiś ca prāṇāyāmais tānto brahmahṛdayena ||
BDhS 2.4.7.9  vāruṇībhyāṃ rātrim upatiṣṭhate | imaṃ me varuṇa | tat tvāyāmīti | dvābhyām ||
BDhS 2.4.7.10  evam eva prātaḥ prāṅmukhas tiṣṭhan ||
BDhS 2.4.7.11  maitrībhyām ahar upatiṣṭhate | mitrasya carṣaṇīdhṛtaḥ | mitrojanān yātayatīti | dvābhyām ||
BDhS 2.4.7.12  supūrvām api pūrvām upakramyodita āditye samāpnuyāt ||
BDhS 2.4.7.13  anastamita upakramya supaścād api paścimām ||
BDhS 2.4.7.14  saṃdhyayoś ca saṃpattāv ahorātrayoś ca saṃtatyai [K: santatiḥ] ||
BDhS 2.4.7.15-1  api cātra prajāpatigītau ślokau bhavataḥ |
BDhS 2.4.7.15-2ab  anāgatāṃ tu ye pūrvām anatītāṃ tu paścimām |
BDhS 2.4.7.15-2cd  saṃdhyāṃ nopāsate viprāḥ kathaṃ te brāhmaṇāḥ smṛtāḥ ||
BDhS 2.4.7.15-3ab  sāyaṃ prātaḥ sadā saṃdhyāṃ ye viprā no upāsate |
BDhS 2.4.7.15-3cd  kāmaṃ tān dhārmiko rājā śūdrakarmasu yojayed || iti ||
BDhS 2.4.7.16  tatra sāyamatikrame rātryupavāsaḥprātaratikrame 'harupavāsaḥ ||
BDhS 2.4.7.17  sthānāsanaphalam avāpnoti ||
BDhS 2.4.7.18-1  athāpy udāharanti |
BDhS 2.4.7.18-2ab  yad upasthakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ padbhyāṃ vā yat kṛtaṃ bhavet |
BDhS 2.4.7.18-2cd  bāhubhyāṃ manasā vāpi vācā vā yat kṛtaṃ bhavet |
BDhS 2.4.7.18-2ef  sāyaṃ saṃdhyām upasthāya tena tasmāt pramucyate ||
BDhS 2.4.7.19  rātryā cāpi saṃdhīyate na cainaṃ varuṇo gṛhṇāti ||
BDhS 2.4.7.20  evam eva prātar upasthāya rātrikṛtāt pāpāt pramucyate ||
BDhS 2.4.7.21  ahnā cāpi saṃdhīyate mitraś cainaṃ gopāyaty ādityaścainaṃ svargaṃ lokam unnayati ||

BDhS 2.5.8.1  atha hastau prakṣālya kamaṇḍaluṃ mṛtpiṇḍaṃ ca saṃgṛhya [K:gṛhya] tīrthaṃ gatvā triḥ pādau prakṣālayate trir ātmānaṃ ||
BDhS 2.5.8.2  atha haike bruvate | śmaśānam āpo devagṛhaṃ goṣṭhaṃ yatra cabrāhmaṇā aprakṣālya pādau tan na praveṣṭavyam iti ||
BDhS 2.5.8.3-1  athāpo 'bhiprapadyate |
BDhS 2.5.8.3-2ab  hiraṇyaśṛṅgaṃ varuṇaṃ prapadye tīrthaṃ me dehi yācitaḥ |
BDhS 2.5.8.3-2cd  yan mayā bhuktam asādhūnāṃ pāpebhyaś ca pratigrahaḥ ||
BDhS 2.5.8.3-3ab  yan me manasā vācā karmaṇā vā duṣkṛtaṃ kṛtam |
BDhS 2.5.8.3-3cd  tan na indro varuṇo bṛhaspatiḥ savitā ca punantupunaḥpunaḥ || iti || [K: tan ma]
BDhS 2.5.8.4  athāñjalināpa upahanti | sumitrā na āpa oṣadhayaḥ santv iti || [K: athāñjalinā upahanti]
BDhS 2.5.8.5  tāṃ diśaṃ nirukṣati yasyām asya diśi dveṣyo bhavati |durmitrās tasmai bhūyāsur yo 'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ ca vayaṃ dviṣma iti ||
BDhS 2.5.8.6  athāpa upaspṛśya triḥ pradakṣiṇam udakam āvartayati | yadapāṃ kruraṃ yad amedhyaṃ yad aśāntaṃ tadapagacchatād iti || [K: yad arpāṃ krūraṃ... apa gacchatād]
BDhS 2.5.8.7  apsu nimajjyonmajjya ||

BDhS 2.5.8.9  yady uparuddhāḥ syur etenopatiṣṭhate | namo 'gnaye 'psumatenama indrāya namo varuṇāya namo vāruṇyai namo 'dbhya iti ||
BDhS 2.5.8.10-1  uttīryācamyācāntaḥ punar ācāmet |
BDhS 2.5.8.10-2  āpaḥ punantu pṛthivīṃ pṛthivī pūtāpunātu mām | punantu brahmaṇaspatir brahma pūtā punātu mām || [K: pṛthivī pṛthivī]
BDhS 2.5.8.10-3  yad ucchiṣṭam abhojyaṃ yad vā duścaritaṃ mama | sarvaṃpunantu mām āpo 'satāṃ ca pratigrahaṃ svāheti ||
BDhS 2.5.8.11  pavitre kṛtvādbhir mārjayati | āpo hi ṣṭhā mayobhuva ititisṛbhiḥ | hiraṇyavarṇāḥ śucayaḥ pāvakā iti catasṛbhiḥ | pavamānaḥ suvarjana iti | etenānuvākenamārjayitvāntarjalagato 'ghamarṣaṇena trīn prāṇāyāmāndhārayitvottīrya vāsaḥ pīḍayitvā prakṣālitopavātānyakliṣṭāni vāsāṃsi paridhāyāpa ācamya darbheṣv āsīno darbhāndhārayamāṇaḥ prāṅmukhaḥ sāvitrīṃ sahasrakṛtvaāvartayec chatakṛtvo 'parimitakṛtvo vā daśāvaram || [K: suvarcana]
BDhS 2.5.8.12  athādityam upatiṣṭhate | ud vayaṃ tamasas pari | ud u tyam | citram | tac cakṣur devahitam | ya udagād iti ||

BDhS 2.5.8.14  pūtaḥ pañcabhir brahmayajñair athottaraṃ devatās tarpayati ||
BDhS 2.5.9.1  om agniḥ prajāpatiḥ somo rudro 'ditir bṛhaspatiḥsarpā ity etāni prāgdvārāṇi daivatāni sanakṣatrāṇi sagrahāṇisāhorātrāṇi samuhūrtāni tarpayāmi | oṃ vasūṃś ca tarpayāmi ||
BDhS 2.5.9.2  oṃ pitaro 'ryamā bhagaḥ savitā tvaṣṭā vāyurindrāgnī ity etāni dakṣiṇadvārāṇi daivatāni sanakṣatrāṇisagrahāṇi sāhorātrāṇi samuhūrtāni tarpayāmi | oṃ rudrāṃś catarpayāmi || [K generally omits: oṃ]
BDhS 2.5.9.3  oṃ mitra indro mahāpitara āpo viśve devā brahmāviṣṇur ity etāni pratyagdvārāṇi daivatāni sanakṣatrāṇi sagrahāṇisāhorātrāṇi samuhūrtāni tarpayāmi | om ādityāṃś ca tarpayāmi ||

BDhS 2.5.10.1  atha prācīnāvīti | oṃ pitṝn svadhā namas tarpayāmi | oṃ pitāmahān svadhā namas tarpayāmi | oṃ prapitāmahān svadhā namas tarpayāmi | oṃ mātṝḥ svadhānamas tarpayāmi | oṃ pitāmahīḥ svadhā namas tarpayāmi | oṃ prapitāmahīḥ svadhā namas tarpayāmi | oṃ mātāmahān svadhā namas tarpayāmi | oṃ mātuḥ pitāmahānsvadhā namas tarpayāmi | oṃ mātuḥ prapitāmahān svadhānamas tarpayāmi | oṃ mātāmahīḥ svadhā namas tarpayāmi | oṃ mātuḥ pitāmahīḥ svadhā namas tarpayāmi | oṃ mātuḥ prapitāmahīḥ svadhā namas tarpayāmi ||
BDhS 2.5.10.2  om ācāryān svadhā namas tarpayāmi | om ācāryapatnīḥ svadhā namas tarpayāmi | oṃ gurūn svadhānamas tarpayāmi | oṃ gurupatnīḥ svadhā namas tarpayāmi | oṃ sakhīn svadhā namas tarpayāmi | oṃ sakhipatnīḥ svadhā namas tarpayāmi | oṃ jñātīn svadhānamas tarpayāmi | oṃ jñātipatnīḥ svadhā namastarpayāmi | om amātyān svadhā namas tarpayāmi | om amātyapatnīḥ svadhā namas tarpayāmi | oṃ sarvān svadhā namas tarpayāmi | oṃ sarvāḥ svadhā namastarpayāmīti ||

BDhS 2.6.11.2  aharahaḥ svāhākuryād ā kāṣṭhāt | tathaitaṃdevayajñaṃ samāpnoti ||
BDhS 2.6.11.3  aharahaḥ svadhākuryād odapātrāt | tathaitaṃpitṛyajñaṃ samāpnoti ||

BDhS 2.6.11.5  aharahar brāhmaṇebhyo 'nnaṃ dadyād ā mūlaphalaśākebhyaḥ | tathaitaṃ manuṣyayajñaṃ samāpnoti ||
BDhS 2.6.11.6  aharahaḥ svādhyāyaṃ kuryād ā praṇavāt | tathaitaṃ brahmayajñaṃ samāpnoti ||
BDhS 2.6.11.7  svādhyāyo vai brahmayajñaḥ | tasya ha vā etasyabrahmayajñasya vāg eva juhūr mana upabhṛc cakṣur dhruvā medhā sruvaḥsatyam avabhṛthaḥ svargo loka udayanam | yāvantaṃ ha vā imāṃ vittasya pūrṇāṃ dadat svargaṃ lokaṃ jayati tāvantaṃ lokaṃ jayati bhūyāṃsaṃ cākṣayyaṃ cāpa punarmṛtyuṃ jayatiya evaṃ vidvān svādhyāyam adhīte | tasmāt svādhyāyo 'dhyetavya iti hi brāhmaṇam || [K omits:tāvantaṃ lokaṃ jayati]
BDhS 2.6.11.8  athāpy udāharanti | svabhyaktaḥ suhitaḥ sukhe śayaneśayāno yaṃ yaṃ kratum adhīte tena tenāsyeṣṭaṃ bhavatīti ||

	[On the Āśramas]

BDhS 2.7.12.3  nyastam annaṃ mahāvyāhṛtibhiḥ pradakṣiṇam udakaṃ pariṣicyasavyena pāṇināvimuñcan | amṛtopastaraṇam asīti | purastād apaḥpītvā pañcānnena prāṇāhutīr juhoti | prāṇe niviṣṭo 'mṛtaṃjuhomi | śivo mā viśāpradāhāya | prāṇāya svāhā | apāneniviṣṭo 'mṛtaṃ juhomi | śivo mā viśāpradāhāya | apānāya svāhā | vyāne niviṣṭo 'mṛtaṃ juhomi
| śivo mā viśāpradāhāya | vyānāyasvāhā | udāne niviṣṭo 'mṛtaṃ juhomi | śivo mā viśāpradāhāya | udānāya svāhā | samāne niviṣṭo 'mṛtaṃ juhomi | śivo māviśāpradāhāya | samānāya svāheti || [K omits: apāne niviṣṭo samānāya svāhā]

BDhS 2.10.17.1  athātaḥ saṃnyāsavidhiṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ ||
BDhS 2.10.17.2  so 'ta eva brahmacaryavān pravrajatīty ekeṣām ||
BDhS 2.10.17.3  atha śālīnayāyāvarāṇām anapatyānām ||
BDhS 2.10.17.4  vidhuro vā prajāḥ svadharme pratiṣṭhāpya vā ||
BDhS 2.10.17.5  saptatyā ūrdhvaṃ saṃnyāsam upadiśanti ||
BDhS 2.10.17.6  vānaprasthasya vā karmavirāme ||
BDhS 2.10.17.7ab  eṣa nityo mahimā brāhmaṇasya na karmaṇā vardhate nokanīyān |
BDhS 2.10.17.7cd  tasyaivātmā padavit taṃ viditvā na karmaṇā lipyatepāpakena || iti || {cf. BDhS 2.6.11.30}
BDhS 2.10.17.8  apunarbhavaṃ nayatīti nityaḥ ||
BDhS 2.10.17.9  mahad enaṃ gamayatīti mahimā ||
BDhS 2.10.17.10  keśaśmaśrulomanakhāni vāpayitvopakalpayate ||
BDhS 2.10.17.11  yaṣṭayaḥ śikyaṃ jalapavitraṃ kamaṇḍaluṃ pātram iti ||
BDhS 2.10.17.12  etat samādāya grāmānte grāmasīmānte 'gnyagārevājyaṃ payo dadhīti trivṛt prāśyopavaset ||
BDhS 2.10.17.13  apo vā ||
BDhS 2.10.17.14  oṃ bhūḥ sāvitrīṃ praviśāmi tat savitur vareṇyam | oṃbhuvaḥ sāvitrīṃ praviśāmi bhargo devasya dhīmahi | oṃ suvaḥ sāvitrīṃ praviśāmi dhiyo yo naḥ pracodayād iti | paccho 'rdharcaśastataḥ samastayā ca vyastayā ca ||
BDhS 2.10.17.15  ātmānam ātmana āśramādāśramam upanīya brahmapūto bhavatīti vijñāyate || [K omits: ātmānam ātmana]
BDhS 2.10.17.16-1  athāpy udāharanti |
BDhS 2.10.17.16-2ab  āśramād āśramaṃ gatvā hutahomo jitendriyaḥ |
BDhS 2.10.17.16-2cd  bhikṣābalipariśrāntaḥ paścād bhavati bhikṣukaḥ || iti ||
BDhS 2.10.17.17  sa eṣa bhikṣur ānantyāya ||
BDhS 2.10.17.18  purādityasyāstamayād gārhapatyamupasamādhāyānvāhāryapacanam āhṛtya jvalantam āhavanīyamuddhṛtya gārhapatya ājyaṃ vilāpyotpūya sruci caturgṛhītaṃgṛhītvā samidvaty āhavanīye pūrṇāhutiṃ juhoti | oṃ svāheti ||
BDhS 2.10.17.19  etad brahmānvādhānam iti vijñāyate ||
BDhS 2.10.17.20  atha sāyaṃ hute 'gnihotra uttareṇa gārhapatyaṃ tṛṇānisaṃstīrya teṣu dvaṃdvaṃ nyañci pātrāṇi sādayitvādakṣiṇenāhavanīyaṃ brahmāyatane darbhān saṃstīrya teṣukṛṣṇājinaṃ cāntardhāyaitāṃ rātriṃ jāgarti ||
BDhS 2.10.17.21  ya evaṃ vidvān brahmarātrim upoṣya brāhmaṇo 'gnīn samāropya pramīyate sarvaṃ pāpmānaṃ tarati taratibrahmahatyām || [K: upoṣyāgnīn]
BDhS 2.10.17.22  atha brāhme muhūrta utthāya kāla eva prātaragnihotraṃjuhoti ||
BDhS 2.10.17.23  atha pṛṣṭhyāṃ stīrtvāpaḥ praṇīya vaiśvānaraṃdvādaśakapālaṃ nirvapati | sā prasiddheṣṭiḥ saṃtiṣṭhate ||
BDhS 2.10.17.24  āhavanīye 'gnihotrapātrāṇi prakṣipaty amṛnmayānyanaśmamayāni || [K: prakṣiped amṛṇmayāny anāyasāni]
BDhS 2.10.17.25  gārhapatye 'raṇī | bhavataṃ naḥ samanasāv iti ||
BDhS 2.10.17.26  ātmany agnīn samāropayate | yā te agneyajñiyā tanūr iti tris trir ekaikaṃ samājighrati || [K: athātmany]
BDhS 2.10.17.27  athāntarvedi tiṣṭhan | oṃ bhūr bhuvaḥ suvaḥ saṃnyastaṃmayā saṃnyastaṃ mayā saṃnyastaṃ mayeti | trir upāṃśūktvā triruccaiḥ ||
BDhS 2.10.17.28  triṣatyā hi devā iti vijñāyate ||
BDhS 2.10.17.29  abhayaṃ sarvabhūtebhyo matta iti cāpāṃ pūrṇam añjaliṃninayati ||
BDhS 2.10.17.30-1  athāpy udāharanti |
BDhS 2.10.17.30-2ab  abhayaṃ sarvabhūtebhyo dattvā yaś carate muniḥ |
BDhS 2.10.17.30-2cd  na tasya sarvabhūtebhyo bhayaṃ cāpīha jāyate || iti || [K: cāpi ha]
BDhS 2.10.17.31  sa vācaṃyamo bhavati ||
BDhS 2.10.17.32  sakhā mā gopāyeti daṇḍam ādatte || [K: me]
BDhS 2.10.17.33  yad asya pāre rajasa iti śikyaṃ gṛhṇāti ||
BDhS 2.10.17.34  yena devāḥ pavitreṇeti jalapavitraṃ gṛhṇāti ||
BDhS 2.10.17.35  yena devā jyotiṣordhvā udāyann iti kamaṇḍaluṃ gṛhṇāti ||
BDhS 2.10.17.36  saptavyāhṛtibhiḥ pātraṃ gṛhṇāti ||
BDhS 2.10.17.37  yaṣṭayaḥ śikyaṃ jalapavitraṃ kamaṇḍaluṃ pātram ity etatsamādāya yatrāpas tatra [K: tad] gatvā snātvāpa ācamyasurabhimatyābliṅgābhir vāruṇībhir hiraṇyavarṇābhiḥ pāvamānībhiriti mārjayitvāntarjalagato 'ghamarṣaṇena ṣoḍaśa prāṇāyāmāndhārayitvottīrya vāsaḥ pīḍayitvānyat prayataṃ vāsaḥparidhāyāpa ācamya | oṃ bhūr bhuvaḥ suvar iti | jalapavitramādāya tarpayati | oṃ bhūs tarpayāmi | oṃ bhuvas tarpayāmi | oṃsuvas tarpayāmi | oṃ mahas tarpayāmi | oṃ janas tarpayāmi | oṃ tapastarpayāmi | oṃ satyaṃ tarpayāmīti ||
BDhS 2.10.17.38  devavat pitṛbhyo 'ñjalim ādāya [K:upādāya] | oṃ bhūḥ svadhā | oṃ bhuvaḥ svadhā | oṃ suvaḥ svadhā | oṃbhūr bhuvaḥ suvar mahar nama iti || [K omits: devavat]
BDhS 2.10.17.39  atha | ud u tyam | citram iti | dvābhyām ādityam upatiṣṭhate ||
BDhS 2.10.17.40  om iti brahma brahma vā eṣa jyotir ya eṣa tapaty eṣa vedo ya eṣa tapati vedyam evaitad ya eṣa tapati | evam evaiṣa ātmānaṃ tarpayati | ātmane namaskaroti | ātmābrahmātmā jyotiḥ || [K: om eṣa jyotiḥ ya eṣa jyotiḥ ya eṣa tarpatyaiṣa vedā ya eva tarpayati vedyam evaitad ya eṣa tarpayati...]
BDhS 2.10.17.41  sāvitrīṃ sahasrakṛtva āvartayec chatakṛtvo 'parimitakṛtvo vā ||
BDhS 2.10.17.42  oṃ bhūr bhuvaḥ suvar iti jalapavitram ādāyāpo gṛhṇāti || [K: pavitram]
BDhS 2.10.17.43  nāta ūrdhvam anuddhṛtābhir adbhiraparisrutābhir aparipūtābhir vācāmet || [K: na cāta]
BDhS 2.10.17.44  na cāta ūrdhvaṃ śuklaṃ vāso dhārayet ||
BDhS 2.10.18.1  ekadaṇḍī tridaṇḍī vā ||
BDhS 2.10.18.2  athemāni vratāni bhavanti | ahiṃsā satyam astainyaṃmaithunasya ca varjanam | tyāga ity eva ||
BDhS 2.10.18.3  pañcaivopavratāni bhavanti | akrodhoguruśuśrūṣāpramādaḥ śaucam āhāraśuddhiś ceti ||
BDhS 2.10.18.4  atha bhaikṣacaryā | brāhmaṇānāṃ śālīnayāyāvarāṇāmapavṛtte vaiśvadeve bhikṣāṃ lipseta ||
BDhS 2.10.18.5  bhavatpūrvāṃ pracodayet [K: pracodayāt] ||
BDhS 2.10.18.6  godohamātram [K: godohanamātram] ākāṅkṣet ||
BDhS 2.10.18.7  atha bhaikṣacaryād upāvṛtya śucau deśenyasya hastapādān prakṣālyādityasyāgraṃ nivedayet | ud u tyam | citram iti | brahmaṇe nivedayate | brahma jajñānam iti || [K: upāvṛttaḥ ... agre]
BDhS 2.10.18.8  vijñāyate | ādhānaprabhṛti yajamāna evāgnayo bhavanti | tasya prāṇo gārhapatyo 'pāno 'nvāhāryapacano vyāna āhavanīyaudānasamānau sabhyāvasathyau | pañca vā ete 'gnaya ātmasthāḥ |ātmany eva juhoti ||
BDhS 2.10.18.9  sa eṣa ātmayajña ātmaniṣṭha ātmapratiṣṭha ātmānaṃkṣemaṃ nayatīti vijñāyate ||
BDhS 2.10.18.10  bhūtebhyo dayāpūrvaṃ saṃvibhajya śeṣam adbhiḥ saṃspṛśyauṣadhavat prāśnīyāt ||
BDhS 2.10.18.11  prāśyāpa ācamya jyotiṣmatyādityam upatiṣṭhate | udvayaṃ tamasas parīti | vāṅ ma āsan nasoḥ prāṇa iti japitvā || [K:prāśyāpa ācamya vāṅ ma āsan nasoḥ prāṇa iti japitvājyotiṣmatyādityam upatiṣṭhate ud vayaṃ tamasas parīti]
BDhS 2.10.18.12ab  ayācitam asaṃkḷptam upapannaṃ yadṛcchayā |
BDhS 2.10.18.12cd  āhāramātraṃ bhuñjīta kevalaṃ prāṇayātrikam || iti ||
BDhS 2.10.18.13-1  athāpy udāharanti |
BDhS 2.10.18.13-2ab  aṣṭau grāsā muner bhakṣyāḥ ṣoḍaśāraṇyavāsinaḥ |
BDhS 2.10.18.13-2cd  dvātriṃśat tu gṛhasthasya amitaṃ brahmacāriṇaḥ || [K: dvātriṃśataṃ]
BDhS 2.10.18.14ab  bhaikṣaṃ vā sarvavarṇebhya ekānnaṃ vā dvijātiṣu |
BDhS 2.10.18.14cd  api vā sarvavarṇebhyo na caikānnaṃ dvijātiṣu || iti ||
BDhS 2.10.18.15-1  atha yatropaniṣadam ācāryā bruvate tatrodāharanti |
BDhS 2.10.18.15-2  sthānamaunavīrāsanasavanopasparśanacaturthaṣaṣṭhāṣṭamakālavratayuktasya kaṇapiṇyākayāvakadadhipayovratatvaṃ ceti ||
BDhS 2.10.18.16  tatra maune yuktas traividyavṛddhair ācāryair munibhiranyair vāśramibhir bahuśrutair dantair [K omits dantair] dantānsaṃdhāyāntarmukha eva yāvadarthasaṃbhāṣī na strībhir na yatra lopobhavatīti vijñāyate || [K: yāvadarthaṃ sambhāṣīta; omits: na strībhir]
BDhS 2.10.18.17  sthānamaunavīrāsanānām anyatamena saṃprayogaḥ | natrayaṃ saṃnipātayet ||
BDhS 2.10.18.18  yatra gataś ca yāvanmātram anuvratayed āpatsu na yatralopo bhavatīti vijñāyate ||
BDhS 2.10.18.19  sthānamaunavīrāsanasavanopasparśanacaturthaṣaṣṭhāṣṭamakālavratayuktasya | aṣṭau tāny avrataghnāni [K: avratadhvani]āpo mūlaṃ ghṛtaṃ payaḥ | havir brāhmaṇakāmyā ca guror vacanamauṣadham iti ||
BDhS 2.10.18.20  sāyaṃprātaragnihotramantrāñ japet ||
BDhS 2.10.18.21  vāruṇībhiḥ sāyaṃ saṃdhyām upasthāya maitrībhiḥ prātaḥ || [K:vāruṇobhis...upatiṣṭhate]
BDhS 2.10.18.22ab  anagnir aniketaḥ syād aśarmāśaraṇo muniḥ |
BDhS 2.10.18.22cd  bhaikṣārthī grāmam anvicchet svādhyāye vācamutsṛjed || iti ||
BDhS 2.10.18.23  vijñāyate ca | parimitā vā ṛcaḥ parimitāni sāmāniparimitāni yajūṃṣi | athaitasyaivānto nāsti yad brahma | tatpratigṛṇata ācakṣīta | sa pratigara iti ||
BDhS 2.10.18.24  evam evaiṣa ā śarīravimokṣaṇād vṛkṣamūlikovedasaṃnyāsī ||
BDhS 2.10.18.25  vedo vṛkṣaḥ | tasya mūlaṃ praṇavaḥ | praṇavātmako vedaḥ ||
BDhS 2.10.18.26  praṇavaṃ dhyāyan sapraṇavo brahmabhūyāya kalpata iti hovāca prajāpatiḥ || [K: praṇavo brahma praṇavaṃdhyāyet praṇavo]
BDhS 2.10.18.27  saptavyāhṛtibhir brahmabhājanaṃ prakṣālayed iti | prakṣālayed iti ||
BDhS 3.1.1  atha śālīnayāyāvaracakracaradharmakāṅkṣiṇāṃ navabhirvṛttibhir vartamānānām ||
BDhS 3.1.2  teṣāṃ tadvartanād vṛttir ity ucyate ||
BDhS 3.1.3  śālāśrayatvāc chālīnatvam ||
BDhS 3.1.4  vṛttyā varayā yātīti yāyāvaratvam ||
BDhS 3.1.5  anukrameṇa caraṇāc cakracaratvam || [K: anukramacaraṇāc]
BDhS 3.1.6  tā anuvyākhyāsyāmaḥ ||
BDhS 3.1.7  ṣaṇṇivartanī kauddālī dhruvā saṃprakṣālanīsamūhā phālanī śiloñchā kāpotāsiddheccheti navaitāḥ || [K: koddālī...pālinī siloñchā]
BDhS 3.1.8  tāsām eva vānyāpi daśamī vṛttir bhavati || [K: vṛddhir]
BDhS 3.1.9  ā navavṛtteḥ ||
BDhS 3.1.10  keśaśmaśrulomanakhāni vāpayitvopakalpayate ||
BDhS 3.1.11  kṛṣṇājinaṃ kamaṇḍaluṃ yaṣṭiṃ vīvadhaṃ kutapahāram iti || [K:kuthahārim]
BDhS 3.1.12  traidhātavīyeneṣṭvā prasthāsyati vaiśvānaryā vā ||
BDhS 3.1.13  atha prātar udita āditye yathāsūtram agnīnprajvālya gārhapatya ājyaṃ vilāpyotpūya sruksruvaṃ niṣṭapyasaṃmṛjya sruci caturgṛhītaṃ gṛhītvāhavanīye vāstoṣpatīyaṃjuhoti || [K omits atha]
BDhS 3.1.14  vāstoṣpate prati jānīhy asmān iti puronuvākyām anūcya | vāstoṣpate śagmayā saṃsadā ta iti yājyayā juhoti ||
BDhS 3.1.15  sarva evāhitāgnir ity eke ||
BDhS 3.1.16  yāyāvara ity eke ||
BDhS 3.1.17  nirgatya grāmānte grāmasīmānte vāvatiṣṭhate | tatrakuṭīṃ maṭhaṃ vā karoti kṛtaṃ vā praviśati ||
BDhS 3.1.18  kṛṣṇājinādīnām upakḷptānāṃ yasminn arthe yena yena yatprayojanaṃ tena tena tat kuryāt || [K: yasmin yasminn]
BDhS 3.1.19  prasiddham agnīnāṃ paricaraṇam | prasiddhaṃdarśapūrṇamāsābhyāṃ yajanam | prasiddhaḥ pañcānāṃ mahatāṃ yajñānāmanuprayogaḥ | utpannānām oṣadhīnāṃ nirvāpaṇaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ bhavati ||
BDhS 3.1.20  viśvebhyo devebhyo juṣṭaṃ nirvapāmīti vā tūṣṇīṃ vā tāḥsaṃskṛtya sādhayati ||
BDhS 3.1.21  tasyādhyāpanayājanapratigrahā nivartante 'nye cayajñakratava iti ||
BDhS 3.1.22  haviṣyaṃ ca vratopāyanīyaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ bhavati ||
BDhS 3.1.23  tad yathā sarpirmiśraṃ dadhimiśram akṣāralavaṇam apiśitam aparyuṣitam || [K omits: tad yathā]
BDhS 3.1.24  brahmacaryam ṛtau vā gacchati ||
BDhS 3.1.25  parvaṇiparvaṇi keśaśmaśrulomanakhavāpanaṃ śaucavidhiś ca ||
BDhS 3.1.26-1  athāpy udāharanti |
BDhS 3.1.26-2ab  śrūyate dvividhaṃ śaucaṃ yac chiṣṭaiḥ paryupāsitam |
BDhS 3.1.26-2cd  bāhyaṃ nirlepanirgandham antaḥśaucam ahiṃsakam || [K: ahiṃsanam]
BDhS 3.1.27ab  adbhiḥ śudhyanti gātrāṇi buddhir jñānena śudhyati | [K:śuddhyatīti]
BDhS 3.1.27cd  ahiṃsayā ca bhūtātmā manaḥ satyena śudhyati || iti ||
BDhS 3.2.1  yatho etat ṣaṇṇivartanīti ||
BDhS 3.2.2  ṣaḍ eva nivartanāni nirupahatāni karoti | svāmine bhāgamutsṛjaty anujñātaṃ vā gṛhṇāti ||
BDhS 3.2.3  prāk prātarāśāt karṣī syād asyūtanāsikābhyāṃsamuṣkābhyām atudann ārayā muhurmuhur abhyucchandayan || {= BDhS 2.2.4.20-21}
BDhS 3.2.4  etena vidhinā ṣaṇnivartanāni karotītiṣaṇṇivartanī || [K: ṣaṇṇivartanāni]
BDhS 3.2.5  kauddālīti | jalābhyāśe kuddālena vā phālena vā tīkṣṇakāṣṭhena vā khanati bījāny āvapati kandamūlaphalaśākauṣadhīr niṣpādayati ||
BDhS 3.2.6  kuddālena karotīti kauddālī ||
BDhS 3.2.7  dhruvayā vartamānaḥ śuklena vāsasā śiro veṣṭayati | bhūtyaitvā śiro veṣṭayāmīti | brahmavarcasam asi brahmavarcasāya tvetikṛṣṇājinam ādatte 'bliṅgābhiḥ pavitram | balam asi balāya [ḥ:valāya] tveti kamaṇḍalum | dhānyam asi puṣṭyai tveti vīvadham | sakhā mā gopāyeti daṇḍam ||
BDhS 3.2.8-1  athopaniṣkramya vyāhṛtīr japitvā diśām anumantraṇaṃ japati |
BDhS 3.2.8-2ab  pṛthivī cāntarikṣaṃ ca dyaur nakṣatrāṇi yā diśaḥ |
BDhS 3.2.8-2cd  agnir vāyuś ca sūryaś ca pāntu māṃ pathi devatāḥ || iti ||
BDhS 3.2.9  mānastokīyaṃ japitvā grāmaṃ praviśya gṛhadvāregṛhadvāraātmānaṃ vīvadhena saha darśanāt saṃdarśanīty ācakṣate ||
BDhS 3.2.10  vṛtter vṛtter avārttāyāṃ tayaiva tasya dhruvaṃ vartanāddhruveti parikīrtitā ||
BDhS 3.2.11  saṃprakṣālanīti | utpannānām oṣadhīnāṃ prakṣepaṇam | nikṣepaṇaṃ nāsti nicayo vā | bhājanāni saṃprakṣālya nyubjatītisaṃprakṣālanī ||
BDhS 3.2.12  samūheti | avāritasthāneṣu pathiṣu vā kṣetreṣuvāpratihatāvakāśeṣu vā yatra yatrauṣadhayo vidyante tatra tatrasamūhanyā samuhya tābhir vartayatīti samūhā ||
BDhS 3.2.13  phālanīty ahiṃsikety evedam uktaṃbhavati | tuṣavihīnāṃs taṇḍulān icchati sajjanebhyo bījāni vā | phālayatīti phālanī || [K: pālanīty...pālayatīti pālanī]
BDhS 3.2.14  śiloñcheti | avāritasthāneṣu pathiṣu vākṣetreṣu vāpratihatāvakāśeṣu vā yatra yatrauṣadhayo vidyante tatratatraikaikaṃ kaṇiśam uñchayitvā kālekāle śilair vartayatītiśiloñchā || [K: siloñcheti ... siloñchā]
BDhS 3.2.15  kāpoteti | avāritasthāneṣu pathiṣu vā [K omits: vā] kṣetreṣu vāpratihatāvakāśeṣu vā yatra yatrauṣadhayo vidyante tatratatrāṅgulībhyām ekaikām oṣadhim uñchayitvā saṃdaṃśanāt [K:sandarśanāt] kapotavad iti kāpotā ||
BDhS 3.2.16  siddheccheti | vṛttibhiḥ śrānto vṛddhatvād dhātukṣayād vāsajjanebhyaḥ siddham annam icchatīti siddhecchā ||

BDhS 3.3.1  atha vānaprasthadvaividhyam [K: vānaprasthasya dvaividhyam] ||

BDhS 3.3.3  tatra pacamānakāḥ pañcavidhāḥ sarvāraṇyakā vaituṣikāḥkandamūlabhakṣāḥ phalabhakṣāḥ śākabhakṣāś ceti ||
BDhS 3.3.4  tatra sarvāraṇyakā nāma dvividhā dvividham āraṇyamāśrayanta indrāvasiktā retovasiktāś ceti ||
BDhS 3.3.5  tatrendrāvasiktā nāma vallīgulmalatāvṛkṣāṇāmānayitvā śrapayitvā sāyaṃprātaragnihotraṃ hutvāyatyatithivratibhyaś ca dattvāthetarac cheṣabhakṣāḥ ||

BDhS 3.4.1  atha yadi brahmacāry avratyam iva caren māṃsaṃ vāśnīyātstriyaṃ vopeyāt sarvāsv evārtiṣu ||
BDhS 3.4.2  antarāgāre 'gnim upasamādhāya saṃparistīryāgnimukhātkṛtvā, athājyāhutīr upajuhoti | kāmena kṛtaṃkāmaḥ karoti kāmāyaivedaṃ sarvaṃ yo mā kārayati tasmai svāhā | manasā kṛtaṃ manaḥ karoti manasa evedaṃ sarvaṃ yo mā kārayati tasmaisvāhā | rajasā kṛtaṃ rajaḥ karoti rajasa evedaṃ sarvaṃ yo mākārayati tasmai svāhā | tamasā kṛtaṃ tamaḥ karoti tamasa evedaṃsarvaṃ yo mā kārayati tasmai svāhā | pāpmanā kṛtaṃ pāpmā karotipāpmana evedaṃ sarvaṃ yo mā kārayati tasmai svāhā | manyunā kṛtaṃmanyuḥ karoti manyava evedaṃ sarvaṃ yo mā kārayati tasmai svāheti || {cf. BDhS 3.8.12  nigrābhyā stheti | apaḥ pītvāthājyāhutīrupajuhoti | prāṇāpānavyānodānasamānā me śudhyantāṃ jyotirahaṃ virajā vipāpmā bhūyāsaṃ svāhā | vāṅmanaḥ | śiraḥpāṇi | tvakcarmamāṃsa [K: tvakcarma] | śabdasparśarūpa pṛthivyaptejo | [K: śabdasparśa pṛthivī]
annamayaprāṇamayamanomayavijñānamayānandamayā me śudhyantāṃ jyotir

BDhS 3.9.2  śucivāsāḥ syāc cīravāsā vā ||
BDhS 3.9.3  haviṣyam annam icched apaḥ phalāni vā ||
BDhS 3.9.4  grāmāt prācīṃ vodīcīṃ vā diśam upaniṣkramya gomayena gocarmamātraṃ caturaśraṃ sthaṇḍilam upalipya prokṣya lakṣaṇam ullikhya adbhir abhyukṣya agnim upasamādhāya saṃparistīryaitābhyo devatābhyo juhuyāt | agnaye svāhā | prajāpataye svāhā | somāyasvāhā [K: somāya svāhā prajāpataye svāhā] viśvebhyo devebhyaḥsvayaṃbhuva ṛgbhyo yajurbhyaḥ sāmabhyo 'tharvabhyaḥ śraddhāyai prajñāyaimedhāyai śriyai hriyai savitre sāvitryai sadasaspataye 'numataye ca || [K adds: vyāharen na cāntarā viramet]
BDhS 3.9.5  hutvā vedādim ārabheta saṃtatam adhīyīta || [K omits]
BDhS 3.9.6  nāntarā vyāharen na cāntarā viramet || [K omits]
BDhS 3.9.7  athāntarā vyāhared athāntarā viramet triḥ prāṇān āyamya vṛttāntād evārabheta || [K: trīn]
BDhS 3.9.8  apratibhāyāṃ yāvatā kālena na veda tāvantaṃ kālaṃ tadadhīyīta sa yadā jānīyād ṛkto yajuṣṭaḥ sāmataiti ||  [K: yaj jānīyāt] 
BDhS 3.9.9  tadbrāhmaṇaṃ takchāndasaṃ taddaivatam adhīyīta ||
BDhS 3.9.10  dvādaśa vedasaṃhitā adhīyīta | yadanenānadhyāye 'dhīyīta yad guravaḥ kopitā yāny akāryāṇi bhavantitābhiḥ punīte | śuddham asya pūtaṃ brahma bhavati ||
BDhS 3.9.11  ata ūrdhvaṃ saṃcayaḥ ||
BDhS 3.9.12  aparā dvādaśa vedasaṃhitā adhītya tābhir uśanaso lokamavāpnoti ||
BDhS 3.9.13  aparā dvādaśa vedasaṃhitā adhītya tābhir bṛhaspater lokamavāpnoti ||
BDhS 3.9.14  aparā dvādaśa vedasaṃhitā adhītya tābhiḥ prajāpater lokamavāpnoti ||
BDhS 3.9.15  anaśnan saṃhitā sahasram adhīyīta | brahmabhūto virajo [K:virājo] brahma bhavati ||
BDhS 3.9.16  saṃvatsaraṃ bhaikṣaṃ prayuñjāno divyaṃ cakṣur labhate ||
BDhS 3.9.17  ṣaṇ māsān yāvakabhakṣaś caturo māsān udakasaktubhakṣo dvaumāsau phalabhakṣo māsam abbhakṣo dvādaśarātraṃ vāprāśnan kṣipramantardhīyate jñātīn punāti saptāvarān sapta pūrvān ātmānaṃpañcadaśaṃ paṅktiṃ ca punāti ||
BDhS 3.9.18  tām etāṃ devaniśrayaṇīty [K: devaniśśrayaṇīty] ācakṣate ||
BDhS 3.9.19  etayā vai devā devatvam agacchann ṛṣaya ṛṣitvam ||
BDhS 3.9.20  tasya ha vā etasya yajñasya trividha evārambhakālaḥprātaḥsavane mādhyaṃdine savane brāhme vāpararātre ||
BDhS 3.9.21  taṃ vā etaṃ prajāpatiḥ saptarṣibhyaḥ provāca saptarṣayomahājajñave mahājajñur brāhmaṇebhyaḥ | brāhmaṇebhyaḥ ||
BDhS 3.10.1  ukto varṇadharmaś cāśramadharmaś ca ||
BDhS 3.10.2  atha khalv ayaṃ puruṣo yāpyena karmaṇā mithyā vā caratyayājyaṃ vā yājayaty apratigrāhyasya vā pratigṛhṇāty anāśyānnasyavānnam aśnāty acaraṇīyena vā carati ||
BDhS 3.10.3  tatra prāyaścittaṃ kuryān na kuryād iti mīmāṃsante [Komits mīmāṃsante] ||
BDhS 3.10.4  nahi karma kṣīyata iti ||
BDhS 3.10.5  kuryād ity eva || [K: kuryāt tv]
BDhS 3.10.6  punastomeneṣṭvā punaḥ savanam āyāntīti vijñāyate || [K: punastomena yajeta ... āyantīti]
BDhS 3.10.7  athāpy udāharanti | sarvaṃ pāpmānaṃ tarati tarati brahmahatyāṃ yo 'śvamedhena yajata iti ||
BDhS 3.10.8  agniṣṭutā vābhiśaṃsyamāno yajeteti ca || [K: vābhiśasyamāno]
BDhS 3.10.9  tasya niṣkrayaṇāni japas tapo homa upavāso dānam ||
BDhS 3.10.10  upaniṣado vedādayo vedāntāḥ sarvacchandaḥsu saṃhitā madhūnyaghamarṣaṇam atharvaśiro rudrāḥ puruṣasūktaṃ rājanarauhiṇe sāmanībṛhadrathaṃtare puruṣagatir mahānāmnyo mahāvairājaṃ mahādivākīrtyaṃjyeṣṭhasāmnām anyatamaṃ bahiṣpavamānaḥ kūśmāṇḍyaḥ pāvamānyaḥ sāvitrī ceti pāvanāni || [K: bahiṣpavamānaṃ]
BDhS 3.10.11  upasannyāyena payovratatā śākabhakṣatā phalabhakṣatāmūlabhakṣatā prasṛtiyāvako hiraṇyaprāśanaṃ ghṛtaprāśanaṃsomapānam iti medhyāni ||
BDhS 3.10.12  sarve śiloccayāḥ sarvāḥ sravantyaḥ saritaḥ puṇyā hradāstīrthāny ṛṣiniketanāni goṣṭhakṣetrapariṣkandā iti deśāḥ ||
BDhS 3.10.13  ahiṃsā satyam astainyaṃ savaneṣūdakopasparśanaṃguruśuśrūṣā brahmacaryam adhaḥśayanam ekavastratānāśaka ititapāṃsi ||
BDhS 3.10.14  hiraṇyaṃ gaur vāso 'śvo bhūmis tilā ghṛtam annam iti deyāni ||
BDhS 3.10.15  saṃvatsaraḥ ṣaṇmāsāś catvāras trayo dvāv ekaścaturviṃśatyaho dvādaśāhaḥ ṣaḍahas tryaho 'horātra ekāha itikālāḥ ||
BDhS 3.10.16  etāny anādeśe kriyeran ||
BDhS 3.10.17  enaḥsu guruṣu gurūṇi laghuṣu laghūni ||
BDhS 3.10.18  kṛcchrātikṛcchrau cāndrāyaṇam iti sarvaprāyaścittiḥ | sarvaprāyaścittiḥ ||

BDhS 4.4.7  api vā vyāhṛtīr vyastāḥ samastāś ceti trir antarjale paṭhansarvasmāt pāpāt pramucyate ||
BDhS 4.4.8  api vā praṇavam eva trir antarjale paṭhan sarvasmāt pāpātpramucyate ||
BDhS 4.4.9  tad etad dharmaśāstraṃ nāputrāya nāśiṣyāya nāsaṃvatsaroṣitāya dadyāt || [K: nābhaktāya nāputrāya]
BDhS 4.4.10  sahasraṃ dakṣiṇa ṛṣabhaikādaśaṃ guruprasādo vā | guruprasādo vā || [K: dakṣiṇā]
BDhS 4.5.1ab  athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi sāmaṛgyajuratharvaṇām |
BDhS 4.5.1cd  karmabhir yair avāpnoti kṣipraṃ kāmān manogatān ||
BDhS 4.5.2ab  japahomeṣṭiyantrādyaiḥ śodhayitvā svavigraham |
BDhS 4.5.2cd  sādhayet sarvakarmāṇi nānyathā siddhim aśnute ||
BDhS 4.5.3ab  japahomeṣṭiyantrāṇi kariṣyann ādito dvijaḥ |
BDhS 4.5.3cd  śuklapuṇyadinaṛkṣeṣu keśaśmaśrūṇi vāpayet ||
BDhS 4.5.4ab  snāyāt triṣavaṇaṃ pāyād ātmānaṃkrodhato 'nṛtāt |
BDhS 4.5.4cd  strīśūdrair nābhibhāṣeta brahmacārī havirvrataḥ ||
BDhS 4.5.5ab  goviprapitṛdevebhyo namaskuryād divāsvapan | [K: namaskurvan]
BDhS 4.5.5cd  japahomeṣṭiyantrastho divāsthāno niśāsanaḥ ||
BDhS 4.5.6ab  prājāpatyo bhavet kṛcchro divā rātrāv ayācitam |
BDhS 4.5.6cd  kramaśo vāyubhakṣaś ca dvādaśāhaṃ tryahaṃtryaham ||
BDhS 4.5.7ab  ahar ekaṃ tathā naktam ajñātaṃ vāyubhakṣaṇam |
BDhS 4.5.7cd  trivṛd eṣa parāvṛtto bālānāṃ kṛcchra ucyate ||
BDhS 4.5.8ab  ekaikaṃ grāsam aśnīyāt pūrvoktena tryahaṃtryaham |
BDhS 4.5.8cd  vāyubhakṣas tryahaṃ cānyad atikṛcchraḥ sa ucyate ||
BDhS 4.5.9ab  ambubhakṣas tryahān etān vāyubhakṣas tataḥ param |
BDhS 4.5.9cd  kṛcchrātikṛcchras tṛtīyo vijñeyaḥ so 'tipāvanaḥ ||
BDhS 4.5.10ab  tryahaṃtryahaṃ pibed uṣṇaṃ payaḥ sarpiḥ kuśodakam |
BDhS 4.5.10cd  vāyubhakṣas tryahaṃ cānyat taptakṛcchraḥ sa ucyate ||
BDhS 4.5.11ab  gomūtraṃ gomayaṃ kṣīraṃ dadhi sarpiḥ kuśodakam |
BDhS 4.5.11cd  ekarātropavāsaś ca kṛcchraḥ sāṃtapanaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
BDhS 4.5.12ab  gāyatryādāya gomūtraṃgandhadvāreti gomayam | [K: gāyatryāgṛhya]
BDhS 4.5.12cd  ā pyāyasveti ca kṣīraṃ dadhikrāvṇeti vai dadhi |
BDhS 4.5.12ef  śukram asi jyotir ity ājyaṃ devasyatveti kuśodakam || [K: jyotir asīty ... kuśodakam iti]
BDhS 4.5.13ab  gomūtrabhāgas tasyārdhaṃ śakṛt kṣīrasya tu trayam | [K: kṣīrasya tayam]
BDhS 4.5.13cd  dvayaṃ dadhno ghṛtasyaika ekaś ca kuśavāriṇaḥ |
BDhS 4.5.13ef  evaṃ sāṃtapanaḥ kṛcchraḥ śvapākam api śodhayet ||
BDhS 4.5.14ab  gomūtraṃ gomayaṃ caiva kṣīraṃ dadhi ghṛtaṃ tathā [K: gomūtraṃ gomayaṃ kṣīraṃ dadhi sarpiḥ kuśodakam] |
BDhS 4.5.14cd  pañcarātraṃ tadāhāraḥ pañcagavyena śudhyati ||
BDhS 4.5.15ab  yat ātmano 'pramattasya dvādaśāham abhojanam |
BDhS 4.5.15cd  parāko nāma kṛcchro 'yaṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśanaḥ ||
BDhS 4.5.16ab  gomūtrādibhir abhyastam ekaikaṃ taṃ trisaptakam |
BDhS 4.5.16cd  mahāsāṃtapanaṃ kṛcchraṃ vadanti brahmavādinaḥ ||
BDhS 4.5.17ab  ekavṛddhyā site piṇḍān ekahānyāsite tataḥ | [K: piṇḍe]
BDhS 4.5.17cd  pakṣayor upavāsau dvau tad dhi cāndrāyaṇaṃ smṛtam ||
BDhS 4.5.18ab  caturaḥ prātar aśnīyāt piṇḍān vipraḥ samāhitaḥ |
BDhS 4.5.18cd  caturo 'stamite sūrye śiśucāndrāyaṇaṃ caret || [K: smṛtam]
BDhS 4.5.19ab  aṣṭāvaṣṭau māsam ekaṃ piṇḍān madhyaṃdine sthite |
BDhS 4.5.19cd  niyatātmā haviṣyasya yaticāndrāyaṇaṃcaret || [K: -cāndāyaṇaṃ]
BDhS 4.5.20ab  yathā kathaṃcit piṇḍānāṃ dvijas tisras tv aśītayaḥ |
BDhS 4.5.20cd  māsenāśnan haviṣyasya candrasyaiti salokatām ||
BDhS 4.5.21ab  yathodyaṃś candramā hanti jagatas tamaso bhayam |
BDhS 4.5.21cd  evaṃ pāpād bhayaṃ hanti dvijaś cāndrāyaṇaṃ caran || [K: tathā pāpād]
BDhS 4.5.22ab  kaṇapiṇyākatakrāṇi yavācāmo 'nilāśanaḥ | [K: tathā cāpo]
BDhS 4.5.22cd  ekatripañcasapteti pāpaghno 'yaṃ tulāpumān ||
BDhS 4.5.23ab  yāvakaḥ saptarātreṇa vṛjinaṃ hanti dehinām |
BDhS 4.5.23cd  saptarātropavāso vā dṛṣṭam etan manīṣibhiḥ ||
BDhS 4.5.24ab  pauṣabhādrapadajyeṣṭhāsv ārdrākāśātapāśrayāt | [K: pauṣabhādrapadajyeṣṭhārdrā-]
BDhS 4.5.24cd  trīñ chuklān mucyate pāpāt patanīyād ṛte dvijaḥ ||
BDhS 4.5.25ab  gomūtraṃ gomayaṃ kṣīraṃ dadhi sarpiḥ kuśodakam |
BDhS 4.5.25cd  yavācāmena saṃyukto brahmakūrco 'tipāvanaḥ ||
BDhS 4.5.26ab  amāvāsyāṃ nirāhāraḥ paurṇamāsyāṃ tilāśanaḥ |
BDhS 4.5.26cd  śuklakṛṣṇakṛtāt pāpān mucyate abdasya parvabhiḥ ||
BDhS 4.5.27ab  bhaikṣāhāro 'gnihotribhyo māsenaikena śudhyati |
BDhS 4.5.27cd  yāyāvaravanasthebhyo daśabhiḥ pañcabhir dinaiḥ ||
BDhS 4.5.28ab  ekāhadhanino 'nnena dinenaikena śudhyati |
BDhS 4.5.28cd  kāpotavṛttiniṣṭhasya pītvāpaḥ śudhyate tribhiḥ ||
BDhS 4.5.29ab  ṛgyajuḥsāmavedānāṃ vedasyānyatamasya vā |
BDhS 4.5.29cd  pārāyaṇaṃ trir abhyasyed anaśnan so 'tipāvanaḥ ||
BDhS 4.5.30ab  atha cet tvarate kartuṃ divasaṃ mārutāśanaḥ | [K: divase]
BDhS 4.5.30cd  rātrau jalasthito vyuṣṭaḥ prājāpatyena tatsamam || [K: jale sthito]
BDhS 4.5.31ab  gāyatryāṣṭasahasraṃ tu japaṃ kṛtvotthite ravau |
BDhS 4.5.31cd  mucyate sarvapāpebhyo yadi na bhrūṇahā bhavet ||
BDhS 4.5.32ab  yo 'nnadaḥ satyavādī ca bhūteṣu kṛpayā sthitaḥ |
BDhS 4.5.32cd  pūrvoktayantraśuddhebhyaḥ sarvebhyaḥ so 'tiricyate ||
BDhS 4.6.1ab  samādhucchandasā rudrā gāyatrī praṇavānvitā |
BDhS 4.6.1cd  sapta vyāhṛtayaś caiva japyāḥ pāpavināśanāḥ || [K: jāpyāḥ]
BDhS 4.6.2ab  mṛgāreṣṭiḥ pavitreṣṭis trihaviḥ pāvamāny api |
BDhS 4.6.2cd  iṣṭayaḥ pāpanāśinyo vaiśvānaryā samanvitāḥ ||
BDhS 4.6.3ab  idaṃ caivāparaṃ guhyam ucyamānaṃ nibodhata |
BDhS 4.6.3cd  mucyate sarvapāpebhyo mahataḥ pātakād ṛte ||
BDhS 4.6.4ab  pavitrair mārjanaṃ kurvan rudraikādaśinīṃ [K:rudraikārdeśikāṃ] japan |
BDhS 4.6.4cd  pavitrāṇi ghṛtair juhvat prayacchan hemagotilān ||
BDhS 4.6.5ab  yo 'śnīyād yāvakaṃ pakvaṃ gomūtre saśakṛdrase |
BDhS 4.6.5cd  sadadhikṣīrasarpiṣke mucyate so 'ṃhasaḥ kṣaṇāt ||
BDhS 4.6.6ab  prasūto yaś ca śūdrāyāṃ yenāgamyā ca laṅghitā |
BDhS 4.6.6cd  saptarātrāt pramucyete vidhinaitena tāv ubhau ||
BDhS 4.6.7ab  retomūtrapurīṣāṇāṃ prāśane 'bhojyabhojane |
BDhS 4.6.7cd  paryādhānejyayor etat parivitte ca bheṣajam ||
BDhS 4.6.8ab  apātakāni karmāṇi kṛtvaiva subahūny api |
BDhS 4.6.8cd  mucyate sarvapāpebhya ity etad vacanaṃ satām ||
BDhS 4.6.9ab  mantramārgapramāṇaṃ tu vidhānaṃ samudīritam | [K: vidhāne]
BDhS 4.6.9cd  bharadvājādayo yena brahmaṇaḥ sātmatāṃ gatāḥ || [K: samatāṃ]
BDhS 4.6.10-1ab  prasannahṛdayo vipraḥ prayogād asya karmaṇaḥ |
BDhS 4.6.10-1cd  kāmāṃs tāṃs tān avāpnoti ye ye kāmā hṛdi sthitāḥ ||
BDhS 4.6.10-2  ye ye kāmā hṛdi sthitā iti ||
BDhS 4.7.1ab  nivṛttaḥ pāpakarmabhyaḥ pravṛttaḥ puṇyakarmasu |
BDhS 4.7.1cd  yo vipras tasya sidhyanti vinā yantrair api kriyāḥ ||
BDhS 4.7.2ab  brāhmaṇā ṛjavas tasmād yad yad icchanti cetasā |
BDhS 4.7.2cd  tat tad āsādayanty āśu saṃśuddhā ṛjukarmabhiḥ ||
BDhS 4.7.3ab  evam etāni yantrāṇi tāvat kāryāṇi dhīmatā |
BDhS 4.7.3cd  kālena yāvatopaiti vigrahaḥ śuddhim ātmanaḥ || [K: vigrahaṃ]
BDhS 4.7.4ab  ebhir yantrair viśuddhātmā trirātropoṣitas tataḥ |
BDhS 4.7.4cd  tad ārabheta yena rddhiṃ karmaṇā prāptum icchati ||
BDhS 4.7.5ab  kṣmāpavitraḥ sahasrākṣo mṛgāro 'ṃhomucau gaṇau | [K: kṣāpavitraṃ]
BDhS 4.7.5cd  pāvamānyaś ca kūśmāṇḍyo vaiśvānarya ṛcaś ca yāḥ ||
BDhS 4.7.6ab  ghṛtaudanena tā juhvat saptāhaṃ savanatrayam |
BDhS 4.7.6cd  maunavratī haviṣyāśī nigṛhītendriyakriyaḥ ||
BDhS 4.7.7ab  siṃhe ma ity apāṃ pūrṇe pātre 'vekṣya catuṣpathe |
BDhS 4.7.7cd  mucyate sarvapāpebhyo mahataḥ pātakād api ||
BDhS 4.7.8ab  vṛddhatve yauvane bālye yaḥ kṛtaḥ pāpasaṃcayaḥ |
BDhS 4.7.8cd  pūrvajanmasu cājñānāt tasmād api vimucyate || [K: vājñānāt]
BDhS 4.7.9ab  bhojayitvā dvijān ante pāyasena sasarpiṣā [K: susarpiṣā] |
BDhS 4.7.9cd  gobhūmitilahemāni bhuktavadbhyaḥ pradāya ca ||
BDhS 4.7.10ab  vipro bhavati pūtātmā nirdagdhavṛjinendhanaḥ |
BDhS 4.7.10cd  kāmyānāṃ karmaṇāṃ yogyas tathādhānādikarmaṇām || [K: yojyaḥ]
BDhS 4.8.1ab  atilobhāt pramādād vā yaḥ karoti kriyām imām |
BDhS 4.8.1cd  anyasya so 'ṃha āviṣṭo garagīr iva sīdati ||
BDhS 4.8.2ab  ācāryasya pitur mātur ātmanaś ca kriyām imām |
BDhS 4.8.2cd  kurvan bhāty arkavad vipraḥ sā kāryaiṣām ataḥ kriyā ||
BDhS 4.8.3ab  ka etena sahasrākṣaṃ pavitreṇākaroc chucim |
BDhS 4.8.3cd  agniṃ vāyuṃ raviṃ somaṃ yamādīṃś ca sureśvarān ||
BDhS 4.8.4ab  yat kiṃcit puṇyanāmeha triṣu lokeṣu viśrutam |
BDhS 4.8.4cd  viprādi tat kṛtaṃ kena pavitrakriyayānayā ||
BDhS 4.8.5ab  prājāpatyam idaṃ guhyaṃ pāpaghnaṃprathamodbhavam | [K: prajāpatyam]
BDhS 4.8.5cd  samutpannāny ataḥ paścāt pavitrāṇi sahasraśaḥ ||
BDhS 4.8.6ab  yo 'bdāyanaṛtupakṣāhāñ juhoty aṣṭau gaṇān imān |
BDhS 4.8.6cd  punāti cātmano vaṃśyān daśa pūrvān daśāvarān [K:daśāparān] ||
BDhS 4.8.7ab  jñāyate cāmarair dyusthaiḥ puṇyakarmeti bhūsthitaḥ |
BDhS 4.8.7cd  devavan modate bhūyaḥ svargaloke 'pi puṇyakṛt || [K putsthis verse after BDhS 4.8.12]
BDhS 4.8.8ab  etān aṣṭau gaṇān hotuṃ na śaknoti yadi dvijaḥ |
BDhS 4.8.8cd  eko 'pi tena hotavyo rajas tenāsya naśyati ||
BDhS 4.8.9ab  sūnavo yasya śiṣyā vā juhvaty aṣṭau gaṇān imān |
BDhS 4.8.9cd  adhyāpanaparikrītair aṃhasaḥ so 'pi mucyate ||
BDhS 4.8.10ab  dhanenāpi parikrītair ātmapāpajighāṃsayā |
BDhS 4.8.10cd  hāvanīyā hy aśaktena nāvasādyaḥ śarīradhṛk ||
BDhS 4.8.11ab  dhanasya kriyate tyāgaḥ karmaṇāṃ sukṛtām api |
BDhS 4.8.11cd  puṃso 'nṛṇasya pāpasya vimokṣaḥ kriyate kvacit ||
BDhS 4.8.12ab  vimukto vidhinaitena sarvapāpārṇasāgarāt | [K: mukto yo]
BDhS 4.8.12cd  ātmānaṃ manyate śuddhaṃ samarthaṃ karmasādhane ||
BDhS 4.8.13ab  sarvapāpārṇamuktātmā kriyā ārabhate tu yāḥ |
BDhS 4.8.13cd  ayatnenaiva tāḥ siddhiṃ yānti śuddhaśarīriṇaḥ ||
BDhS 4.8.14ab  prājāpatyam idaṃ puṇyam ṛṣiṇā samudīritam | [K: prajāpatyam ... ṛṣīṇāṃ]
BDhS 4.8.14cd  idam adhyāpayen nityaṃ dhārayec chṛṇute 'pi vā |
BDhS 4.8.14ef  mucyate sarvapāpebhyo brahmaloke mahīyate ||
BDhS 4.8.15ab  yān siṣādhayiṣur mantrān dvādaśāhāni tāñ japet |
BDhS 4.8.15cd  ghṛtena payasā dadhnā prāśya niśy odanaṃ sakṛt || [K before BDhS 4.8.16-1ab: ṛgyajussāmavedānām atharvāṅgirasām api |]
BDhS 4.8.16-1ab  daśavāraṃ tathā homaḥ sarpiṣā savanatrayam |
BDhS 4.8.16-1cd  pūrvasevā bhaved eṣāṃ mantrāṇāṃ karmasādhane || [K: eṣā]
BDhS 4.8.16-2  mantrāṇāṃ karmasādhana iti || atilobhāt pramādād vā | nivṛttaḥ pāpakarmabhyaḥ || samādhuśchandasārudrāḥ || athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi || prāyaścittāni vakṣyāmaḥ || prāyaścittāni vakṣyāmaḥ || prāyaścittāni vakṣyāmaḥ || prāyaścittānivakṣyāmaḥ || iti caturthaḥ praśnaḥ || 4

|| iti baudhāyanadharmasūtraṃsamāptam ||
