Vasiṣṭha Dharmasūtra

Originally typed and analyzed as part of the project of the Joint Seminar on `Law and Society in Classical India' headed by Prof. Y. Ikari at the Institute for Research in Humanities, Kyoto University.
Converted to Unicode plain text without hyphens, sandhi resolution, and analytical markers by Timothy Lubin, October 2021.

Text based on the edition:
Vasiṣṭhadharmaśāstra, Bombay Sanskrit and Prakrit Series, 23, input by M Fushimi, checked by F. Enomoto.


VaDhS 1.1  athātaḥ puruṣaniḥśreyasārthaṃ dharmajijñāsā ||
VaDhS 1.2  jñātvā cānutiṣṭhan dhārmikaḥ ||
VaDhS 1.3  praśasyatamo bhavati loke pretya ca svargalokaṃ samaśnute ||
VaDhS 1.4  śrutismṛtivihito dharmaḥ ||
VaDhS 1.5  tadalābhe śiṣṭācāraḥ pramāṇam ||
VaDhS 1.6  śiṣṭaḥ punar akāmātmā ||
VaDhS 1.7  agṛhyamāṇakāraṇo dharmaḥ ||
VaDhS 1.8  āryāvartaḥ prāg ādarśāt pratyak kālakavanād udak pāriyātrād dakṣiṇena himavataḥ ||
VaDhS 1.9  uttareṇa ca vindhyasya ||
VaDhS 1.10  tasmin deśe ye dharmās ye cācārās te sarvatra pratyetavyāḥ ||
VaDhS 1.11  na tv anye pratilomakadharmāṇām ||
VaDhS 1.12  gaṅgāyamunayor antare 'py eke ||
VaDhS 1.13  yāvad vā kṛṣṇamṛgo vicarati tāvad brahmavarcasam ity anye ||
VaDhS 1.14  athāpi bhāllavino nidāne gāthām udāharanti ||
VaDhS 1.15  paścāt sindhur vidhāraṇī sūryasyodayanaṃ puraḥ |
VaDhS 1.15  yāvat kṛṣṇo 'bhidhāvati tāvad vai brahmavarcasam iti ||
VaDhS 1.16  traividyavṛddhā yaṃ brūyur dharmaṃ dharmavido janāḥ {C}|
VaDhS 1.16  pavane pāvane caiva sa dharmo nātra saṃśaya iti {C} ||
VaDhS 1.17  deśadharmajātidharmakuladharmāñ chrutyabhāvād abravīn manuḥ ||
VaDhS 1.18  sūryābhyuditaḥ sūryābhinimruktas
VaDhS 1.19  pañca mahāpātakāny ācakṣate ||
VaDhS 1.20  gurutalpaṃ surāpānaṃ bhrūṇahatyā brāhmaṇasuvarṇāpaharaṇaṃ patitasaṃyogaś ca ||
VaDhS 1.21  brāhmeṇa vā yaunena vā ||
VaDhS 1.22  athāpy udāharanti |
VaDhS 1.22  saṃvatsareṇa patati patitena sahācaran {C}|
VaDhS 1.22  yājanādhyāpanād yaunān na tu yānāsanād dānāditi {C} ||
VaDhS 1.23  yo 'gnīn apavidhyed guruṃ ca yaḥ pratidaghnuyān nāstiko nāstikavṛttiḥ somaṃ ca vikrīṇīyād ity upapātakāni ||
VaDhS 1.24  tisro brāhmaṇasya bhāryā varṇānupūrveṇa dve rājanyasya ekaikā vaiśyaśūdrayoḥ ||
VaDhS 1.25  śūdrām apy eke mantravarjaṃ tadvat ||
VaDhS 1.26  tathā na kuryāt ||
VaDhS 1.27  ato hi dhruvaḥ kulāpakarṣaḥ pretya cāsvargaḥ ||
VaDhS 1.28  ṣaḍ vivāhāḥ ||
VaDhS 1.29  brāhmo daiva ārṣo gāndharvaḥ kṣātro mānuṣaś ceti ||
VaDhS 1.30  icchata udakapūrvaṃ yāṃ dadyāt sa brāhmaḥ ||
VaDhS 1.31  yajñatantre vitata ṛtvije karma kurvate kanyāṃ dadyād alaṃkṛtya taṃ daivam ity ācakṣate ||
VaDhS 1.32  gomithunena cārṣaḥ ||
VaDhS 1.33  sakāmāṃ kāmayamānaḥ sadṛśīṃ yonim uhyāt sa gāndharvaḥ ||
VaDhS 1.34  yāṃ [balena sahasā pramathya] haranti sa kṣātraḥ ||
VaDhS 1.35  [paṇitvā dhanakrītāṃ] sa mānuṣaḥ ||
VaDhS 1.36  tasmād duhitṛmate 'dhirathaṃ śataṃ deyam itīha krayo vijñāyate ||
VaDhS 1.37  yā patyuḥ krītā saty athānyaiś caratīti ha cāturmāsyeṣu ||
VaDhS 1.38  athāpy udāharanti |
VaDhS 1.38  vidyā pranaṣṭā punar abhyupaiti kulapraṇāśe tv iha sarvanāśaḥ {C}|
VaDhS 1.38  kulāpadeśena hayo 'pi pūjyas tasmāt kulīnāṃ striyam udvahantīti {C} ||
VaDhS 1.39  trayo varṇā brāhmaṇasya nirdeśena varteran ||
VaDhS 1.40  brāhmaṇo dharmān prabrūyāt ||
VaDhS 1.41  rājā cānuśiṣyāt ||
VaDhS 1.42  rājā tu dharmeṇānuśāsan ṣaṣṭhaṃ dhanasya haret ||
VaDhS 1.43  anyatra brāhmaṇāt ||
VaDhS 1.44  iṣṭāpūrtasya tu ṣaṣṭham aṃśaṃ bhajatīti ha ||
VaDhS 1.45  brāhmaṇo vedam āḍhyaṃ karoti brāhmaṇo āpada uddharati tasmād brāhmaṇo 'nādyaḥ | somo 'sya rājā bhavatīti ha ||
VaDhS 1.46  pretya cābhyudayikam iti ha vijñāyate ha vijñāyata iti ||

VaDhS 2.1  catvāro varṇā brāhmaṇakṣatriyavaiśyaśūdrāḥ ||
VaDhS 2.2  trayo varṇā dvijātayo brāhmaṇakṣatriyavaiśyāḥ ||
VaDhS 2.3  teṣām
VaDhS 2.3  mātur agre vijananaṃ dvitīyaṃ mauñjibandhane {C}|
VaDhS 2.3  atrāsya mātā sāvitrī pitā tv ācārya ucyate {C}||
VaDhS 2.4  vedapradānāt pitety ācāryam ācākṣate ||
VaDhS 2.5  tathāpy udāharanti |
VaDhS 2.5  dvayam u ha vai puruṣasya reto brāhmaṇasyordhvaṃ nābher adhastād avācīnam anyat tad yad ūrdhvaṃ nābhes tena haitat prajā jāyate yad brāhmaṇān upanayati yad adhyāpayati yad yājayati yat sādhukaroti | atha yad avācīnaṃ nābhes tenehāsyaurasī prajā jāyate | tasmāc chrotriyam anūcānam aprajo 'sīti na vadantīti ||
VaDhS 2.6  hārīto 'py udāharati |
VaDhS 2.6  na hy asmin vidyate karma kimcid ā mauñjibandhanāt {C}|
VaDhS 2.6  vṛttyā śūdrasamo hy eṣa yāvad vede na jāyateiti {C} ||
VaDhS 2.7  anyatrodakakarmasvadhāpitṛsaṃyuktebhyaḥ ||
VaDhS 2.8  vidyā ha vai brāhmaṇam ājagāma gopāya māṃ śevadhis te 'ham asmi {C}|
VaDhS 2.8  asūyakāyānṛjave 'yatāya na māṃ brūyā vīryavatī tathā syām {C}||
VaDhS 2.9  yam eva vidyāḥ śucim apramattaṃ medhāvinaṃ brahmacaryopapannam {C}|
VaDhS 2.9  yas te na druhyet katamaccanāhas tasmai māṃ brūyā nidhipāya brahman {C}||
VaDhS 2.10  ya ātṛṇatty avitathena karṇav aduḥkhaṃ kurvann amṛtaṃ saṃprayacchan {C}|
VaDhS 2.10  taṃ manyeta pitaraṃ mātaraṃ ca tasmai na druhyet katamaccanāhaḥ {C}||
VaDhS 2.11  adhyāpitā ye guruṃ nādriyante viprā vācā manasā karmaṇā vā {C}|
VaDhS 2.11  yathaiva te na guror bhojanīyās tathaiva tān na bhunakti śrutaṃ tat {C}||
VaDhS 2.12  dahaty agnir yathā kakṣaṃ brahma pṛṣṭam anādṛtam {C}|
VaDhS 2.12  na brahma tasmai prabrūyāc chakyaṃ mānam akurvataiti {C} ||
VaDhS 2.13  ṣaṭ karmāṇi brāhmaṇasya ||
VaDhS 2.14  svādhyāyādhyayanam adhyāpanaṃ yajño yajanaṃ dānaṃ pratigrahaś ceti ||
VaDhS 2.15  trīṇi rājanyasya ||
VaDhS 2.16  adhyayanaṃ yajño dānaṃ ca ||
VaDhS 2.17  śastreṇa ca prajāpālanaṃ svadharmas tena jīvet ||
VaDhS 2.18  etāny eva vaiśyasya ||
VaDhS 2.19  kṛṣir vāṇijyaṃ pāśupālyaṃ kusīdaṃ ca ||
VaDhS 2.20  teṣāṃ paricaryā śūdrasya niyatā ca vṛttiḥ ||
VaDhS 2.21  niyatakeśaveṣāḥ sarve vā muktakeśāḥ śikhāvarjam ||
VaDhS 2.22  ajīvantaḥ svadharmeṇānantarāṃ yavīyasīṃ vṛttim ātiṣṭheran ||
VaDhS 2.23  na tu kadācid jyāyasīm ||
VaDhS 2.24  vaiśyajīvikām āsthāya paṇyena jīvato 'śmalavaṇamaṇiśāṇakauśeyakṣaumājināni ca ||
VaDhS 2.25  tāntavaṃ raktaṃ sarvaṃ ca ||
VaDhS 2.26  kṛtānnaṃ puṣpaphalamūlāni gandharasā udakaṃ cauṣadhīnāṃ rasaḥ somaś ca śastraṃ viṣaṃ māṃsaṃ ca kṣīraṃ ca savikāram ayas trapu jatu sīsaṃ ca ||
VaDhS 2.27  athāpy udāharanti |
VaDhS 2.27  sadyaḥ patati māṃsena lākṣayā lavaṇena ca {C}|
VaDhS 2.27  tryaheṇa śūdro bhavati brāhmaṇaḥ kṣīravikrayāditi {C} ||
VaDhS 2.28  grāmapaśūnām ekaśaphāḥ keśinaś ca sarve cāraṇyāḥ paśavo vayāṃsi daṃṣṭriṇaś ca ||
VaDhS 2.29  dhānyānāṃ tilān āhuḥ ||
VaDhS 2.30  athāpy udāharanti |
VaDhS 2.30  bhojanābhyañjanād dānād yad anyat kurute tilaiḥ {C}|
VaDhS 2.30  kṛmibhūtaḥ śvaviṣṭhāyāṃ pitṛbhiḥ saha majjatīti {C} ||
VaDhS 2.31  kāmaṃ vā svayaṃ kṛṣyotpādya tilān vikrīṇīran ||
VaDhS 2.32  tasmāc chāṇḍābhyām anasyotābhyāṃ prāk prātarāśāt karṣī syāt ||
VaDhS 2.33  nidāghe 'paḥ prayacchet ||
VaDhS 2.34  lāṅgalaṃ pavīravatsuśevaṃ somapitsaru | tad ud vapati gām aviṃ prapharvyaṃ ca pīvarīṃ prasthāvad rathavāhanam iti ||
VaDhS 2.35  [dūre 'pavidhyati somapitsaru somo hy asya prāpnoti tat saru tad udvapati gāṃ cāviṃ cājān aśvān aśvatarakharoṣṭāṃś ca prapharvyaṃ ca pīvarīṃ darśanīyāṃ kalyāṇīṃ ca prathamayuvatīm ||] [Böhtlingk and Führer bracket as commentarial interpolation; Bühler accepts it as an "old nirukta".]
VaDhS 2.36  kathaṃ hi laṅgalam udvaped anyatra dhānyavikrayāt ||
VaDhS 2.37  rasā rasair mahato hīnato vā vibhātavyāḥ ||
VaDhS 2.38  na tv eva lavaṇaṃ rasaiḥ ||
VaDhS 2.39  tilataṇḍulapakvānaṃ vidyā mānuṣyāś ca vihitāḥ parivarttakena ||{BSPS parivarttakena}{F parivartakena}
VaDhS 2.40  brāhmaṇarājanyau vārdhuṣī na dadyātām ||
VaDhS 2.41  athāpy udāharanti |
VaDhS 2.41  samarghaṃ dhānyam uddhṛtya mahārghaṃ yaḥ prayacchati {C}|
VaDhS 2.41  sa vai vārdhuṣiko nāma brahmavādiṣu garhitaḥ {C}||
VaDhS 2.42  brahmahatyāṃ ca vṛddhiṃ ca tulayā samatolayat {C}|
VaDhS 2.42  atiṣṭhad bhrūṇahā koṭyāṃ vārdhuṣiḥ samakampataiti {C} ||
VaDhS 2.43  kāmaṃ vā pariluptakṛtyāya pāpīyase dadyātām ||
VaDhS 2.44  dviguṇaṃ hiraṇyaṃ triguṇaṃ dhānyam ||
VaDhS 2.45  dhānyenaiva rasā vyākhyātāḥ ||
VaDhS 2.46  puṣpamūlaphalāni ca ||
VaDhS 2.47  tulādhṛtam aṣṭaguṇam ||
VaDhS 2.48  athāpy udāharanti |
VaDhS 2.48  dvikaṃ trikaṃ catuṣkaṃ ca pañcakaṃ ca śataṃ smṛtam {C}|
VaDhS 2.48  māsasya vṛddhiṃ gṛhṇīyād varṇānām anupūrvaśaḥ {C}||
VaDhS 2.49  rājā tu mṛtabhāvena dravyavṛddhiṃ vināśayet {C}|

VaDhS 2.50  na hīyate iti ||

VaDhS 3.1  aśrotriyā ananuvākyā anagnayo vā śūdrasadharmāṇo bhavanti ||
VaDhS 3.2  mānavaṃ cātra ślokam udāharanti |
VaDhS 3.2  yo 'nadhītya dvijo vedam anyatra kurute śramam {C}|
VaDhS 3.2  sa jīvann eva śūdratvam āśu gacchati sānvayaḥ {C}||
VaDhS 3.3  nānṛg brāhmaṇo bhavati na vaṇiṅ na kuśīlavaḥ {C}|
VaDhS 3.3  na śūdrapreṣaṇaṃ kurvan na steno na cikitsakaḥ {C}||
VaDhS 3.4  avratā hy anadhīyānā yatra bhaikṣacarā dvijāḥ {C}|
VaDhS 3.4  taṃ grāmaṃ daṇḍayed rājā corabhaktaprado hi saḥ {C}||
VaDhS 3.5  avratānām amantrāṇāṃ jātimātropajīvinām {C}|
VaDhS 3.5  sahasraśaḥ sametānāṃ pariṣattvaṃ na vidyate {C}||
VaDhS 3.6  yad vadanti tamomūḍhā mūrkhā dharmam ajānataḥ {C}|
VaDhS 3.6  tad pāpaṃ śatadhā bhūtvā tadvaktṝn adhigacchati {C}||
VaDhS 3.7  catvāro vā trayo vāpi yaṃ brūyur vedapāragāḥ {C}|
VaDhS 3.7  sa dharma iti vijñeyo netareṣāṃ sahasraśaḥ {C}||
VaDhS 3.8  śrotriyāya ca deyāni havyakavyāni nityaśaḥ {C}|
VaDhS 3.8  aśrotriyāya dattaṃ hi pitṝn naiti na devatāḥ {C}||
VaDhS 3.9  yasya caikagṛhe mūrkho dūre vāpi bahuśrutaḥ {C}|
VaDhS 3.9  bahuśrutāya dātavyaṃ nāsti mūrkhe vyatikramaḥ {C}||
VaDhS 3.10  brāhmaṇātikramo nāsti vipre vedavivarjite {C}|
VaDhS 3.10  jvalantam agnim utsṛjya na hi bhasmani hūyate {C}||
VaDhS 3.11  yaś ca kāṣṭhamayo hastī yaś ca carmamayo mṛgaḥ {C}|
VaDhS 3.11  yaś ca vipro 'nadhīyānas trayas te nāmadhārakāḥ {C}||
VaDhS 3.12  vidvadbhojyāny avidvāṃso yeṣu rāṣṭreṣu bhuñjate {C}|
VaDhS 3.12  tāny anāvṛṣṭim ṛcchanti mahad vā jāyate bhayamiti {C} ||
VaDhS 3.13  aprajñāyamānam
VaDhS 3.14  brāhmaṇaś ced adhigacchet ṣaṭsu karmasu varttamāno na rājā haret ||{BSPS varttamānas}{F vartamānas}
VaDhS 3.15  ātatāyinaṃ hatvā nātra prāṇachettuḥ kimcit kilviṣam āhuḥ ||
VaDhS 3.16  athāpy udāharanti |

VaDhS 3.18  svādhyāyinaṃ kule jātaṃ yo hanyād ātatāyinam {C}|

VaDhS 3.19  triṇāciketaḥ pañcāgnis trisuparṇaś caturmedhā vājasaneyī ṣaḍaṅgavid brahmadeyānusantānaś chandogo jyeṣṭhasāmago mantrabrāhmaṇavidyaś ca dharmān adhīte yasya ca daśapuruṣaṃ mātṛpitṛvaṃśaḥ śrotriyo vijñāyate vidvāṃsaḥ snātakāś caite paṅktipāvanā bhavanti ||
VaDhS 3.20  cāturvidyaṃ vikalpī ca aṅgavid dharmapāṭhakaḥ {C}|

VaDhS 3.21  upanīya kṛtsnaṃ vedam adhyāpayet sa ācāryaḥ {C}||
VaDhS 3.22  yas tv ekadeśaṃ sa upādhyāyaḥ ||
VaDhS 3.23  yaś ca vedāṅgāni ||
VaDhS 3.24  ātmatrāṇe varṇasaṃvarge brāhmaṇavaiśyau śastram ādadīyātām ||
VaDhS 3.25  kṣatriyasya tu tan nityam eva rakṣaṇādhikārāt ||
VaDhS 3.26  prakṣālya pādau pāṇī cā maṇibandhāt prāg vodag vāsīno 'ṅguṣṭhamūlasyottararekhā brāhmaṃ tīrthaṃ tena trir ācāmed aśabdavat ||
VaDhS 3.27  dviḥ parimṛjīta ||
VaDhS 3.28  khāny adbhiḥ saṃspṛśet ||
VaDhS 3.29  mūrdhany apo ninayet savye ca pāṇau ||
VaDhS 3.30  vrajaṃs tiṣṭhañ chayānaḥ praṇato vā nācāmet ||
VaDhS 3.31  hṛdayaṅgamābhir adbhir abudbudābhir aphenābhir brāhmaṇaḥ ||
VaDhS 3.32  kaṇṭhagābhis tu kṣatriyaḥ ||
VaDhS 3.33  vaiśyo 'dbhiḥ prāśitābhiḥ ||
VaDhS 3.34  strīśūdraṃ spṛṣṭābhir eva ca ||
VaDhS 3.35  pradarād api yā gos taparṇasamarthāḥ syuḥ ||{BSPS taparṇa}{F tarpaṇa}
VaDhS 3.36  na varṇagandharasaduṣṭābhir yāś ca syur aśubhāgamāḥ ||
VaDhS 3.37  na mukhyā vipruṣa ucchiṣṭaṃ kurvanty anaṅgaspṛṣṭāḥ ||

VaDhS 3.40  na śmaśrugato lepaḥ ||
VaDhS 3.41  dantavad dantasakteṣu yac cāntarmūkhe bhaven nigirann eva tac chucir iti ||

VaDhS 3.43  carann abhyavahāreṣu ucchiṣṭaṃ yadi saṃspṛśet {C}|
VaDhS 3.43  bhūmau nidhāya tad dravyām ācamya pracaret punaḥ {C}||
VaDhS 3.44  yad yan mīmāṃsyaṃ syād adbhiḥ saṃspṛśet ||
VaDhS 3.45  śvahatāś ca mṛgā vanyāḥ pātitaṃ ca khagaiḥ phalam {C}|

VaDhS 3.46  prasāritaṃ ca yat paṇyaṃ ye doṣāḥ strīmukheṣu ca {C}||
VaDhS 3.47  maśakair makṣikābhiś ca nilīnair nopahanyate {C}|

VaDhS 4.1  prakṛtiviśiṣṭaṃ cāturvarṇyaṃ saṃskāraviśeṣāc ca ||
VaDhS 4.2  brāhmaṇo 'sya mukham āsīd bāhū rājanyaḥ kṛtaḥ {C}|
VaDhS 4.2  urū tad asya yad vaiśyaḥ padbhyāṃ śūdro ajāyata {C}||{BSPS urū}{F ūrū}{śūdro < śūdras}
VaDhS 4.2  ity api nigamo bhavati ||
VaDhS 4.3  gāyatryā brāhmaṇam asṛjata triṣṭubhā rājanyaṃ jagatyā vaiśyaṃ na kenacic chandasā śūdram ity asaṃskāryo vijñāyate ||
VaDhS 4.4  sarveṣāṃ satyam akrodho dānam ahiṃsā prajananaṃ ca ||
VaDhS 4.5  pitṛdevatātithipūjāyām apy eva paśuṃ hiṃsyād iti mānavam ||
VaDhS 4.6  madhuparke ca yajñe ca pitṛdaivatakarmaṇi {C}|
VaDhS 4.6  atraiva ca paśuṃ hiṃsyān nānyathety abravīn manuḥ {C}||
VaDhS 4.7  nākṛtvā prāṇināṃ hiṃsāṃ māṃsam utpadyate kvacit {C}|
VaDhS 4.7  na ca prāṇivadhaḥ svargyas tasmād yāge vadho 'vadhaḥ {C}||
VaDhS 4.8  athāpi brāhmaṇāya vā rājanyāya vābhyāgatāya mahokṣāṇaṃ vā mahājaṃ vā paced evam asmay ātithyaṃ kurvantīti ||
VaDhS 4.9  udakakriyāśaucaṃ ca dvivarṣaṃ prabhṛti ||
VaDhS 4.10  ā dantajananād ity eke ||
VaDhS 4.11  śarīram agninā saṃyojyān avekṣamāṇā apo 'bhyavayanti ||
VaDhS 4.12  savyetarābhyāṃ pāṇibhyam udakakriyāṃ kurvīrann ayugmāsu dakṣiṇāmukhāḥ ||{BSPS pāṇibhyam}{F pāṇibhyām}
VaDhS 4.13  pitṝṇāṃ vay eṣā dig yā dakṣiṇā ||
VaDhS 4.14  gṛhān vrajitvā prastare tryaham anaśnanta āsīran ||
VaDhS 4.15  aśaktau krītotpannena varteran ||
VaDhS 4.16  daśāhaṃ śāvam āśaucaṃ sapiṇḍeṣu vidhīyate ||
VaDhS 4.17  sapiṇḍatvaṃ sāptapuruṣaṃ vijñāyate ||
VaDhS 4.18  prattānāṃ ca strīṇāṃ tripuruṣaṃ vijñāyate ||
VaDhS 4.19  prattānām itare kurvīraṃs tāś ca teṣām ||
VaDhS 4.20  janane 'py evam eva syān nipuṇāṃ śuddhim icchatām ||
VaDhS 4.21  mātāpitror vā ||
VaDhS 4.22  tadnimittatvān mātur ity eke ||
VaDhS 4.23  athāpy udāharanti |
VaDhS 4.23  nāśaucaṃ sūtake puṃsaḥ saṃsargaṃ cen na gacchati {C}|
VaDhS 4.23  rajas tatrāśuci jñeyaṃ tac ca puṃṣi na vidyateiti {C} ||
VaDhS 4.24  tac ced antaḥ punar āpatec cheṣeṇa śudhyeran ||
VaDhS 4.25  rātriśeṣe dvābhyām ||
VaDhS 4.26  prabhāte tisṛbhiḥ ||
VaDhS 4.27  brāhmaṇo daśarātreṇa ||
VaDhS 4.28  pañcadaśarātreṇa rājanyaḥ ||
VaDhS 4.29  viṃśatirātreṇa vaiśyaḥ ||
VaDhS 4.30  śūdro māsena śudhyati ||
VaDhS 4.31  athāpy udāharanti |
VaDhS 4.31  aśauce yas tu śūdrasya sūtake vāpi bhuktavān {C}|
VaDhS 4.31  sa gacchen narakaṃ ghoraṃ tiryagyonyāṃ ca jāyate {C}||
VaDhS 4.32  anirdaśāhe paraśave niyogād bhuktavān dvijaḥ {C}|
VaDhS 4.32  kṛmir bhūtvā sa dehānte tāṃ viṣṭhāṃ samupāśnuteiti {C} ||
VaDhS 4.33  dvādaśa māsān dvādaśārdhamāsān vānaśnan saṃhitādhyayanam adhīyānaḥ pūto bhavatīti vijñāyate ||
VaDhS 4.34  ūnadvivarṣe prete garbhapatane vā sapiṇḍānāṃ trirātram āśaucam ||
VaDhS 4.35  sadyaḥ śaucam iti gautamaḥ ||
VaDhS 4.36  deśāntarasthe preta ūrdhvaṃ daśāhāc chrutvaikarātram āśaucam ||
VaDhS 4.37  āhitāgniś cet pravasan mriyeta punaḥ saṃskāraṃ kṛtvā śavavacchaucam iti gautamaḥ ||
VaDhS 4.38  yūpacitiśmaśānarajasvalāsūtikāśucīṃś ca spṛṣṭvā saśirasko 'bhyupeyād apa ity apa iti ||

VaDhS 5.1  asvatantrā strī puruṣapradhānā ||
VaDhS 5.2  anagnikānudakyā vāmṛtam iti vijñāyate ||
VaDhS 5.3  athāpy udāharanti |
VaDhS 5.3  pitā rakṣati kaumāre bhartā rakṣati yauvane {C}|
VaDhS 5.3  putraś ca sthavire bhāve na strī svātantryam arhatīti {C} ||
VaDhS 5.4  tasyā bhartur abhicāra uktaṃ prāyaścittaṃ rahasyeṣu ||
VaDhS 5.5  māsi māsi rajo hy āsāṃ duṣkṛtāny apakarṣati ||
VaDhS 5.6  trirātraṃ rajasvalāśucir bhavati ||
VaDhS 5.7  sā nāñjyān nābhyañjyān nāpsu snāyād adhaḥ śayīta na divā svapyān nāgniṃ spṛśen na rañjuṃ sṛjen na dantān dhāvayen na māṃsam aśnīyān na grahān nirīkṣeta na hasen na kiṃcetad ācaren na dhāvetad akharveṇa pātreṇa pibed añjalinā vā pibel lohitāyasena vā ||{BSPS kiṃcetad}{F kiṃcid}{BSPS dhāvetad}{F dhāved}
VaDhS 5.8  vijñāyate hi | indras triśīrṣāṇaṃ tvāṣṭraṃ hatvā pāpmagṛhīto mahattamādharmasaṃbaddho 'ham ity evam ātmānam amanyata | taṃ sarvāṇi bhūtāny abhyakrośan bhrūṇahan bhrūṇahann iti | sa striya upādhāvad asyai me bhrūṇahatyāyai tṛtīyaṃ bhāgaṃ pratigṛhṇīteti | tā abravan kiṃ no bhūyād iti | so 'bravīd varaṃ vṛṇīdhvam iti | tā abruvann ṛtau prajāṃ vindāmaha iti kāmam ā vijanitoḥ saṃbhavāmeti | tatheti tāḥ pratijagṛhuḥ saiṣā bhrūṇahatyā māsi-māsy āvir bhavati tasmād rajasvalāyā annaṃ nāśnīyād bhrūṇahatyāyā evaiṣā rūpaṃ pratimucyāste ||
VaDhS 5.9  tad āhuḥ | añjanābhyañjanam evāsyā na pratigrāhyaṃ tad dhi striyā annam iti | tasmāt tasyai ca tatra ca bībhatsante meyam upāgād iti ||
VaDhS 5.10  udakyās tv āsate yeṣāṃ ye ca kecid anagnayaḥ {C}|
VaDhS 5.10  kulaṃ cāśrotriyām yeṣāṃ sarve te śūdradharmiṇa iti {C} ||
VaDhS 5.10  sarve te śūdradharmiṇa iti ||

VaDhS 6.1  ācāraḥ paramo dharmaḥ sarveṣām iti niścayaḥ {C}|
VaDhS 6.1  hīnācāraparītātmā pretya ceha ca naśyati {C}||
VaDhS 6.2  nainaṃ tapāṃsi na brahma nāgnihotraṃ na dakṣiṇāḥ {C}|
VaDhS 6.2  hīnācāram ito bhraṣṭaṃ tārayanti kathamcana {C}||
VaDhS 6.3  ācārahīnaṃ na punanti vedā yady apy adhītāḥ saha ṣaḍbhir aṅgaiḥ {C}|
VaDhS 6.3  chandāṃsy enaṃ mṛtyukāle tyajanti nīḍaṃ śakuntā iva jātapakṣāḥ {C}||
VaDhS 6.4  ācārahīnasya tu brāhmaṇasya vedāḥ ṣaḍaṅgās tv akhilāḥ sayajñāḥ {C}|
VaDhS 6.4  kāṃ prītim utpādayituṃ samarthā andhasya dārā iva darśanīyāḥ {C}||
VaDhS 6.5  nainaṃ chandāṃsi vṛjināt tārayanti māyāvinaṃ māyayā vartamānam {C}|
VaDhS 6.5  dve 'py akṣare samyag adhīyamāne punāti tad brahma yathā iṣe 'bdāḥ {C}||
VaDhS 6.6  durācāro hi puruṣo loke bhavati ninditaḥ {C}|
VaDhS 6.6  duḥkhabhāgī ca satataṃ vyādhito 'lpāyur eva ca {C}||
VaDhS 6.7  ācārāt phalate dharma ācārāt phalate dhanam {C}|
VaDhS 6.7  ācārāc chriyam āpnoti ācāro hanty alakṣaṇam {C}||
VaDhS 6.8  sarvalakṣaṇahīno 'pi yaḥ sadācāravān naraḥ {C}|
VaDhS 6.8  śraddadhāno 'nasūyaś ca śataṃ varṣāṇi jīvati {C}||
VaDhS 6.9  āhāranirhāravihārayogāḥ susaṃvṛtā dharmavidā tu kāryāḥ {C}|
VaDhS 6.9  vāgbuddhikāryāṇi tapas tathaiva dhanāyuṣī guptatame tu kārye {C}||
VaDhS 6.10  ubhe mūtrapurīṣe divā kuryād udaṅmukhaḥ {C}|
VaDhS 6.10  rātrau tu dakṣiṇā kuryād evaṃ hy āyur na riṣyati {C}||
VaDhS 6.11  praty agniṃ prati sūryaṃ ca prati gāṃ prati brāhmaṇam {C}|
VaDhS 6.11  prati somodakaṃ sandhyāṃ prajñā naśyati mehataḥ {C}||
VaDhS 6.12  na nadyāṃ mehanaṃ kuryān na pathi na ca bhasmani {C}|
VaDhS 6.12  na gomaye na kṛṣṭe nopte na śādvalopajīvyachāyāsu ||
VaDhS 6.13  chāyāyām andhakāre vā rātrav ahani vā dvijaḥ {C}|
VaDhS 6.13  yathāsukhamukhaḥ kuryāt prāṇabādhābhayeṣu ca {C}||
VaDhS 6.14  uddhṛtābhir adbhiḥ kāryaṃ kuryāt ||
VaDhS 6.15  snānam anuddhṛtābhir api ||
VaDhS 6.16  āharen mṛttikāṃ vipraḥ kūlāt sasikatā tu yā {C}||
VaDhS 6.17  antarjale devagṛhe valmīke mūṣakasthale {C}|
VaDhS 6.17  kṛtaśaucāvaśiṣṭā vā na grāhyāḥ pañca mṛttikāḥ {C}||
VaDhS 6.18  ekā liṅge kare tisra ubhayor mṛddvayaṃ smṛtam {C}|
VaDhS 6.18  pañcāpāne daśaikasminn ubhayoḥ sapta mṛttikāḥ {C}||
VaDhS 6.19  etac chaucaṃ gṛhasthānāṃ dviguṇaṃ brahmacāriṇām {C}|
VaDhS 6.19  triguṇaṃ vānaprasthānāṃ yatīnāṃ tac caturguṇamiti {C} ||
VaDhS 6.20  aṣṭau grāsā muner bhaktaṃ vānaprasthasya ṣoḍaśa {C}|
VaDhS 6.20  dvātriṃśat tu gṛhasthasyāparimitaṃ brahmacāriṇaḥ ||
VaDhS 6.21  āhitāgnir anaḍvāṃś ca brahmacārī ca te trayaḥ {C}|
VaDhS 6.21  aśnanta eva sidhyanti naiṣāṃ siddhir anaśnatām {C}||
VaDhS 6.22  vrateṣu niyameṣu cejyādhyayanadharmeṣu {C}||
VaDhS 6.23  yogas tapo damo dānaṃ satyaṃ śaucaṃ śrutaṃ ghṛṇā {C}|
VaDhS 6.23  vidyā vijñānam āstikyam etad brāhmaṇalakṣaṇam {C}||
VaDhS 6.24  dīrghavairam asūyā cāsatyaṃ brāhmaṇadūṣaṇam {C}|
VaDhS 6.24  paiśunyaṃ nirdayatvaṃ ca jānīyāc chūdralakṣaṇam {C}||
VaDhS 6.25  ye śāntadāntāḥ śrutipūrṇakarṇā jitendriyāḥ prāṇivadhān nivṛttāḥ {C}|
VaDhS 6.25  pratigrahe saṅkucitāgrahastās te brāhmaṇās tārayituṃ samarthāḥ {C}||
VaDhS 6.26  kimcid vedamayaṃ pātraṃ kimcit pātraṃ tapomayam {C}|
VaDhS 6.26  pātrāṇām api tat pātraṃ śūdrānnaṃ yasya nodare {C}||
VaDhS 6.27  śūdrānnenodarasthena yadi kaścin mṛto dvijaḥ {C}|
VaDhS 6.27  sa bhavec chūkaro grāmyas tasya vā jāyate kule {C}||
VaDhS 6.28  śūdrānnarasapuṣṭāṅgo hy adhīyāno 'pi nityaśaḥ {C}|
VaDhS 6.28  juhvan vāpi japan vāpi gatim ūrdhvāṃ na vindati {C}||
VaDhS 6.29  śūdrānnena tu bhuktena maithunaṃ yo 'dhigacchati {C}|
VaDhS 6.29  yasyānnaṃ tasya te putrā na ca svargāruho bhavet {C}||
VaDhS 6.30  svādhyāyotthaṃ yonimantaṃ praśāntaṃ vaitānasthaṃ pāpabhīruṃ bahujñam {C}|
VaDhS 6.30  strīṣu kṣāntaṃ dhārmikaṃ gośaraṇyaṃ vrataiḥ klāntaṃ tādṛśaṃ pātram āhuḥ {C}||
VaDhS 6.31  āmapātre yathā nyastaṃ kṣīraṃ dadhi ghṛtaṃ madhu {C}|
VaDhS 6.31  vinaśyet pātradaurbalyān na ca pātraṃ rasāś ca te {C}||
VaDhS 6.32  evaṃ gā vā hiraṇyaṃ vā vastram aśvaṃ mahīṃ tilān {C}|
VaDhS 6.32  avidvān pratigṛhṇāno bhasmībhavati kāṣṭhavat {C}||
VaDhS 6.33  nāṅganakhavādanaṃ kuryāt ||
VaDhS 6.34  nakhaiś ca bhojanādau ||
VaDhS 6.35  na cāpo 'ñjalinā pibet ||
VaDhS 6.36  na pādena pāṇinā vā jalam abhihanyāt ||
VaDhS 6.37  na jalena jalam ||
VaDhS 6.38  neṣṭakābhiḥ phalaṃ śātayīta ||
VaDhS 6.39  na phalena phalam ||
VaDhS 6.40  na kalko na kuhako bhavet ||
VaDhS 6.41  na mlecchabhāṣāṃ śikṣeta ||
VaDhS 6.42  athāpy udāharanti |
VaDhS 6.42  na pāṇipādacapalo na netracapalo bhavet {C}|
VaDhS 6.42  na ca vāgaṅgacapala iti śiṣṭasya gocaraḥ {C}||
VaDhS 6.43  pāraṃparyagato yeṣāṃ vedaḥ saparibṛṃhaṇaḥ {C}|
VaDhS 6.43  te śiṣṭā brāhmaṇā jñeyāḥ śrutipratyakṣahetavaḥ {C}||
VaDhS 6.44  yaṃ na santaṃ na vāsantaṃ nāśrutaṃ na bahuśrutam {C}|
VaDhS 6.44  na suvṛttaṃ na durvṛttaṃ veda kaścit sa brāhmaṇaḥ {C}||
VaDhS 6.44  sa brāhmaṇa iti ||

VaDhS 7.1  catvāra āśramāḥ ||
VaDhS 7.2  brahmacārigṛhasthavānaprasthaparivrājakāḥ ||
VaDhS 7.3  teṣāṃ vedam adhītya vedau vedān vāviśīrṇabrahmacaryo yam icchet tam āvaset ||
VaDhS 7.4  brahamacāry ācāryaṃ paricared ā śarīravimokṣaṇāt ||
VaDhS 7.5  ācārye ca prete 'gniṃ paricaret ||
VaDhS 7.6  vijñāyate hy agnir ācāryas taveti ||
VaDhS 7.7  saṃyatavāc ||
VaDhS 7.8  caturthaṣaṣṭhāṣṭamakālabhojī ||
VaDhS 7.9  bhaikṣam ācaret ||
VaDhS 7.10  gurvadhīnaḥ ||
VaDhS 7.11  jaṭilaḥ śikhājaṭo vā ||

VaDhS 7.13  āhūtādhyāyī ||

VaDhS 7.17  trir ahno 'bhyupeyād apa ity apa iti ||

VaDhS 8.1  gṛhastho vinītakrodhaharṣo guruṇānujñātaḥ snātvāsamānārṣeyām aspṛṣṭamaithunām avaravayasīṃ sadṛśīṃ bhāryāṃ vindeta ||
VaDhS 8.2  pañcamīṃ mātṛbandhubhyaḥ saptamīṃ pitṛbandhubhyaḥ ||
VaDhS 8.3  vaivāhyam agnim indhīta ||
VaDhS 8.4  sāyam āgatam atithiṃ nāparundhyāt ||
VaDhS 8.5  nāsyānaśnan gṛhe vaset ||
VaDhS 8.6  yasya nāśnāti vāsārthī brāhmaṇo gṛham āgataḥ {C}|
VaDhS 8.6  sukṛtaṃ tasya yat kimcit sarvam ādāya gacchati {C}||
VaDhS 8.7  ekarātraṃ tu nivasann atithir brāhmaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ {C}|
VaDhS 8.7  anityaṃ hi sthito yasmāt tasmād atithir ucyate {C}||
VaDhS 8.8  naikagrāmīṇa atithir vipraḥ sāṅgatikas tathā {C}|{grāmīṇa < grāmīṇaḥ}
VaDhS 8.8  kāle prāpte akāle vā nāsyānaśnan gṛhe vaset {C}||
VaDhS 8.9  śraddhāśīlo 'spṛhayālur alam agnyādheyāya nānāhitāgniḥ syāt ||
VaDhS 8.10  alaṃ ca somāya nāsomayājī ||
VaDhS 8.11  yuktaḥ svādhyāye yajñe prajanane ca ||
VaDhS 8.12  gṛheṣv abhyāgataṃ pratyutthānāsanavāksūnṛtānasūyābhir mahayet ||
VaDhS 8.13  yathāśakti cānnena sarvāṇi bhūtāni ||
VaDhS 8.14  gṛhastha eva yajate gṛhasthas tapyate tapaḥ {C}|
VaDhS 8.14  caturṇām āśramāṇāṃ tu gṛhasthaś ca viśiṣyate {C}||
VaDhS 8.15  yathā nadīnadāḥ sarve samudre yānti saṃsthitim {C}|
VaDhS 8.15  evam āśramiṇaḥ sarve gṛhasthe yānti saṃsthitim {C}||
VaDhS 8.16  yathā mātaram āśritya sarve jīvanti jantavaḥ {C}|
VaDhS 8.16  evaṃ gṛhastham āśritya sarve jīvanti bhikṣukāḥ {C}||
VaDhS 8.17  nityodakī nityayajñopavītī nityasvādhyāyī patitānnavarjī {C}|
VaDhS 8.17  ṛtau ca gacchan vidhivac ca juhvan na brāhmaṇaś cyavate brahmalokāt {C}||
VaDhS 8.17  brahmalokād iti ||

VaDhS 9.1  vānaprastho jaṭilaś cīrājinavāsī ||
VaDhS 9.2  grāmaṃ ca na praviśet ||
VaDhS 9.3  na phālakṛṣṭam adhitiṣṭhet ||
VaDhS 9.4  akṛṣṭaṃ mūlaphalaṃ sañcinvīta ||
VaDhS 9.5  urdhvaretāḥ ||{BSPS urdhva}{F ūrdhva}
VaDhS 9.6  kṣamāśayaḥ ||
VaDhS 9.7  mūlaphalabhaikṣeṇāśramāgatam atithim abhyarcayet ||
VaDhS 9.8  dadyād eva na pratigṛhṇīyāt ||
VaDhS 9.9  tripavaṇam udakopasparśī ||
VaDhS 9.10  śrāmaṇakenāgnim ādhāyāhitāgniḥ syāt ||
VaDhS 9.11  vṛkṣamūlaniketana ūrdhvaṃ ṣaḍbhyo māsebhyo 'nagnir aniketaḥ ||
VaDhS 9.12  dadyād devapitṛmanuṣyebhyaḥ sa gacchet svargam ānantyam ity ānantyam ||

VaDhS 10.1  parivrājakaḥ sarvabhūtābhayadakṣiṇāṃ dattvā pratiṣṭheta ||
VaDhS 10.2  athāpy udāharanti |
VaDhS 10.2  abhayaṃ sarvabhūtebhyo dattvā carati yo muniḥ {C}|
VaDhS 10.2  tasyāpi sarvabhūtebhyo na bhayaṃ jātu vidyate {C}||
VaDhS 10.3  abhayaṃ sarvabhūtebhyo dattvā yas tu nivarttate {C}|{BSPS nivarttate}{F nivartate}
VaDhS 10.3  hanti jātān ajātāṃś ca dravyāṇi pratigṛhya ca {C}||
VaDhS 10.4  sannyaset sarvakarmāṇi vedam ekaṃ na sannyaset {C}|
VaDhS 10.4  vedasannyasanāc chūdras tasmād vedaṃ na sannyaset {C}||
VaDhS 10.5  ekākṣaraṃ paraṃ brahma prāṇāyāmāḥ paraṃ tapaḥ {C}|
VaDhS 10.5  upavāsāt paraṃ bhaikṣaṃ dayā dānād viśiṣyateiti {C} ||
VaDhS 10.6  muṇḍo 'mamo 'parigrahaḥ ||
VaDhS 10.7  saptāgārāṇy asaṅkalpitāni cared bhikṣām ||
VaDhS 10.8  vidhūme sann amusale ||
VaDhS 10.9  ekaśāṭīparihitaḥ ||
VaDhS 10.10  ajinena vā gopralūnais tṛṇair avastṛtaśarīraḥ ||
VaDhS 10.11  sthaṇḍilaśāyī ||
VaDhS 10.12  anityāṃ vasatiṃ vaset ||
VaDhS 10.13  grāmānte devagṛhe śūnyāgāre vā vṛkṣamūle vā ||
VaDhS 10.14  manasā jñānam adhīyānaḥ ||
VaDhS 10.15  araṇyanityaḥ ||
VaDhS 10.16  na grāmyapaśūnāṃ sandarśane vicaret ||
VaDhS 10.17  araṇyanityasya jitendriyasya sarvendriyaprītinivartakasya {C}|
VaDhS 10.17  adhyātmacintāgatamānasasya dhruvā hy anāvṛttir upekṣakasyeti {C} ||
VaDhS 10.18  avyaktaliṅgo 'vyaktācāraḥ ||
VaDhS 10.19  anunmatta unmattaveṣaḥ ||
VaDhS 10.20  athāpy udāharanti |
VaDhS 10.20  na śabdaśāstrabhiratasya mokṣo na cāpi lokagrahaṇe ratasya {C}|{BSPS śāstrabhiratasya}{F śāstrābhiratasya}
VaDhS 10.20  na bhojanācchādanatatparasya na cāpi ramyāvasathapriyasya {C}||
VaDhS 10.21  na cotpātanimittābhyām na nakṣatrāṅgavidyayā {C}|
VaDhS 10.21  nānuśāsanavādābhyāṃ bhikṣāṃ lipseta karhicit {C}||
VaDhS 10.22  alābhe na viṣādī syāl lābhe naiva ca harṣayet {C}|
VaDhS 10.22  prāṇayātrikamātraḥ syān mātrāsaṅgād vivarjitaḥ ||
VaDhS 10.23  na kuṭyāṃ nodake saṅgo na caile na tripuṣkare {C}|
VaDhS 10.23  nāgāre nāsane nānne yasya vai mokṣavid tu sa iti {C} ||
VaDhS 10.24  brāhmaṇakule yāval labheta tad bhuñjīta sāyaṃ prātar madhumāṃsavarjam ||
VaDhS 10.25  na ca tṛpyet ||
VaDhS 10.26  grāme vā vaset ||
VaDhS 10.27  ajihmo 'śaḍho 'śaraṇo 'vasaṅkusukaḥ ||
VaDhS 10.28  na cendriyasaṃsargaṃ kurvīta kenacit ||
VaDhS 10.29  upekṣakaḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ hiṃsānugrahaparihāreṇa ||

VaDhS 11.1  ṣaḍ arghārhā bhavanti ||
VaDhS 11.2  ṛtvig vivāhyo rājāpitṛvyasnātakamātulāś ca ||
VaDhS 11.3  vaiśvadevasya siddhasya sāyaṃ prātar gṛhyāgnau juhuyāt ||
VaDhS 11.4  gṛhadevatābhyo baliṃ haret ||
VaDhS 11.5  śrotriyāyāgrabhāgaṃ dattvā brahmacāriṇe vānantaraṃ pitṛbhyo dadyāt ||
VaDhS 11.6  tato 'tithiṃ bhojayec chreyāṃsaṃ śreyāṃsam ānupūrvyeṇa ||
VaDhS 11.7  svagṛhyāṇāṃ kumārībālavṛddhataruṇaprajātāḥ ||

VaDhS 11.11  śeṣaṃ dampatī bhuñjīyātām ||
VaDhS 11.12  sarvopayogena punarpāko yadi nirupte vaiśvadeve 'tithir āgacched viśeṣeṇāsmay annaṃ kārayet ||

VaDhS 11.14  taṃ bhojayitvopāsīta ||
VaDhS 11.15  ā sīmāntam anuvrajed anujñānād vā ||

VaDhS 11.18  śiṣyān api guṇavato bhojayet ||
VaDhS 11.19  nagnaśuklaklībāndhaśyāvadantakuṣṭhikunakhivarjam ||
VaDhS 11.20  athāpy udāharanti |

VaDhS 11.21  śrāddhe nodvāsanīyāni ucchiṣṭāny ā dinakṣayāt {C}|
VaDhS 11.21  ścyotante hi sudhādhārās tāḥ pibanty akṛtodakāḥ {C}||
VaDhS 11.22  ucchiṣṭaṃ na pramṛjyāt tu yāvan nāstamito raviḥ {C}|
VaDhS 11.22  kṣīradhārās tato yānty akṣayyāḥ sañcarabhāginaḥ {C}||
VaDhS 11.23  prāk saṃskārāt pramītānāṃ svavaṃśyānām iti sthitiḥ {C}|
VaDhS 11.23  bhāgadheyaṃ manuḥ prāha ucchiṣṭoccheṣaṇe ubhe {C}||
VaDhS 11.24  uccheṣaṇaṃ bhūmigataṃ vikiraṃ lepanodakam {C}|
VaDhS 11.24  annaṃ preteṣu visṛjed aprajānām anāyuṣām {C}||
VaDhS 11.25  ubhayor hastayor muktaṃ pitṛbhyo 'nnaṃ niveditam {C}|
VaDhS 11.25  tadantaraṃ pratīkṣante hy asurā duṣṭacetasaḥ {C}||
VaDhS 11.26  tasmād aśūnyahastena kuryād annam upāgatam {C}|

VaDhS 11.27  dvau daive pitṛkṛtye trīn ekaikam ubhayatra vā {C}||
VaDhS 11.27  bhojayet susamṛddho 'pi na prasajjeta vistare {C}||
VaDhS 11.28  satkriyāṃ deśakālau ca śaucaṃ brāhmaṇasaṃpadam {C}|

VaDhS 11.29  api vā bhojayed ekaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ vedapāragam {C}|
VaDhS 11.29  śrutaśīlopasaṃpannaṃ sarvālakṣaṇavarjitam {C}||
VaDhS 11.30  yady ekaṃ bhojayec chrāddhe daivaṃ tatra kathaṃ bhavet {C}|
VaDhS 11.30  annaṃ pātre samuddhṛtya sarvasya prakṛtasya tu {C}||
VaDhS 11.31  devatāyatane kṛtvā tataḥ śrāddhaṃ pravartayet {C}|
VaDhS 11.31  prāsyed agnau tad annaṃ vā dadyād vā brahmacāriṇe {C}||
VaDhS 11.32  yāvad uṣṇaṃ bhavaty annaṃ yāvad aśnanti vāgyatāḥ {C}|
VaDhS 11.32  tāvad dhi pitaro 'śnanti yāvan noktā havirguṇāḥ {C}||
VaDhS 11.33  havirguṇā na vaktavyāḥ pitaro yāvad atarpitāḥ {C}|
VaDhS 11.33  pitṛbhis tarpitaiḥ paścād vaktavyaṃ śobhanaṃ haviḥ {C}||
VaDhS 11.34  niyuktas tu yatiḥ śrāddhe daive vā māṃsam utsṛjet {C}|
VaDhS 11.34  yāvanti paśuromāṇi tāvan narakam ṛcchati {C}||
VaDhS 11.35  trīṇi śrāddhe pavitrāṇi dauhitraḥ kutapas tilāḥ {C}|
VaDhS 11.35  trīṇi cātra praśaṃsānti śaucam akrodham atvarām {C}||
VaDhS 11.36  divasasyāṣṭame bhāge mandībhavati bhāskaraḥ {C}|
VaDhS 11.36  sa kālaḥ kutapo jñeyaḥ pitṝṇāṃ dattam akṣayam {C}||
VaDhS 11.37  śrāddhaṃ dattvā bhuktvā ca maithunaṃ yo 'dhigacchati {C}|
VaDhS 11.37  bhavanti pitaras tasya tadmāsaṃ retaso bhujaḥ {C}||
VaDhS 11.38  yas tato jāyate garbho dattvā bhuktvā ca paitṛkam {C}|
VaDhS 11.38  na sa vidyāṃ samāpnoti kṣīṇāyuś caiva jāyate {C}||
VaDhS 11.39  pitā pitāmahaś caiva tathaiva prapitāmahaḥ {C}|
VaDhS 11.39  upāsate sutaṃ jātaṃ śakuntā iva pippalam {C}||
VaDhS 11.40  madhumāṃsaiś ca śākaiś ca payasā pāyasena ca {C}|
VaDhS 11.40  eṣa no dāsyati śrāddhaṃ varṣāsu ca maghāsu ca {C}||
VaDhS 11.41  santānavarddhanaṃ putram udyataṃ pitṛkarmaṇi {C}|{BSPS varddhanam}{E vardhanam}

VaDhS 11.42  nandanti pitaras tasya sukṛṣṭair iva karṣakāḥ {C}|
VaDhS 11.42  yad gayāstho dadāty annaṃ pitaras tena putriṇa iti {C} ||
VaDhS 11.43  śrāvaṇyāgrahāyiṇyoś cānvaṣṭakyāṃ ca pitṛbhyo dadyāt ||{BSPS āgrahāyiṇyos}{F āgrahāyaṇyos}
VaDhS 11.44  dravyadeśabrāhmaṇasannidhāne vākālaniyamaḥ ||
VaDhS 11.45  avaśyaṃ brāhmaṇo 'gnīn ādadhīta ||
VaDhS 11.46  darśapūrṇamāsāgrayaṇeṣṭicāturmāsyapaśusomaiś ca yajeta ||
VaDhS 11.47  naiyamikaṃ hy etad ṛṇasaṃstutaṃ ca ||
VaDhS 11.48  vijñāyate hi | tribhir ṛṇair ṛṇavān brāhmaṇo jāyata iti | yajñena devebhyaḥ prajayā pitṛbhyo brahmacaryeṇa ṛṣibhya ity eṣa vānṛṇo yajvā yaḥ putrī brahmacaryavān iti ||
VaDhS 11.49  garbhāṣṭameṣu brāhmaṇam upanayīta ||{BSPS garbhāṣṭameṣu}{F garbhād aṣṭameṣu}
VaDhS 11.50  garbhād ekādaśeṣu rājanyam ||

VaDhS 12.1  athātaḥ snātakavratāni ||

VaDhS 13.1  athātaḥ svādhyāyopākarma śrāvaṇyām paurṇamāsyāṃ prauṣṭhapadyāṃ vā ||
