VaDhS 13.55  śastraṃ viṣaṃ surā cāpratigṛhyāṇi brāhmaṇasya ||
VaDhS 13.56  vidyāvittavayaḥsaṃbandhāḥ karma ca mānyam ||
VaDhS 13.57  pūrvaḥ pūrvo garīyān ||
VaDhS 13.58  sthavirabālāturabhārikastrīcakrīvagatāṃ panthāḥ samāgame parasmaiparasmai deyaḥ ||{BSPS cakrīvagatām}{F cakrīvatām}
VaDhS 13.59  rājasnātakayoḥ samāgame rājñā snātakāya deyaḥ ||
VaDhS 13.60  sarvair eva vadhvay ūhyamānāyai ||
VaDhS 13.61  tṛṇabhūmyagnyudakavāksūnṛtānasūyāḥ satāṃ gṛhe nocchidyante kadācana kadācaneti ||

VaDhS 14.1  athāto bhojyābhojyaṃ ca varṇayiṣyāmaḥ ||
VaDhS 14.2  cikitsakamṛgayupuṃścalīdaṇḍikastenābhiśastaṣaṇḍhapatitānām annam abhojyam ||
VaDhS 14.3  kadaryadīkṣitabaddhāturasomavikrayitakṣarajakaśauṇḍikasūcakavārdhuṣikacarmāvakṛntānām ||
VaDhS 14.4  śūdrasya ca ||
VaDhS 14.5  astrabhṛtaś ca ||
VaDhS 14.6  upapater yaś copapatiṃ manyate ||
VaDhS 14.7  yaś ca gṛhān dahet ||
VaDhS 14.8  yaś ca vadhārhān nopahanyāt ||
VaDhS 14.9  ko bhokṣyata iti vācābhighuṣṭam ||
VaDhS 14.10  gaṇānnaṃ gaṇikānnaṃ ceti ||
VaDhS 14.11  athāpy udāharanti |
VaDhS 14.11  nāśnanti śvavato devā nāśnanti vṛṣalīpateḥ {C}|
VaDhS 14.11  bhāryājitasya nāśnanti yasya copapatir gṛheiti {C} ||
VaDhS 14.12  edhodakayavasakuśalājābhyudyatayānāvasathaśapharīpriyaṅgusraggandhamadhumāṃsānīty eteṣāṃ pratigṛhṇīyāt ||
VaDhS 14.13  athāpy udāharanti |
VaDhS 14.13  gurvarthaṃ dāram ujjihīrṣann arciṣyan devatātithīn {C}|
VaDhS 14.13  sarvataḥ pratigṛhṇīyān na tu tṛpyet svayaṃ tata iti {C} ||
VaDhS 14.14  na mṛgayor iṣucāriṇaḥ parivarjyam annam ||
VaDhS 14.15  vijñāyate hi | agastyo varṣasāhasrike satre mṛgayāṃ cacāra | tasyāsaṃstarasamayāḥ puroḍāśā mṛgapakṣiṇāṃ praśastānām ||{BSPS satre}{F sattre}
VaDhS 14.16  api hy atra prājāpatyāñ chlokān udāharanti |
VaDhS 14.16  udyatām āhṛtāṃ bhikṣāṃ purastād apracoditām {C}|
VaDhS 14.16  bhojyāṃ prajāpatir mene api duṣkṛtakāriṇaḥ {C}||
VaDhS 14.17  śraddadhānasya bhoktavyaṃ corasyāpi viśeṣataḥ {C}|
VaDhS 14.17  na tv eva bahuyājyasya yaś copanayate bahūn {C}||
VaDhS 14.18  na tasya pitaro 'śnanti daśa varṣāṇi pañca ca {C}|
VaDhS 14.18  na ca havyaṃ vahaty agnir yas tām abhyavamanyate {C}||
VaDhS 14.19  cikitsakasya mṛgayoḥ śalyahartus tu pāpinaḥ {C}|
VaDhS 14.19  ṣaṇḍhasya kulaṭāyāś ca udyatāpi na gṛhyateiti {C} ||
VaDhS 14.20  ucchiṣṭam aguror abhojyam ||
VaDhS 14.21  svam ucchiṣṭam ucchiṣṭopahataṃ ca ||
VaDhS 14.22  yad vasanakeśakīṭopahataṃ ca ||
VaDhS 14.23  kāmaṃ tu keśakīṭān uddhṛtyādbhiḥ prokṣya bhasmanāvakīrya vācā praśastam upayuñjīta ||
VaDhS 14.24  api hy atra prājāpatyāñ chlokān udāharanti |
VaDhS 14.24  trīṇi devāḥ pavitrāṇi brāhmaṇānām akalpayan {C}|
VaDhS 14.24  adṛṣṭam adbhir nirṇiktaṃ yac ca vācā praśasyate {C}||
VaDhS 14.25  devadroṇyāṃ vivāheṣu yajñeṣu prakṛteṣu ca {C}|
VaDhS 14.25  kākaiḥ śvabhiś ca saṃspṛṣṭam annam tan na visarjayet {C}|
VaDhS 14.26  tasmād annam apoddhṛtya śeṣaṃ saṃskāram arhati {C}|
VaDhS 14.26  dravāṇāṃ plāvanenaiva ghanānāṃ prokṣaṇena tu {C}||
VaDhS 14.27  mārjāramukhasaṃspṛṣṭaṃ śuci eva hi tad bhavet {C}||
VaDhS 14.28  annaṃ paryuṣitaṃ bhāvaduṣṭaṃ sahṛdlekhaṃ punaḥsiddham āmamāṃsaṃ pakvaṃ ca ||
VaDhS 14.29  kāmaṃ tu dadhnā ghṛtena vābhighāritam upayuñjīta ||
VaDhS 14.30  api hy atra prājāpatyāñ chlokān udāharanti |
VaDhS 14.30  ghṛtaṃ vā yadi vā tailaṃ vipro nādyān nakhaścyutam {C}|
VaDhS 14.30  yamas tad aśuci prāha tulyaṃ gomāṃsabhakṣaṇaiḥ {C}||
VaDhS 14.31  hastadattās tu ye snehā lavaṇavyañjanāni ca {C}|
VaDhS 14.31  dātāraṃ nopatiṣṭhanti bhoktā bhuñjīta kilbiṣam {C}||
VaDhS 14.32  pradadyān na tu hastena nāyasena kadācaneti {C} ||
VaDhS 14.33  laśunapalāṇḍukyākugṛñjanaśleṣmāntakavṛkṣaniryāsalohitavraścanaśvakākāvalīḍhaśūdroccheṣaṇabhojaneṣv atikṛcchraḥ ||
VaDhS 14.34  sandhinīkṣīram avatsākṣīram ||
VaDhS 14.35  gomahiṣyajānām anirdaśāhānām ||
VaDhS 14.36  antarnāvy udakam ||
VaDhS 14.37  apūpadhānākarambhasaktuvaṭakatailapāyasaśākāni śuktāni varjayet ||
VaDhS 14.38  anyāṃś ca kṣīrayavapiṣṭavikārān ||
VaDhS 14.39  śvāvidhśalyakaśaśakacchapagodhāḥ pañcanakhānāṃ bhakṣyāḥ ||
VaDhS 14.40  anuṣṭrāḥ paśūnām anyatodantāś ca ||
VaDhS 14.41  matsyānāṃ vā ceṭagavayaśiśumāranakrakulīrāḥ ||
VaDhS 14.42  vikṛtarūpāḥ sarpaśīrṣāś ca ||
VaDhS 14.43  gauragavayaśarabhāś ca ||
VaDhS 14.44  anuddiṣṭās tathā ||
VaDhS 14.45  dhenvanaḍuhav apannadantāṃś ca ||
VaDhS 14.46  bhakṣyau tu dhenvanaḍuhau medhyau vājasaneyake vijñāyate ||
VaDhS 14.47  khaḍge tu vivadanty agrāmyaśūkare ca ||
VaDhS 14.48  śakunānāṃ ca viṣuviṣkarajālapādakalaviṅkaplavahaṃsacakravākabhāsavāyasapārāvatakurarasāraṅgapāṇḍukapotakrauñcakrakarakaṅkagṛdhraśyenabakabalākamadguṭiṭṭibhamāndhālanaktañcaradārvāghāṭacakaṭarailātakāhārītakhañjarīṭagrāmyakukkuṭaśukaśārikakokilakravyādo grāmacāriṇaś ca grāmacāriṇaś ceti ||

VaDhS 15.1  śoṇitaśukrasaṃbhavaḥ puruṣo bhavati mātāpitṛnimittakaḥ ||
VaDhS 15.2  tasya pradānavikrayatyāgeṣu mātāpitarau prabhavataḥ ||
VaDhS 15.3  na tv ekaṃ putraṃ dadyāt pratigṛhṇīyād vā ||
VaDhS 15.4  sa hi santānāya pūrveṣām ||
VaDhS 15.5  na strī putraṃ dadyāt pratigṛhṇīyād vānyatrānujñānād bhartuḥ ||
VaDhS 15.6  putraṃ pratigṛhīṣyan bandhūn āhūya rājani ca nivedya niveśanasya madhye vyāhṛtibhir hutvādūrabāndhavaṃ bandhusannikṛṣṭam eva pratigṛhṇīyāt ||
VaDhS 15.7  sandehe cotpanne dūrebāndhavaṃ śūdram iva sthāpayet ||
VaDhS 15.8  vijñāyate hy ekena bahūṃs trāyata iti ||
VaDhS 15.9  tasmiṃś cet pratigṛhīte 'urasaḥ putra utpadyeta caturthabhāgabhāgī syād dattakaḥ ||
VaDhS 15.10  yadi nābhyudayikeṣu yuktaḥ syāt ||
VaDhS 15.11  vedaviplāvakaḥ śūdrayājaka uttamavarṇavargapatitās teṣāṃ pātraninayanam ||
VaDhS 15.12  apātrasaṅkarād akṛtsnaṃ pātram ādāya dāso 'savarṇāputro vā bandhur asadṛśo vā guṇahīnaḥ savyena pādena pravṛttāgrān darbhāṃl lohitān vopastīrya pūrṇapātram asmai ninayet ||
VaDhS 15.13  ninetāraṃ cāsya prakīrṇakeśā jñātayo 'nvālabheran ||
VaDhS 15.14  apasavyaṃ kṛtvā gṛheṣu svairam āpadyeran ||
VaDhS 15.15  ata ūrdhvaṃ na taṃ dharmayeyuḥ ||
VaDhS 15.16  taddharmāṇas taṃ dharmayantaḥ ||
VaDhS 15.17  patitānāṃ tu caritavratānāṃ pratyuddhāraḥ ||
VaDhS 15.18  athāpy udāharanti |
VaDhS 15.18  agre 'bhyuddharatāṃ gacchet krīḍann iva hasann iva {C}|
VaDhS 15.18  paścāt pātayatāṃ gacchec chocann iva rudann iveti {C} ||
VaDhS 15.19  ācāryamātṛpitṛhantāras tatprasādād apayāpyād vā eṣā teṣāṃ pratyāpattiḥ ||
VaDhS 15.20  puṇyahradāt prasṛtād vā kāñcanaṃ pātraṃ māheyaṃ vā pūrayitvāpohiṣṭhābhir enam adbhir abhiṣiñcanti || {cf. 9.1  obhtūrjhātana | mahṇāya ce ||}
VaDhS 15.21  sarva evābhiṣiktasya pratyuddhāraḥ putrajanmanā vyākhyāto vyākhyāta iti ||

VaDhS 16.1  atha vyavahārāḥ ||
VaDhS 16.2  rājā mantrī vā sadaskāryāṇi kuryāt ||
VaDhS 16.3  dvayor vivadamānayoḥ pakṣāntaraṃ na gacchet ||
VaDhS 16.4  yathāsanam aparādhohy antenāparādhaḥ ||
VaDhS 16.5  samaḥ sarveṣu bhūteṣu yathāsanam aparādhohy ādyavarṇayor vidyāntataḥ ||
VaDhS 16.6  saṃpannaṃ ca rakṣayet ||
VaDhS 16.7  rājabāladhanāni ||
VaDhS 16.8  aprāptavyavahārāṇām ||
VaDhS 16.9  prāptakāle tu tad yat ||
VaDhS 16.10  likhitaṃ sākṣiṇo bhuktiḥ pramāṇaṃ trividhaṃ smṛtam {C}|
VaDhS 16.10  dhanasvīkaraṇaṃ pūrvaṃ dhanī dhanam avāpnuyāditi {C} ||
VaDhS 16.11  mārgakṣetrebhyo visargas tathā parivarttanam ||{BSPS parivarttanam}{F parivartanam}
VaDhS 16.12  taruṇagṛheṣv arthāntareṣu tripādamātram ||
VaDhS 16.13  gṛhakṣetravirodhe sāmantapratyayaḥ ||
VaDhS 16.14  sāmantavirodhe lekhyapratyayaḥ ||
VaDhS 16.15  pratyabhilekhyavirodhe grāmanagaravṛddhaśreṇīpratyayaḥ ||
VaDhS 16.16  athāpy udāharanti |
VaDhS 16.16  paitṛkaṃ krītam ādheyam anvādheyaṃ pratigraham {C}|
VaDhS 16.16  yajñād upagamo veṇis tathā dhūmaśikhāṣṭamīti {C} ||
VaDhS 16.17  tatra bhuktānubhuktadaśavarṣam ||
VaDhS 16.18  anyathāpy udāharanti |
VaDhS 16.18  ādhiḥ sīmā bāladhano nikṣepopanidhistriyaḥ {C}|
VaDhS 16.18  rājasvaṃ śrotriyadravyaṃ na saṃbhogena hīyate {C}||
VaDhS 16.19  prahīṇadravyāṇi rājagāmīni bhavanti ||
VaDhS 16.20  tato 'nyathā rājā mantribhiḥ saha nāgaraiś ca kāryāṇi kuryāt ||
VaDhS 16.21  vedhaso vā rājā śreyān gṛdhraparivāraṃ syāt ||
VaDhS 16.22  gṛdhraparivāraṃ vā rājā śreyān ||
VaDhS 16.23  gṛdhraparivāraṃ syān na gṛdhro gṛdhraparivāraṃ syāt ||
VaDhS 16.24  parivārād dhi doṣāḥ prādurbhavanti ||
VaDhS 16.25  steyahāravināśanaṃ ca ||
VaDhS 16.26  tasmāt pūrvam eva parivāraṃ pṛcchet ||
VaDhS 16.27  atha sākṣiṇaḥ ||
VaDhS 16.28  śrotriyo rūpavāñ chīlavān puṇyavān satyavān sākṣiṇaḥ ||
VaDhS 16.29  sarveṣu sarva eva vā ||
VaDhS 16.30  strīṇāṃ sākṣiṇaḥ striyaḥ kuryād dvijānāṃ sadṛśā dvijāḥ śūdrāṇāṃ santaḥ śūdrāś cāntyānām antyayonayaḥ ||
VaDhS 16.31  athāpy udāharanti |
VaDhS 16.31  pratibhāvyaṃ vṛthādānam ākṣikaṃ saurikaṃ ca yat {C}|
VaDhS 16.31  daṇḍaśulkāvaśiṣṭaṃ ca na putro dātur mahati {C}||
VaDhS 16.32  brūhi sākṣin yathātattvaṃ lambante pitaras tava {C}|
VaDhS 16.32  tava vākyam udīkṣāṇā utpatanti patanti ca {C}||
VaDhS 16.33  nagno muṇḍaḥ kapālī ca bhikṣārthī kṣutpipāsitaḥ {C}|
VaDhS 16.33  andhaḥ śatrukule gacched yaḥ sākṣyam anṛtaṃ vadet {C}||
VaDhS 16.34  pañca kanyānṛte hanti daśa hanti gavānṛte {C}|
VaDhS 16.34  śatam aśvānṛte hanti sahasraṃ puruṣānṛte {C}||
VaDhS 16.35  vyavahāre mṛte dāre prāyaścittaṃ kulastriyāḥ {C}|
VaDhS 16.35  teṣāṃ pūrvaparicchedāc chidyante 'trāpavādibhiḥ {C}||
VaDhS 16.36  udvāhakāle ratisaṃprayoge prāṇātyaye sarvadhanāpahāre {C}|
VaDhS 16.36  viprasya cārthe hy anṛtaṃ vadeyuḥ pañcānṛtāny āhur apātakāni {C}||
VaDhS 16.37  svajanasvārthe yadi vārthahetoḥ pakṣāśrayeṇaiva vadanti kāryam {C}|
VaDhS 16.37  te śabdavaṃśasya kulasya pūrvān svargasthitāṃs tān api pātayanti {C}||
VaDhS 16.37  api pātayantīti ||

VaDhS 17.1  ṛṇam asmin sannayaty amṛtatvaṃ ca gacchati {C}|
VaDhS 17.1  pitā putrasya jātasya paśyec ced jīvato mukham {C}||
VaDhS 17.2  anantāḥ putriṇāṃ lokā nāputrasya loko 'sti ||
VaDhS 17.3  apajāḥ santv atriṇa ity abhiśāpaḥ || {cf. ṚV<1.021.05c>  aprajāḥ santv atriṇaḥ || }
VaDhS 17.4  prajābhir agne amṛtatvam aśyām ity api nigamo bhavati || {cf. ṚV<5.004.10c>  jātavedo yaśo asmāsu dhehi prajābhir agne amṛtatvam aśyām ||}
VaDhS 17.5  putreṇa lokāñ jayati pautreṇānantyam aśnute {C}|
VaDhS 17.5  atha putrasya pautreṇa bradhnasyāpnoti viṣṭapamiti {C} ||
VaDhS 17.6  kṣetriṇaḥ putro janayituḥ putra iti vivadante ||
VaDhS 17.7  tatrobhayathāpy udāharanti |
VaDhS 17.8  yady anyagoṣu vṛṣabho vatsānāṃ janayec chatam {C}|
VaDhS 17.8  gominām eva te vatsā moghaṃ syanditam ārṣabhamiti {C} ||
VaDhS 17.9  apramattā rakṣata tantum etaṃ mā vaḥ kṣetre parabījāni vāpsuḥ | janayituḥ putro bhavati saṃparāye moghaṃ tettā kurute tantum etam iti ||{BSPS tettā}{E vettā}
VaDhS 17.10  bahūnām ekajātānām ekaś cet putravān naraḥ {C}|
VaDhS 17.10  sarve te tena putreṇa putravanta iti śrutiḥ {C}||
VaDhS 17.11  bahvīnām ekapatnīnām ekā putravatī yadi {C}|
VaDhS 17.11  sarvās tās tena putreṇa putravantya iti śrutiḥ {C}||
VaDhS 17.12  dvādaśa ity eva putrāḥ purāṇadṛṣṭāḥ ||
VaDhS 17.13  svayam utpāditaḥ svakṣetre saṃskṛtāyāṃ prathamaḥ ||
VaDhS 17.14  tadalābhe niyuktāyāṃ kṣetrajo dvitīyaḥ ||
VaDhS 17.15  tṛtīyaḥ putrikā ||
VaDhS 17.16  vijñāyate 'bhrātṛkā puṃsaḥ pitṝn abhyeti pratīcīnaṃ gacchati putratvam ||
VaDhS 17.17  tatra ślokaḥ |
VaDhS 17.17  abhrātṛkāṃ pradāsyāmi tubhyaṃ kanyām alaṃkṛtām {C}|
VaDhS 17.17  asyāṃ yo jāyate putraḥ sa me putro bhaved iti {C}||
VaDhS 17.18  paunarbhavaś caturthaḥ ||
VaDhS 17.19  yā kaumāraṃ bharttāram uttsṛjyānyaiḥ saha caritvā tasyaiva kuṭumbam āśrayati sā punarbhūr bhavati ||{BSPS bharttāram}{F bhartāram}{BSPS uttsṛjya}{F utsṛjya}
VaDhS 17.20  yā ca klībaṃ patitam unmattaṃ vā bharttāram utsṛjyānyaṃ patiṃ vindate mṛte vā sā punarbhūr bhavati ||{BSPS bharttāram}{F bhartāram}
VaDhS 17.21  kānīnaḥ pañcamaḥ ||
VaDhS 17.22  yaṃ pitṛgṛhe 'saṃskṛtā kāmād utpādayen mātāmahasya putro bhavatīty āhuḥ ||
VaDhS 17.23  athāpy udāharanti |
VaDhS 17.23  aprattā duhitā yasya putraṃ vindeta tulyataḥ {C}|
VaDhS 17.23  putrī mātāmahas tena dadyāt piṇḍaṃ hared dhanamiti {C} ||
VaDhS 17.24  gṛhe ca gūḍhotpannaḥ ṣaṣṭhaḥ ||
VaDhS 17.25  ity ete dāyādā bāndhavās trātāro mahato bhayād ity āhuḥ ||
VaDhS 17.26  athādāyādabandhūnāṃ sahoḍha eva prathamaḥ ||
VaDhS 17.27  yā garbhiṇī saṃskriyate sahoḍhaḥ putro bhavati ||
VaDhS 17.28  dattako dvitīyaḥ ||
VaDhS 17.29  yaṃ mātāpitarau dadyātām ||
VaDhS 17.30  krītas tṛtīyaḥ ||
VaDhS 17.31  tac chunaḥśepena vyākhyātam ||
VaDhS 17.32  hariścandro vai rājā so 'jīgartasya sauyavaseḥ putraṃ cikrāya ||
VaDhS 17.33  svayam upāgataś caturthaḥ ||
VaDhS 17.34  tac chunaḥśepena vyākyātam ||
VaDhS 17.35  śunaḥśepo vai yūpe niyukto devatās tuṣṭāva | tasyeha devatāḥ pāśaṃ vimumucus tam ṛtvija ūcuḥ | mamaivāyaṃ putro 'stv iti tān ha na saṃpede | te saṃpādayāmāsuḥ | eṣa eva yaṃ kāmayet tasya putro 'stv iti | tasya ha viśvāmitro hotāsīt tasya putratvam iyāya ||
VaDhS 17.36  apaviddhaḥ pañcamaḥ ||
VaDhS 17.37  yaṃ mātāpitṛbhyām apāstaṃ pratigṛhṇīyāt ||
VaDhS 17.38  śūdrāputra eva ṣaṣṭho bhavatīty āhur ity ete 'dāyādabāndhavāḥ ||
VaDhS 17.39  athāpy udāharanti |
VaDhS 17.39  yasya pūrveṣāṃ ṣaṇṇāṃ na kaścid dāyādaḥ syād ete tasya dāyaṃ harerann iti ||
VaDhS 17.40  atha bhrātṝṇāṃ dāyavibhāgaḥ ||
VaDhS 17.41  yaś cānapatyās tāsām ā putralābhāt ||
VaDhS 17.42  dvyaṃśaṃ jyeṣṭho haret ||
VaDhS 17.43  gavāśvasya cānudaśamam ||
VaDhS 17.44  ajāvayo gṛhaṃ ca kaniṣṭhasya ||
VaDhS 17.45  kārṣṇāyasaṃ guhopakaraṇāni ca madhyamasya ||
VaDhS 17.46  mātuḥ pāriṇeyaṃ striyo vibhajeran ||
VaDhS 17.47  yadi brāhmaṇasya brāhmaṇīkṣatriyāvaiśyāsu putrāḥ syuḥ ||
VaDhS 17.48  tryaṃśaṃ brāhmaṇyāḥ putro haret ||
VaDhS 17.49  dvyaṃśaṃ rājanyāyāḥ putraḥ ||
VaDhS 17.50  samam itare vibhajeran ||
VaDhS 17.51  yena caiṣāṃ svayam utpāditaṃ syād dvyaṃśam eva haret ||
VaDhS 17.52  anaṃśās tv āśramāntaragatāḥ ||
VaDhS 17.53  klībonmattapatitāś ca ||
VaDhS 17.54  bharaṇaṃ klībonmattānām ||
VaDhS 17.55  pretapatnī ṣaṭmāsān vratacāriṇy akṣāralavaṇaṃ bhuñjānādhaḥ śayīta ||
VaDhS 17.56  ūrdhvaṃ ṣaḍbhyo māsebhyaḥ snāttvā śrāddhaṃ ca patye dattvā vidyākarmaguruyonisaṃbandhān saṃnipātya pitā bhrātā vā niyogaṃ kārayet ||{BSPS snāttvā}{F snātvā}
VaDhS 17.57  na sonmādām avaśāṃ vyādhitāṃ vā niyuñjyāt ||
VaDhS 17.58  jyāyasīm api ||
VaDhS 17.59  ṣoḍaśa varṣāṇi ||
VaDhS 17.60  na ced āmayāvī syāt ||
VaDhS 17.61  prajāpatye muhūrte pāṇigrāhavad upacared anyatra samprahāsya vākpāruṣyadaṇḍapāruṣyāc ca ||
VaDhS 17.62  grāsācchādanasnānānulepaneṣu prāggāminī syāt ||
VaDhS 17.63  aniyuktāyām utpanna utpādayituḥ putro bhavatīty āhuḥ ||
VaDhS 17.64  syāc cen niyoginoḥ ||
VaDhS 17.65  rikthalobhān nāsti niyogaḥ ||
VaDhS 17.66  prāyaścittaṃ vāpy upadiśya niyuñjyād ity eke ||
VaDhS 17.67  kumāry ṛtumatī trīṇi varṣāṇy upāsīta ||
VaDhS 17.68  tribhyo varṣebhyaḥ patiṃ vindet tulyam ||
VaDhS 17.69  athāpy udāharanti |
VaDhS 17.69  pituḥ pramādāt tu yadīha kanyā vayaḥpramāṇaṃ samatītya dīyate {C}|
VaDhS 17.69  sā hanti dātāram udīkṣamāṇā kālātiriktā gurudakṣiṇeva {C}||
VaDhS 17.70  prayacchen nagnikāṃ kanyām ṛtukālabhayāt pitā {C}|
VaDhS 17.70  ṛtumatyāṃ hi tiṣṭhantyāṃ doṣaḥ pitaram ṛcchati {C}||
VaDhS 17.71  yāvantaḥ kanyām ṛtavaḥ spṛśanti tulyaiḥ sakāmām abhiyācyamānām {C}|
VaDhS 17.71  bhrūṇāni tāvanti hatāni tābhyāṃ mātāpitṛbhyām iti dharmavādaḥ {C}||
VaDhS 17.72  adbhir vācā ca dattāyāṃ mriyetādau varo yadi {C}|
VaDhS 17.72  na ca mantropanītā syāt kumārī pitur eva sā {C}||
VaDhS 17.73  balāc cet prahṛtā kanyā mantrair yadi na saṃskṛtā {C}|
VaDhS 17.73  anyasmai vidhivad deyā yathā kanyā tathaiva sā {C}||
VaDhS 17.74  pāṇigrāhe mṛte bālā kevalaṃ mantrasaṃskṛtā {C}|
VaDhS 17.74  sā ced akṣatayoniḥ syāt punaḥ saṃskāram arhatīti {C} ||
VaDhS 17.75  proṣitapatnī pañca varṣāṇy upāsīta ||
VaDhS 17.76  ūrdhvaṃ pañcabhyo varṣebhyo bhartṛsakāśaṃ gacchet ||
VaDhS 17.77  yadi dharmārthābhyāṃ pravāsaṃ pratyanukāmā na syād yathā preta evaṃ vartitavyaṃ syāt ||
VaDhS 17.78  evaṃ brāhmaṇī pañca prajātāprajātā catvāri rājanyā prajātā pañcāprajātā trīṇi vaiśyā prajātā catvāry aprajātā dve śūdrā prajātā trīṇy aprajātaikam ||
VaDhS 17.79  ata ūrdhvaṃ samānārthajanmapiṇḍodakagotrāṇāṃ pūrvaḥ pūrvo garīyān ||
VaDhS 17.80  na tu khalu kulīne vidyamāne paragāminī syāt ||
VaDhS 17.81  yasya pūrveṣāṃ ṣaṇṇāṃ na kaścid dāyādaḥ syāt sapiṇḍāḥ putrasthānīyās vā tasya dhanaṃ vibhajeran ||
VaDhS 17.82  teṣām alābha ācāryāntevāsinau hareyātām ||
VaDhS 17.83  tayor alābhe rājā haret ||
VaDhS 17.84  na tu brāhmaṇasya rājā haret ||
VaDhS 17.85  brahmasvaṃ tu viṣaṃ ghoram ||
VaDhS 17.86  na viṣaṃ viṣam ity āhur brahmasvaṃ viṣam ucyate {C}|
VaDhS 17.86  viṣam ekākinaṃ hanti brahmasvaṃ putrapautrakamiti {C} ||
VaDhS 17.87  traividyasādhubhyaḥ saṃprayacchet saṃprayacched iti ||

VaDhS 18.1  śūdreṇa brāhmaṇyām utpannaś cāṇḍālo bhavatīty āhuḥ ||
VaDhS 18.2  rājanyāyāṃ vaiṇaḥ ||
VaDhS 18.3  vaiśyāyām antyāvasāyī ||
VaDhS 18.4  vaiśyena brāhmaṇyām utpanno rāmako bhavatīty āhuḥ ||
VaDhS 18.5  rājanyāyāṃ pulkasaḥ ||
VaDhS 18.6  rājanyena brāhmaṇyām utpannaḥ sūto bhavatīty āhuḥ ||
VaDhS 18.7a athāpy udāharanti |
VaDhS 18.7b channotpannāś ca ye kecit prātilomyaguṇāśritāḥ {C}|
VaDhS 18.7c guṇācāraparibhraṃśāt karmabhis tān vijānīyuriti {C} ||
VaDhS 18.8  ekāntarādvyantarātryantarāsu jātā brāhmaṇakṣatriyavaiśyair ambaṣṭhograniṣādā bhavanti ||
VaDhS 18.9  śūdrāṇāṃ pāraśavaḥ ||
VaDhS 18.10  pāraśavo neva jīvann eva śavo bhavatīty āhuḥ ||
VaDhS 18.11  eke vay etac chmaśānaṃ ye śūdrāḥ ||
VaDhS 18.12  tasmāc chūdrasamīpe nādhyetavyam ||
VaDhS 18.13a athāpi yamagītāñ chlokān udāharanti |
VaDhS 18.13b śmaśānam etat pratyakṣaṃ ye śūdrāḥ pāpacāriṇaḥ {C}|
VaDhS 18.13c tasmāc chūdrasamīpe tu nādhyetavyaṃ kadācana {C}||
VaDhS 18.14a na śūdrāya matiṃ dadyān nocchiṣṭaṃ na haviṣkṛtam {C}|
VaDhS 18.14b na cāsyopadiśed dharmaṃ na cāsya vratam ādiśet {C}||
VaDhS 18.15  yaś cāsyopadiśed dharmaṃ yaś cāsya vratam ādiśet {C}|
VaDhS 18.15  so 'saṃvṛttaṃ tamo ghoraṃ saha tena prapadyateiti {C} ||{BSPS asaṃvṛttam}{F asaṃvṛtam}
VaDhS 18.16  vraṇadvāre kṛmir yasya saṃbhaveta kadācana {C}|
VaDhS 18.16  prājāpatyena śudhyeta hiraṇyaṃ gaur vāso dakṣiṇā iti ||
VaDhS 18.17  nāgniṃ citvā rāmām upeyāt ||
VaDhS 18.18  kṛṣṇavarṇā yā rāmā ramaṇāyaiva na dharmāya na dharmāyeti ||

VaDhS 19.1  svadharmo rājñaḥ pālanaṃ bhūtānāṃ tasyānuṣṭhānāt siddhiḥ ||
VaDhS 19.2  bhayakāruṇyahānaṃ jarāmaryaṃ vay etat satttram āhur vidvāṃsaḥ ||{BSPS satttram}{F sattram}
VaDhS 19.3  tasmād gārhasthyanaiyamikeṣu purohitaṃ dadhyāt ||
VaDhS 19.4  vijñāyate | brahmapurohitaṃ rāṣṭram ṛdhnotīti ||
VaDhS 19.5  ubhayasya pālanāt ||
VaDhS 19.6  asāmarthyāc ca ||
VaDhS 19.7  deśadharmajātikuladharmān sarvān evaitān anupraviśya rājā caturo varṇān svadharme sthāpayet ||
VaDhS 19.8  teṣv apacaratsu daṇḍaṃ dhārayet ||
VaDhS 19.9  daṇḍas tu deśakāladharmavayovidyāsthānaviśeṣair hiṃsākrośayoḥ kalpyaḥ ||
VaDhS 19.10  āgamād dṛṣṭāntāc ca ||
VaDhS 19.11  puṣpaphalopagān pādapān na hiṃsyāt ||
VaDhS 19.12  karṣaṇakāraṇārthaṃ copahanyāt ||
VaDhS 19.13  gārhasthyāṅgānāṃ ca mānonmāne rakṣite syātām ||
VaDhS 19.14  adhiṣṭhānān na nīhāraḥ svārthānām ||
VaDhS 19.15  mānamūlyamātraṃ naihārikaṃ syāt ||
VaDhS 19.16  mahāmahayoḥ sthānāt pathaḥ syāt ||
VaDhS 19.17  saṃyāne daśavāhavāhinī dviguṇakāriṇī syāt ||
VaDhS 19.18  pratyekaṃ prapāḥ syuḥ ||
VaDhS 19.19  puṃṣāṃ śatāvarārdhyaṃ cāhavayet ||
VaDhS 19.20  avyarthāḥ striyaḥ syuḥ ||
VaDhS 19.21  karāṣṭhīlā māṣaḥ śaramadhyāpaḥ pādaḥ kārṣāpaṇāḥ syuḥ ||
VaDhS 19.22  nirudakas taro moṣyaḥ ||
VaDhS 19.23  akaraḥ śrotriyo rājapumān anāthapravrajitabālavṛddhataruṇaprajātāḥ ||
VaDhS 19.24  prāggāmikāḥ kumāryo bhṛtapatnyaś ca ||
VaDhS 19.25  bāhubhyām uttarañ chataguṇaṃ dadyāt ||
VaDhS 19.26  nadīkakṣavanadāhaśailopabhogā niṣkarāḥ syuḥ ||
VaDhS 19.27  tadupajīvino vā dadyuḥ ||
VaDhS 19.28  pratimāsam udvāhakaraṃ tv āgamayet ||
VaDhS 19.29  rājani ca prete dadyāt prāsaṅgikam ||
VaDhS 19.30  etena mātṛvṛttir vyākhyātā ||
VaDhS 19.31  rājamahiṣyāḥ pitṛvyamātulān rājā bibhṛyāt ||
VaDhS 19.32  tadbandhūṃś cānyāṃś ca ||
VaDhS 19.33  rājapatnyo grāsācchādanaṃ labheran ||
VaDhS 19.34  anicchantyo vā pravrajeran ||
VaDhS 19.35  klībonmattān rājā bibhṛyāt ||
VaDhS 19.36  tadgāmitvād rikthasya ||
VaDhS 19.37  śulke cāpi mānavaṃ ślokam udāharanti |
VaDhS 19.37  na bhinnakārṣāpaṇam asti śulke na śilpavṛtto na śiśau na dūte {C}|
VaDhS 19.37  na bhaikṣalabdhe na hṛtāvaśeṣe na śrotriye pravrajite na yajñeiti {C} ||
VaDhS 19.38  steno 'nupraveśān na duṣyate ||
VaDhS 19.39  śastradhārī sahoḍho vraṇasaṃpanno vyapadiṣṭas tv ekeṣām ||
VaDhS 19.40  daṇḍyotsarge rājaikarātram upavaset ||
VaDhS 19.41  trirātraṃ purohitaḥ ||
VaDhS 19.42  kṛcchram adaṇḍyadaṇḍane purohitaḥ ||
VaDhS 19.43  trirātraṃ rājā ||
VaDhS 19.44  athāpy udāharanti |
VaDhS 19.44  annāde bhrūṇahā mārṣṭiḥ patyau bhāryāpacāriṇī {C}|
VaDhS 19.44  gurau śiṣyaś ca yājyaś ca steno rājani kilbiṣam {C}||
VaDhS 19.45  rājabhir dhṛtadaṇḍās tu kṛtvā pāpāni mānavāḥ {C}|
VaDhS 19.45  nirmalāḥ svargam āyānti santaḥ sukṛtino yathā {C}||
VaDhS 19.46  eno rājānam ṛcchati utsṛjantaṃ sakilbiṣam {C}|
VaDhS 19.46  taṃ ced vā ghātayed rājā hanti dharmeṇa duṣkṛtamiti {C} ||
VaDhS 19.47  rājñām ātyayike kārye sadyaḥ śaucaṃ vidhīyate {C}|
VaDhS 19.47  tathā nātyayike nityaṃ kāla evātra kāraṇamiti {C} ||
VaDhS 19.48  yamagītaṃ cātra ślokam udāharanti |
VaDhS 19.48  nādya doṣo 'sti rājñāṃ vai vratināṃ na ca satriṇām {C}|
VaDhS 19.48  aindrasthānam upāsīnā brahmabhūtā hi te sadeti {C} ||
VaDhS 19.48  hi te sadeti ||

VaDhS 20.1  anabhisaṃdhikṛte prāyaścittam aparādhe ||
VaDhS 20.2  abhisaṃdhikṛte 'py eke ||
VaDhS 20.3  gurur ātmavatāṃ śāstā śāstā rājā durātmanām {C}|
VaDhS 20.3  atha pracchann apāpānāṃ śāstā vaivasvato yama iti {C} ||
VaDhS 20.4  tatra ca sūryābhyuditaḥ sann ahas tiṣṭhet sāvitrīṃ ca japet ||
VaDhS 20.5  evaṃ sūryābhinirmukto rātrav āsīt ||
VaDhS 20.6  kunakhī śyāvadantas tu kṛcchraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ caret ||
VaDhS 20.7  parivittiḥ kṛcchraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ caritvā niviśeta tāṃ caivopayacchet ||
VaDhS 20.8  atha parivividānaḥ kṛcchrātikṛcchrau caritvā tasmai dattvā punar niviśeta tāṃ caivopayacchet ||
VaDhS 20.9  agredidhiṣūpatiḥ kṛcchraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ caritvā niviśeta tāṃ copayacchet ||
VaDhS 20.10  didhiṣūpatiḥ kṛcchrātikṛcchrau caritvā tasmai dattvā punar niviśet ||
VaDhS 20.11  vīrahaṇaṃ parastād vakṣyāmaḥ ||
VaDhS 20.12  brahmojjhaḥ kṛcchraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ caritvā punar upayuñjīta vedam ācāryāt ||
VaDhS 20.13  gurutalpagaḥ savṛṣaṇaṃ śiśnam uddhṛtyāñjalav ādhāya dakṣiṇāmukho gacched yatraiva pratihanyāt tatra tiṣṭhed ā pralayam ||
VaDhS 20.14  niṣkālako vā ghṛtābhyaktas taptāṃ sūrmīṃ pariṣvajen maraṇāt pūto bhavatīti vijñāyate ||
VaDhS 20.15  ācāryaputraśiṣyabhāryāsu caivam ||
VaDhS 20.16  yoniṣu gurvīṃ sakhīṃ gurusakhīm apapātraṃ patitāṃ ca gatvā kṛcchrābdapādaṃ caret ||
VaDhS 20.17  etad eva cāṇḍālapatitānnabhojaneṣu tataḥ punarupanayanaṃ vapanādīnāṃ tu nivṛttiḥ ||
VaDhS 20.18  mānavaṃ cātra ślokam udāharanti |
VaDhS 20.18  vapanaṃ mekhalā daṇḍo bhaikṣacaryā vratāni ca {C}|
VaDhS 20.18  etāni tu nivartante punaḥsaṃskārakarmaṇīti {C} ||
VaDhS 20.19  matyā madyapāne tv asurāyāḥ surāyāś cājñāne kṛcchrātikṛcchrau ghṛtaṃ prāśya punaḥsaṃskāraś ca ||
VaDhS 20.20  mūtraśakṛcchukrābhyavahāreṣu caivam ||
VaDhS 20.21  madyabhāṇḍe sthitā āpo yadi kaścid dvijaḥ pibet padmodumbarabilvapalāśānām udakaṃ pītvā trirātreṇaiva śudhyati ||
VaDhS 20.22  abhyāse tu surāyā agnivarṇāṃ tāṃ dvijaḥ piben maraṇāt pūto bhavatīti ||
VaDhS 20.23  bhrūṇahanaṃ vakṣyāmo brāhmaṇaṃ hatvā bhrūṇahā bhavaty avijñātaṃ ca garbham ||
VaDhS 20.24  avijñātā hi garbhāḥ pumāṃso bhavati tatmāt puṃskṛtyā juhvatīti ||{BSPS tatmāt}{F tasmāt}
VaDhS 20.25  bhrūṇahāgnim upasamādhāya juhuyād etāḥ ||
VaDhS 20.26  lomāni mṛtyor juhomi lomabhir mṛtyuṃ vāsaya iti prathamām | tvacaṃ mṛtyor juhomi tvacā mṛtyuṃ vāsaya iti dvitīyām | lohitaṃ mṛtyor juhomi lohitena mṛtyuṃ vāsaya iti tṛtīyām | māṃsaṃ mṛtyor juhomi māṃsena mṛtyuṃ vāsaya iti caturthīm | snāvāni mṛtyor juhomi snāvabhir mṛtyuṃ vāsaya iti pañcamīm |
medo mṛtyor juhomi medasā ṛtyuṃ vāsaya iti ṣaṣṭhīm | asthīni mṛtyor juhomy asthibhir mṛtyuṃ vāsaya iti saptamīmm || majjānaṃ mṛtyor juhomi majjābhir mṛtyuṃ vāsaya ity aṣṭamīm iti ||
VaDhS 20.27  rājārthe brāhmaṇārthe vā saṃgrāme 'bhimukham ātmānaṃ ghātayet ||
VaDhS 20.28  trir ajito vāparāddhaḥ pūto bhavatīti vijñāyate hi ||
VaDhS 20.29  niruktaṃ hy enaḥ kanīyo bhavatīti ||
VaDhS 20.30  tathāpy udāharanti |
VaDhS 20.30  patitaṃ patitety uktvā coraṃ coreti vā punaḥ {C}|
VaDhS 20.30  vacanāt tulyadoṣaḥ syān mithyā dvir doṣatāṃ vrajediti {C} ||
VaDhS 20.31  evaṃ rājanyāṃ hatvāṣṭau varṣāṇi caret ||
VaDhS 20.32  ṣaḍ vaiṣyam ||
VaDhS 20.33  trīṇi śūdram ||
VaDhS 20.34  brāhmaṇīṃ cātreyīṃ hatvā savanagatau ca rājanyavaiśyau ||
VaDhS 20.35  ātreyīṃ vakṣyāmo rajasvalām ṛtusnātām ātreyīm āhuḥ ||
VaDhS 20.36  atra hy eṣyadagpatyam iti ||
VaDhS 20.37  anātreyīṃ rājanyahiṃsāyām ||
VaDhS 20.38  rājanyāṃ vaiśyahiṃsāyām ||
VaDhS 20.39  vaiśyāṃ śūdrahiṃsāyām ||
VaDhS 20.40  śūdrāṃ hatvā saṃvatsaram ||
VaDhS 20.41  brāhmaṇasuvarṇaharaṇe prakīrya keśān rājānam abhidhāvet steno 'smi bho śāstu māṃ bhavān iti tasmai rājaudumbaraṃ śastraṃ dadyāt tenātmānaṃ pramāpayen maraṇāt pūto bhavatīti vijñāyate ||{BSPS bho}{F bhoḥ}
VaDhS 20.42  niṣkālako vā ghṛtākto gomayāgninā pādaprabhṛty ātmānam abhidāhayen maraṇāt pūto bhavatīti vijñāyate ||
VaDhS 20.43  athāpy udāharanti |
VaDhS 20.43  purā kālāt pramītānāṃ pāpād vividhakarmaṇām {C}|
VaDhS 20.43  punar āpannadehānām aṅgaṃ bhavati tac chṛṇu {C}||
VaDhS 20.44  stenaḥ kunakhī bhavati śvitrī bhavati brahmahā {C}|
VaDhS 20.44  surāpaḥ śyāvadantas tu duścarmā gurutalpaga iti {C} ||
VaDhS 20.45  patitasaṃprayoge ca brāhmeṇa vā yaunena vā yās tebhyaḥ sakāśān mātrā upalabdhās tāsāṃ parityāgas taiś ca na saṃvaset ||
VaDhS 20.46  udīciṃ diśaṃ gatvānaśnan saṃhitādhyayanam adhīyānaḥ pūto bhavatīti vijñāyate ||
VaDhS 20.47  tathāpy udāharanti |
VaDhS 20.47  śarīraparitāpena tapasādhyayanena ca {C}|
VaDhS 20.47  mucyate pāpakṛt pāpād dānāc cāpi pramucyate {C}||
VaDhS 20.47  iti vijñāyate vijñāyata iti ||

VaDhS 21.1  śūdraś ced brāhmaṇīm abhigacched vīraṇair veṣṭayitvā śūdram agnau prāsyed brāhmaṇyāḥ śirasi vapanaṃ kārayitvā sarpiṣā samabhyajya nagnāṃ kṛṣṇakharam āropya mahāpatham anusaṃvrājayet pūtā bhavatīti vijñāyate ||
VaDhS 21.2  vaiśyaś ced brāhmaṇīm abhigacchel lohitadarbhair veṣṭayitvā vaiśyam agnau prāsyed brāhmaṇyāḥ śirasi vapanaṃ kārayitvā sarpiṣā samabhyajya nagnāṃ gaurakharam āropya mahāpatham anusaṃvrājayet pūtā bhavatīti vijñāyate ||
VaDhS 21.3  rājanyaś ced brāhmaṇīm abhigacchec charapatrair veṣṭayitvā rājanyam agnau prāsyet sarpiṣā samabhyajya nagnāṃ śvetakharam āropya mahāpatham anusaṃvrājayet pūtā bhavatīti vijñāyate ||
VaDhS 21.4  evaṃ vaiśyo rājanyāyām ||
VaDhS 21.5  śūdraś ca rājanyāvaiśyayoḥ ||
VaDhS 21.6  manasā bhartur aticāre trirātraṃ yāvakaṃ kṣīrodanaṃ vā bhuñjānādhaḥ śayītordhvaṃ trirātrād apsu nimagnāyāḥ sāvitryāṣṭaśatena śirobhir juhuyāt pūtā bhavatīti vijñāyate ||{BSPS kṣīrodanam}{F kṣīraudanam}
VaDhS 21.7  vāksaṃbandha etad eva māsaṃ caritvordhvaṃ māsād apsu nimagnāyāḥ sāvitryāś caturbhir aṣṭaśataiḥ śirobhir juhuyāt pūtā bhavatīti vijñāyate ||
VaDhS 21.8  vyavāye tu saṃvatsaraṃ ghṛtapaṭaṃ dhārayed gomayagarte kuśaprastare vā śayītordhvaṃ saṃvatsarād apsu nimagnāyāḥ sāvitryaṣṭaśatena śirobhir juhuyāt pūtā bhavatīti vijñāyate ||
VaDhS 21.9  vyavāye tīrthagamane dharmebhyas tu nivartate {C}||
VaDhS 21.10  catasras tu parityājyāḥ śiṣyagā gurugā ca yā {C}|
VaDhS 21.10  patighnī ca viśeṣeṇa juṅgitopagatā ca yā {C}||
VaDhS 21.11  yā brāhmaṇī ca surāpī na tāṃ devāḥ patilokaṃ nayantīhaiva sā carati kṣīṇapuṇyāpsu lug bhavati śuktikā vā ||
VaDhS 21.12  brāhmaṇakṣatriyaviśāṃ striyaḥ śudreṇa saṅgatāḥ {C}|{BSPS śudreṇa}{F śūdreṇa}
VaDhS 21.12  aprajātā viśudhyanti prāyaścittena netarāḥ {C}||
VaDhS 21.13  pratilomaṃ careyus tāḥ kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇottaram {C}||
VaDhS 21.14  pativratānāṃ gṛhamedhinīnāṃ satyavratānāṃ ca śucivratānām {C}|
VaDhS 21.14  tāsāṃ tu lokāḥ patibhiḥ samānā gomāyulokā vyabhicāriṇīnām {C}||
VaDhS 21.15  pataty ardhaṃ śarīrasya yasya bhāryā surāṃ pibet {C}|
VaDhS 21.15  patitārdhaśarīrasya niṣkṛtir na vidhīyate {C}||
VaDhS 21.16  brāhmaṇaś ced aprekṣāpūrvaṃ brāhmaṇadārān abhigacched anivṛttadharmakarmaṇaḥ kṛcchro nivṛttadharmakarmaṇo 'tikṛcchraḥ ||
VaDhS 21.17  evaṃ rājanyavaiśyayoḥ ||
VaDhS 21.18  gāṃ ced dhanyāt tasyāś carmaṇārdreṇa pariveṣṭitaḥ ṣaṭmāsān kṛcchraṃ taptakṛcchraṃ vātiṣṭhet ||
VaDhS 21.19  tayor vidhiḥ ||
VaDhS 21.20  tryahaṃ divā bhuṅke naktam aśnāti vai tryaham api {C}|
VaDhS 21.20  tryaham ayācitavratas tryahaṃ na bhug iti kṛcchraḥ {C}||
VaDhS 21.21  tryaham uṣṇāḥ pibed āpas tryaham uṣṇaṃ payaḥ pibet {C}|
VaDhS 21.21  tryaham uṣṇaṃ ghṛtaṃ pītvā vāyubhakṣaḥ paraṃ tryaham {C}||
VaDhS 21.21  iti taptakṛcchraḥ ||
VaDhS 21.22  ṛṣabhavehatau ca dadyāt ||
VaDhS 21.23  athāpy udāharanti |
VaDhS 21.23  traya eva purā rogā īrṣyā anaśanaṃ jarā {C}|
VaDhS 21.23  pṛṣadbastavayaṃ hatvā aṣṭānavatim āharediti {C} ||
VaDhS 21.24  śvamārjāranakulasarpadarduramūṣkān hatvā kṛcchraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ caret kimcid dadyāt ||
VaDhS 21.25  anasthimatāṃ tu sattvānāṃ gomātraṃ rāśiṃ hatvā kṛcchraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ caret kimcid dadyāt ||
VaDhS 21.26  asthimatāṃ tv ekaikam ||
VaDhS 21.27  yo 'gnīn apavidhyet kṛcchraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ caritvā punar ādhānaṃ kārayet ||
VaDhS 21.28  guroś cālīkanirbandhe sacailaṃ snāto guruṃ prasādayet prasādāt pūto bhavatīti vijñāyate ||
VaDhS 21.29  nāstikaḥ kṛcchraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ caritvā viramen nāstikyāt ||
VaDhS 21.30  nāstikavṛttis tv atikṛcchram ||
VaDhS 21.31  etena somavikrayī vyākhyātaḥ ||
VaDhS 21.32  vānaprastho dīkṣābhede kṛcchraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ caritvā mahākakṣe vardhayet ||
VaDhS 21.33  bhikṣukair vānaprasthavat somavṛddhivardhanaṃ svaśāstrasaṃskāraś ca svaśāstrasaṃskāraś ceti ||

VaDhS 22.1  atha khalv ayaṃ puruṣo mithyā vyākaroty ayājyaṃ vā yājayaty apratigrāhyaṃ vā pratigṛhṇāty anannaṃ vāśnāty anācaraṇīyam evācarati ||
VaDhS 22.2  tatra prāyaścittaṃ kuryān na kuryād iti mīmāṃsante ||
VaDhS 22.3  na kuryād ity āhuḥ ||
VaDhS 22.4  na hi karma kṣīyata iti ||
VaDhS 22.5  kuryād ity eva tasmāc chrutinidarśanāt ||
VaDhS 22.6  tarati sarvaṃ pāpmānaṃ tarati brahmahattyāṃ yo 'śvamedhena yajata iti ||{BSPS hattyām}{F hatyām}
VaDhS 22.7  iti cābhiśasto gosavenāgniṣṭutā yajeta ||
VaDhS 22.8  tasya niṣkrayaṇāni japas tapo homa upavāso dānam ||
VaDhS 22.9  upaniṣado vedādayo vedāntāḥ sarvachandaḥsāṃhitā madhūny aghamarṣaṇam atharvaśiro rudrāḥ puruṣasūktaṃ rājanarauhiṇe sāmanī kūṣmāṇḍāni pāvamānyaḥ sāvitrī ceti pāvanāni ||
VaDhS 22.10  athāpy udāharanti |
VaDhS 22.10  vaiśvānarīṃ vrātapatīṃ pavitreṣṭiṃ tathaiva ca {C}|
VaDhS 22.10  sakṛd ṛtau prayuñjānaḥ punāti daśapūruṣamiti {C} ||
VaDhS 22.11  upavāsanyāyena payovratatā phalabhakṣatā prasṛtayāvako hiraṇyaprāśanaṃ somapānam iti medhyāni ||
VaDhS 22.12  sarve śiloccayāḥ sarvāḥ sravantyaḥ puṇyā hradās tīrthāny ṛṣinivāsagoṣṭhapariṣkandhā iti deśāḥ ||
VaDhS 22.13  saṃvatsaro māsaś caturviṃśatyahar dvādaśāhaḥ ṣaḍahas tryahar ahorātrā iti kālāḥ ||
VaDhS 22.14  etāny evānādeśe vikalpena kriyeran ||
VaDhS 22.15  enaḥsu guruṣu gurūṇi laghuṣu laghūni ||
VaDhS 22.16  kṛcchrātikṛcchrau cāndrāyaṇam iti sarvaprāyaścittiḥ sarvaprāyaścittir iti ||

VaDhS 23.1  brahmacārī cet striyam upeyād araṇye catuṣpathe laukike 'gnau rakṣodaivataṃ gardabhaṃ paśum ālabhet ||
VaDhS 23.2  nairṛtaṃ vā caruṃ nirvapet ||
VaDhS 23.3  tasya juhuyāt kāmāya svāhā kāmakāmāya svāhā nairṛtyai svāhā rakṣodevatābhyaḥ svāheti ||
VaDhS 23.4  etad eva retasaḥ prayatnotsarge divā svapne vratāntareṣu vā samāvartanāt ||
VaDhS 23.5  tiryagyonivyavāye śuklam ṛṣabhaṃ dadyāt ||
VaDhS 23.6  gāṃ gatvā śūdrāvadhena doṣo vyākhyātaḥ ||
VaDhS 23.7  brahmacāriṇaḥ śavakarmaṇo vratān nivṛttiḥ ||
VaDhS 23.8  anyatra mātāpitroḥ ||
VaDhS 23.9  sa ced vyādhīyīta kāmaṃ guror ucchiṣṭaṃ bheṣajārthaṃ sarvaṃ prāśnīyāt ||
VaDhS 23.10  guruprayuktaś cen mriyeta trīn kṛcchāṃś cared guruḥ ||{BSPS kṛcchān}{F kṛcchrān}
VaDhS 23.11  brahmacārī cen māṃsam aśnīyād ucchiṣṭabhojanīyaṃ kṛcchraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ caritvā vrataśeṣaṃ samāpayet ||
VaDhS 23.12  śrāddhasūtakabhojaneṣu caivam ||
VaDhS 23.13  akāmatopanataṃ madhu vājasaneyake na duṣyatīti vijñāyate ||
VaDhS 23.14  ya ātmatyāgy abhiśasto bhavati sapiṇḍānāṃ pretakarmachedaḥ ||
VaDhS 23.15  kāṣṭhajalaloṣṭapāṣāṇaśastraviṣarajjubhir ya ātmānam avasādayati sa ātmahā bhavati ||
VaDhS 23.16  athāpy udāharanti |
VaDhS 23.16  ya ātmatyāginaḥ kuryāt snehāt pretakriyāṃ dvijaḥ {C}|
VaDhS 23.16  sa taptakṛcchrasahitaṃ carec cāndrāyaṇavratamiti {C} ||
VaDhS 23.17  cāndrāyaṇaṃ parastād vakṣyāmaḥ ||
VaDhS 23.18  ātmahananādhyavasāye trirātram ||
VaDhS 23.19  jīvann ātmatyāgī kṛchraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ caret trirātraṃ hy upavasen nityaṃ snigdhena vāsasā prāṇān ātmani cāyamya triḥ paṭhed aghamarṣaṇam iti ||{BSPS kṛchraṃ}{F kṛcchraṃ}
VaDhS 23.20  api vaitena kalpena gāyatrīṃ parivartayet ||
VaDhS 23.21  api vāgnim upasamādhāya kūṣmāṇḍair juhuyād dhṛtam ||
VaDhS 23.22  yac cānyan mahāpātakebhyaḥ sarvam etena pūyata iti ||
VaDhS 23.23  athāpy ācamed agniś ca mā manyuś ceti prātarmanasā pāpaṃ dhyātvoṃpūrvāḥ satyāntā vyāhṛtīr japed aghamarṣaṇaṃ vā paṭhet ||{dhyātvoṃpūrvās < dhyātvā oṃpūrvās}
VaDhS 23.24  mānuṣāsthi snigdhaṃ spṛṣṭvā trirātram āśaucam ||
VaDhS 23.25  asnigdhe tv ahorātram ||
VaDhS 23.26  śavānugamane caivam ||
VaDhS 23.27  adhīyānānām antarāgamane 'horātram abhojanam ||
VaDhS 23.28  trirātram abhiṣeko vivāsaś cānyonyena ||
VaDhS 23.29  śvamārjāranakulaśīghragāṇām ahorātram ||
VaDhS 23.30  śvakukkuṭagrāmyaśūkarakaṅkagṛdhrabhāsapārāvatamānuṣakākolūkamāṃsādane saptarātram upavāso niṣpurīṣībhāvo ghṛtaprāśaḥ punaḥ saṃskāraś ca ||
VaDhS 23.31  brāhmaṇas tu śunā daṣṭo nadīṃ gatvā samudragām {C}|
VaDhS 23.31  prāṇāyāmaśataṃ kṛtvā ghṛtaṃ prāśya tataḥ śuciriti {C} ||
VaDhS 23.33  śvacāṇḍālapatitopasparśane sacailaṃ snātaḥ sadyaḥ pūto bhavatīti vijñāyate ||
VaDhS 23.34  patitacāṇḍālārāvaśravaṇe trirātraṃ vāgyatā anaśnanta āsīran ||
VaDhS 23.35  sahasraparamaṃ vā tadabhyasantaḥ pūtā bhavantīti vijñāyate ||
VaDhS 23.36  etenaiva garhitādhyāpakayājakā vyākhyātā dakṣiṇātyāgāc ca pūtā bhavantīti vijñāyate ||
VaDhS 23.37  etenaivābhiśasto vyākhyātaḥ ||
VaDhS 23.38  bhrūṇahattyāyāṃ dvādaśarātram abbhakṣo dvādaśarātram upavaset ||{BSPS hattyāyām}{F hatyāyām}
VaDhS 23.39  brāhmaṇam anṛtenābhiśaṃsya patanīyenopapatanīyena vā māsam abbhakṣaḥ śuddhavatīr āvartayet ||
VaDhS 23.40  aśvamedhāvabhṛthaṃ vā gacchet ||
VaDhS 23.41  etenaiva cāṇḍālīvyavāyo vyākhyātaḥ ||
VaDhS 23.42  athāparaḥ kṛcchravidhiḥ sādhāraṇo vyūḍhaḥ ||
VaDhS 23.43  ahaḥ prātar ahar naktamahar ekam ayācitam {C}|
VaDhS 23.43  ahaḥ parākaṃ tantraikam evaṃ caturahau parau {C}||
VaDhS 23.43  anugrahārthaṃ viprāṇāṃ manur dharmabhṛtāṃ varaḥ {C}|
VaDhS 23.43  bālavṛddhātureṣv evaṃ śiśukṛcchram uvāca ha {C}||
VaDhS 23.44  atha cāndrāyaṇavidhiḥ ||
VaDhS 23.45  māsasya kṛṣṇapakṣādau grāsān adyāc caturdaśa {C}|
VaDhS 23.45  grāsopacayabhojī syāt pakṣaśeṣaṃ samāpayet {C}||
VaDhS 23.45  evaṃ hi śuklapakṣādau grāsam ekaṃ tu bhakṣayet {C}|
VaDhS 23.45  grāsāpacayabhojī syāt pakṣaśeṣaṃ samāpayet {C}||
VaDhS 23.46  atraiva gāyet sāmāni api vā vyāhṛtīr japet {C}||
VaDhS 23.47  eṣa cāndrāyaṇo māsaḥ pavitram ṛṣisaṃstutaḥ {C}|
VaDhS 23.47  anādiṣṭeṣu sarveṣu prāyaścittaṃ vidhīyate {C}||
VaDhS 23.47  vidhīyata iti ||

VaDhS 24.1  athātikṛcchraḥ ||
VaDhS 24.2  tryahaṃ prātas tathā sāyam ayācitaṃ parāka iti kṛcchro yāvat sakṛd ādadīta tāvad aśnīyāt pūrvavat so 'tikṛcchraḥ ||
VaDhS 24.3  kṛcchro 'bbhakṣaḥ sa kṛchrātikṛcchraḥ ||{BSPS kṛchra}{F kṛcchra}
VaDhS 24.4  kṛcchrāṇāṃ vratarūpāṇi ||
VaDhS 24.5  śmaśrukeśān vāpayed bhruvokṣilomaśikhāvarjaṃ nakhān nikṛtyaikavāso 'ninditabhojī [sakṛd bhaikṣam aninditaṃ] triṣavaṇam udakopasparśī daṇḍī kamaṇḍaluḥ strīśūdrasaṃbhāṣaṇavarjī sthānāsanaśīlo 'has tiṣṭhed rātrav asītety āha bhagavān vasiṣṭhaḥ ||
VaDhS 24.6  sa tad yad etad dharmaśāstraṃ nāputrāya nāśiṣyāya nāsaṃvatsaroṣitāya dadyāt ||
VaDhS 24.7  sahasraṃ dakṣiṇā ṛṣabhaikādaśā guruprasādo vā guruprasādo veti ||

VaDhS 25.1  avikhyāpitadoṣāṇāṃ pāpānāṃ mahatāṃ tathā {C}|
VaDhS 25.1  sarveṣāṃ copapāpānāṃ śuddhiṃ vakṣyāmy aśeṣataḥ {C}||
VaDhS 25.2  āhitāgner vinītasya vṛddhasya viduṣo 'pi vā {C}|
VaDhS 25.2  rahasyoktaṃ prāyaścittaṃ pūrvoktam itare janāḥ {C}||
VaDhS 25.3  prāṇāyāmaiḥ pavitraiś ca dānair homair japais tathā {C}|
VaDhS 25.3  nityayuktāḥ pramucyante pātakebhyo na saṃśayaḥ {C}||
VaDhS 25.4  prāṇāyāmān pavitrāṇi vyāhṛtīḥ praṇavaṃ tathā {C}|
VaDhS 25.4  pavitrapāṇir āsīno brahma naityakam abhyaset {C}||
VaDhS 25.5  āvartayan sadā yuktaḥ prāṇāyāmān punaḥ punaḥ {C}|
VaDhS 25.5  ā lomāgrān nakhāgrāc ca tapas tapyatu uttamam {C}||
VaDhS 25.6  nirodhād jāyate vāyurvāyor agnir hi jāyate {C}|
VaDhS 25.6  tāpenāpo 'tha jāyante tato 'ntaḥ śudhyate tribhiḥ {C}||
VaDhS 25.7  na tāṃ tīvreṇa tapasā na svādhyāyair na cejyayā {C}|
VaDhS 25.7  gatiṃ gantuṃ dvijāḥ śaktā yogāt saṃprā vanti yām }
VaDhS 25.8  yogāt saṃprāpyate jñānaṃ yogo dharmasya lakṣaṇam {C}|
VaDhS 25.8  yogaḥ paraṃ tapo nityaṃ tasmād yuktaḥ sadā bhavet {C}||
VaDhS 25.9  praṇave nityayuktas syād vyāhṛtīṣu ca saptasu {C}|
VaDhS 25.9  tripadāyāṃ ca gāyatryāṃ na bhayaṃ vidyate kvacit {C}||
VaDhS 25.10  praṇavād yās tathā vedāḥ praṇave paryavasthitāḥ {C}|
VaDhS 25.10  vāṅmayaṃ praṇavaḥ sarvaṃ tasmāt praṇavam abhyaset {C}||
VaDhS 25.11  ekākṣaraṃ paraṃ brahma pāvanaṃ paramaṃ smṛtam {C}||
VaDhS 25.12  sarveṣām eva pāpānāṃ saṅkare samupasthite {C}|
VaDhS 25.12  abhyāso daśasāhasraḥ sāvitryāḥ śodhanaṃ mahat {C}||
VaDhS 25.13  savyāhṛtiṃ sapraṇavāṃ gāyatrīṃ śirasā saha {C}|
VaDhS 25.13  triḥ paṭhed āyataprāṇaḥ prāṇāyāmaḥ sa ucyate {C}||
VaDhS 25.13  sa ucyata iti ||

VaDhS 26.1  prāṇāyāmān dhārayet trīnyo yathāvidhy atandritaḥ {C}|{BSPS ttrīn}{F trīn}
VaDhS 26.1  ahorātrakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ tatkṣaṇād eva naśyati {C}||
VaDhS 26.2  karmaṇā manasā vācā yad ahnā kṛtam ainasam {C}|
VaDhS 26.2  āsīnaḥ paścimāṃ sandhyāṃ prāṇāyāmair vyapohati {C}||
VaDhS 26.3  karmaṇā manasā vācā yad rātryā kṛtam ainasam {C}|
VaDhS 26.3  uttiṣṭhan pūrvasandhyāṃ tu prāṇāyāmair vyapohati {C}||
VaDhS 26.4  savyāhṛtikāḥ sapraṇavāḥ prāṇāyāmās tu ṣoḍaśa {C}|
VaDhS 26.4  api bhrūṇahanaṃ māsātpunanty aharahaḥ kṛtāḥ {C}||
VaDhS 26.5  japtvā kautsam apety etadvāsiṣṭhaṃ ca tricaṃ prati {C}| {cf. ṚV<1.097.01a>  apa naḥ śośucad agham agne śuśugdhy ā rayim |ṚV<7.080.01a>  prati stomebhir uṣasaṃ vasiṣṭhā gīrbhir viprāsaḥ prathamā abudhran |}
VaDhS 26.5  māhitraṃ śuddhavatyaś ca surāpo 'pi viśudhyati {C}||
VaDhS 26.6  sakṛd japtvāsyavāmīyaṃ śivasaṅkalpam eva ca {C}|
VaDhS 26.6  suvarṇam apahṛtyāpi kṣaṇād bhavati nirmalaḥ {C}||
VaDhS 26.7  haviṣyantīyam abhyasya na tam aṃha iti tricam {C}| {cf. ṚV<10.126.01ab>  na tam aṃho na duritaṃ devāso aṣṭa martyam |}
VaDhS 26.7  sūktaṃ ca pauruṣaṃ japtvā mucyate gurutalpagaḥ {C}||
VaDhS 26.8  api vāpsu nimajjānas trir japed aghamarṣaṇam {C}|
VaDhS 26.8  yathāśvamedhāvabhṛthas tādṛśaṃ manur abravīt {C}||
VaDhS 26.9  ārabhbhayajñād japayajño viśiṣṭho daśabhir guṇaiḥ {C}|{BSPS ārabhbha}{F ārambha}
VaDhS 26.9  upāṃśuḥ syāc chataguṇaḥ sāhasro mānasaḥ smṛtaḥ {C}||
VaDhS 26.10  ye pākayajñāś catvāro vidhiyajñasamanvitāḥ {C}|
VaDhS 26.10  sarve te japayajñasya kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm {C}||
VaDhS 26.11  jāpyenaiva tu saṃsidhyed brāhmaṇo nātra saṃśayaḥ {C}|
VaDhS 26.11  kuryād anyaṃ na vā kuryān maitro brāhmaṇa ucyate {C}||
VaDhS 26.12  jāpināṃ homināṃ caiva dhyāyināṃ tīrthavāsinām {C}|
VaDhS 26.12  na parivasanti pāpāni ye ca snātāḥ śirovrataiḥ {C}||
VaDhS 26.13  yathāgnir vāyunā dhūto haviṣā caiva dīpyate {C}|
VaDhS 26.13  evaṃ jāpyaparo nityaṃ brāhmaṇaḥ saṃpradīpyate {C}||
VaDhS 26.14  svādhyāyādhyāyināṃ nityaṃ nityaṃ ca prayatātmanām {C}|
VaDhS 26.14  japatāṃ juhvatāṃ cāpi vinipāto na vidyate {C}||
VaDhS 26.15  sahasraparamāṃ devīṃ śatamadhyāṃ daśāvarām {C}|
VaDhS 26.15  śuddhikāmaḥ prayuñjīta sarvapāpeṣv api sthitaḥ {C}||
VaDhS 26.16  kṣatriyo bāhuvīryeṇa tared āpadam ātmanaḥ {C}|
VaDhS 26.16  dhanena vaiśyaśudrau tu japaihomair dvijottamaḥ {C}||{BSPS śudrau}{F śūdrau}{BSPS japai}{F japaiḥ }
VaDhS 26.17  yathāśvā rathahīnāḥ syur ratho vāśvair vinā yathā {C}|
VaDhS 26.17  evaṃ tapas tv avidyasya vidyā vāpy atapasvinaḥ {C}||
VaDhS 26.18  yathānnaṃ madhusaṃyuktaṃ madhu vānnena saṃyutam {C}|
VaDhS 26.18  evaṃ tapaś ca vidyā ca saṃyuktaṃ bheṣajaṃ mahat {C}||
VaDhS 26.19  vidyātapobhyāṃ saṃyuktaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ japanaityakam {C}|
VaDhS 26.19  sadāpi pāpakarmāṇam eno na pratiyujyate {C}||
VaDhS 26.19  eno na pratiyujyata iti ||

VaDhS 27.1  yady akāryaśataṃ sāgraṃ kṛtaṃ vedaś ca dhāryate {C}|
VaDhS 27.1  sarvaṃ tat tasya vedāgnir dahaty agnir ivendhanam {C}||
VaDhS 27.2  yathā jātabalo vahnir dahaty ārdrān api drumān {C}|
VaDhS 27.2  tathā dahati vedāgniḥ karmajaṃ doṣam ātmanaḥ {C}||
VaDhS 27.3  hatvāpi sa imāṃl lokān bhuñjāno 'pi yatas tataḥ {C}|
VaDhS 27.3  ṛgvedaṃ dhārayan vipro nainaḥ prāpnoti kimcana {C}||
VaDhS 27.4  na vedabalam āśritya pāpakarmaratir bhavet {C}|
VaDhS 27.4  ajñānāc ca pramādāc ca dahyate karma netarat {C}||
VaDhS 27.5  tapas tapyati yo 'raṇye munir mūlaphalāśanaḥ {C}|
VaDhS 27.5  ṛcam ekāṃ ca yo 'dhīte tac ca tāni ca tat samam {C}||
VaDhS 27.6  itihāsapurāṇābhyāṃ vedaṃ samupabṛṃhayet {C}|
VaDhS 27.6  bibhety alpaśrutād vedo mām ayaṃ prahariṣyati {C}||
VaDhS 27.7  vedābhyāso 'nvahaṃ śaktyā mahāyajñakriyākramaḥ {C}|
VaDhS 27.7  nāśayaty āśu pāpāni mahāpātakajāny api {C}||
VaDhS 27.8  vedoditaṃ svakaṃ karma nityaṃ kuryād atandritaḥ {C}|
VaDhS 27.8  tad dhi kurvan yathāśaktyā prāpnoti paramāṃ gatim {C}||
VaDhS 27.9  yājanādhyāpanād yaunāt tathaivāsatpratigrahāt {C}|
VaDhS 27.9  vipreṣu na bhaved doṣo jvalanārkasamo hi saḥ {C}||
VaDhS 27.10  śaṅkāsthāne samutpanne abhojyabhojyasaṃjñake {C}|
VaDhS 27.10  āhāraśuddhiṃ vakṣyāmi tan me nigadataḥ śṛṇu {C}||
VaDhS 27.11  akṣāralavaṇāṃ rūkṣāṃ pibedbrāhmīṃ suvarcalām {C}|
VaDhS 27.11  trirātraṃ śaṅkhapuṣpīṃ ca brāhmaṇaḥ payasā saha {C}||
VaDhS 27.12  palāśabilbapatrāṇi kuśān padmān udumbarān {C}|
VaDhS 27.12  kvāthayitvā pibed āpas trirātreṇaiva śudhyati {C}||
VaDhS 27.13  gomūtraṃ gomayaṃ kṣīraṃ dadhi sarpiḥ kuśodakam {C}|
VaDhS 27.13  ekarātropavāsaś ca śvapākam api śodhayet {C}||
VaDhS 27.14  gomūtraṃ gomayaṃ caiva kṣīraṃ dadhi ghṛtaṃ tathā {C}|
VaDhS 27.14  pañcarātraṃ tadāhāraḥ pañcagavyena śudhyati {C}||
VaDhS 27.15  yavān vidhinopayuñjānaḥ pratyakṣeṇaiva śudhyati {C}|
VaDhS 27.15  viśuddhabhāve śuddhāḥ syur aśuddhe tu sarāgiṇaḥ {C}||
VaDhS 27.16  haviṣyān prātar āśāṃs trīn sāyam āśāṃs tathaiva ca {C}|
VaDhS 27.16  ayācitaṃ tathaiva syād upavāsatrayaṃ bhavet {C}||
VaDhS 27.17  atha cet tvarate kartuṃ divasaṃ mārutāśanaḥ {C}|
VaDhS 27.17  rātrau jalāśaye vyuṣṭaḥ prājāpatyena tat samam {C}||
VaDhS 27.18  sāvitryaṣṭasahasraṃ tu japaṃ kṛtvotthite ravau {C}|
VaDhS 27.18  mucyate pātakaiḥ sarvair yadi na brahmahā bhavet {C}||
VaDhS 27.19  yo vai stenaḥ surāpo vā bhrūṇahā gurutalpagaḥ {C}|
VaDhS 27.19  dharmaśāstram adhītyaiva mucyate sarvapātakaiḥ {C}||
VaDhS 27.20  duritānāṃ duriṣṭānāṃ pāpānāṃ mahatāṃ tathā {C}|
VaDhS 27.20  kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ caiva sarvapāpapraṇāśanam {C}||
VaDhS 27.21  ekaikaṃ vardhayet piṇḍaṃ śukle kṛṣṇe ca hrāsayet {C}|
VaDhS 27.21  amāvāsyāṃ na bhuñjīta evaṃ cāndrāyaṇo vidhiḥ {C}||
VaDhS 27.21  evaṃ cāndrāyāṇo vidhir iti ||

VaDhS 28.1  na strī duṣyati jāreṇa na vipro vedakarmaṇā {C}|
VaDhS 28.1  nāpo mūtrapurīṣeṇa nāgnir dahanakarmaṇā {C}||
VaDhS 28.2  svayaṃ vipratipannā vā yadi vā vipravāsitā {C}|
VaDhS 28.2  balāt kāropabhuktā vā corahastagatāpi vā {C}||
VaDhS 28.3  na tyājyā dūṣitā nārī nāsyās tyāgo vidhīyate {C}|
VaDhS 28.3  puṣpakālam upāsīta ṛtukālena śudhyati {C}||
VaDhS 28.4  striyaḥ pavitram atulaṃ naitā duṣyanti karhicit {C}|
VaDhS 28.4  māsi māsi rajo hy āsāṃ duṣkṛtāny apakarṣati {C}||
VaDhS 28.5  pūrvaṃ striyaḥ surair bhuktāḥ somagandharvavahnibhiḥ {C}|
VaDhS 28.5  gacchanti mānuṣān paścānaitā duṣyanti dharmataḥ {C}||{BSPS paścānaitā}{F paścān na etāḥ}
VaDhS 28.6  tāsāṃ somo 'dadac chaucaṃ gandharvaḥ śikṣitāṃ giram {C}|
VaDhS 28.6  agniś ca sarvamedhyatvaṃ tasmān niskalmaṣāḥ striyaḥ {C}||
VaDhS 28.7  trīṇi striyaḥ pātakāni loke dharmavido viduḥ {C}|{BSPS striyas}{F striyās}
VaDhS 28.7  bhartṛvadho bhrūṇahatyā svasya garbhasya pātanam {C}||
VaDhS 28.8  vatsaḥ prasravaṇe medhyaḥ śakuniḥ phalapātane {C}|
VaDhS 28.8  striyaś ca ratisaṃsarge śvā mṛgagrahaṇe śuciḥ {C}||
VaDhS 28.9  ajāśvā mukhato medhyās gāvo medhyās tu sarvataḥ {C}|| [Bühler seems to read: gāvo medhyāś ca pṛṣṭhataḥ; also cf. Devala quoted in KK śuddhi-kāṇḍa p176:1]
VaDhS 28.10  sarvavedapavitrāṇi vakṣyāmy aham ataḥ param {C}|
VaDhS 28.10  yeṣāṃ japaiś ca homaiś ca pūyante nātra saṃśayaḥ {C}||
VaDhS 28.11  aghamarṣaṇaṃ devakṛtaṃ śuddhavatyas taratsamāḥ {C}|
VaDhS 28.11  kūṣmāṇḍāni pāvamānyo durgāsāvitrir eva ca {C}||
VaDhS 28.12  atīṣaṅgāḥ padastobhāḥ sāmāni vyāhṛtīs tathā {C}|
VaDhS 28.12  bhāruṇḍāni sāmāni ca gāyatraṃ raivataṃ tathā {C}||
VaDhS 28.13  puruṣavrataṃ ca bhāsaṃ ca tathā devavratāni ca {C}|
VaDhS 28.13  abliṅgaṃ bārhaspatyaṃ tu vāksūktaṃ madhvṛcas tathā {C}||
VaDhS 28.14  śatarudriyam atharvaśiras trisuparṇaṃ mahāvratam {C}|
VaDhS 28.14  gosūktaṃ cāśvasūktaṃ ca śuddhāśuddhīye sāmanī {C}||
VaDhS 28.15  trīṇ ājyadohāni rathantaraṃ ca agner vrataṃ vāmadevyaṃ bṛhac ca {C}|
VaDhS 28.15  etāni japtāni punanti jantūnjātismaratvaṃ labhate yadīcchet {C}||
VaDhS 28.16  agner apatyaṃ prathamaṃ suvarṇaṃ bhūr vaiṣṇavī sūryasutāś ca gāvaḥ {C}|
VaDhS 28.16  tāsām anantaṃ phalam aśnuvīta yaḥ kāṃcanaṃ gāṃ ca mahīṃ ca dadyāt {C}||
VaDhS 28.17  uparundhanti dātāraṃ gaur aśvaḥ kanakaṃ kṣitiḥ {C}|
VaDhS 28.17  aśrotriyasya viprasya hastaṃ dṛṣṭvā nirākṛteḥ {C}||
VaDhS 28.18  vaiśākhyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ tu brāhmaṇān sapta pañca vā {C}|
VaDhS 28.18  tilān kṣaudreṇa saṃyuktān kṛṣṇān vā yadi vetarān {C}||
VaDhS 28.19  prīyatāṃ dharmarājeti yad vā manasi vartate {C}|
VaDhS 28.19  yāvad jīvakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ tatkṣaṇād eva naśyati {C}||
VaDhS 28.20  suvarṇanābhaṃ kṛtvā tu sakhuraṃ kṛṣṇamārgajam {C}|
VaDhS 28.20  tilaiḥ pracchādya yo dadyāt tasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu {C}||
VaDhS 28.21  sasuvarṇaguhā tena saśailavanakānanā {C}|
VaDhS 28.21  caturvaktrā bhaved dattā pṛthivī nātra saṃśayaḥ {C}||
VaDhS 28.22  kṛṣṇājine tilān kṛtvā hiraṇyaṃ madhusarpiṣī {C}|
VaDhS 28.22  dadāti yas tu viprāya sarvaṃ tarati duṣkṛtāmiti {C} ||
VaDhS 28.22  sarvaṃ tarati duṣkṛtam iti ||

VaDhS 29.1  dānena sarvān kāmān avāpnoti ||
VaDhS 29.2  cirajīvitvaṃ brahmacārī rūpavān ||
VaDhS 29.3  ahiṃsy upapadyate svargam ||
VaDhS 29.4  agnipraveśād brahmalokaḥ ||
VaDhS 29.5  maunāt saubhāgyam ||
VaDhS 29.6  nāgādhipatir udakavāsāt ||
VaDhS 29.7  nirujaḥ kṣiṇakoṣaḥ ||
VaDhS 29.8  toyadaḥ sarvakāmasamṛddhaḥ ||
VaDhS 29.9  annapradātā sucakṣuḥ smṛtimān ||
VaDhS 29.10  medhāvī sarvato 'bhayadātā ||
VaDhS 29.11  goprayukte sarvatīrthopasparśanam ||
VaDhS 29.12  śayyāsanadānād antaḥpurādhipatyam ||
VaDhS 29.13  chatradānād gṛhalābhaḥ ||
VaDhS 29.14  gṛhaprado nagaram āpnoti ||
VaDhS 29.15  upānatpradātā yanam āsādayate ||{BSPS yanam}{E yānam}
VaDhS 29.16  athāpy udāharanti |
VaDhS 29.16  yat kimcit kurute pāpaṃ puruṣo vṛttikarṣitaḥ {C}|
VaDhS 29.16  api gocarmamātreṇa bhūmidānena śudhyati {C}||
VaDhS 29.17  viprāyācamanārthaṃ tu dadyāt pūrṇakamaṇḍalum {C}|
VaDhS 29.17  pretya tṛptiṃ parāṃ prāpya somapo jāyate punaḥ {C}||
VaDhS 29.18  anaḍuhāṃ sahasrāṇāṃ dattānāṃ dhūryavāhinām {C}|
VaDhS 29.18  supātre vidhivaddānaṃ kanyādānena tat samam {C}||
VaDhS 29.19  trīṇy āhur atidānāni gāvaḥ pṛthvī sarasvatī {C}|
VaDhS 29.19  atidānaṃ hi dānānāṃ vidyādānaṃ tato 'dhikam {C}||
VaDhS 29.20  ātyantikaphalapradaṃ mokṣasaṃsāramocanam {C}|
VaDhS 29.20  yo 'nasūyur imaṃ vidvān ācāram anuvartate {C}||
VaDhS 29.21  śraddadhānaḥ śucir dānto dhārayec chruṇuyād api {C}|
VaDhS 29.21  vihāya sarvapāpāni nākapṛṣṭhe mahīyateiti {C} ||
VaDhS 29.21  nākapṛṣṭhe mahīyata iti ||

VaDhS 30.1  dharmaṃ carata mādharmaṃ satyaṃ vadata mānṛtam {C}|
VaDhS 30.1  dīrghaṃ paśyata mā hrasvaṃ paraṃ paśyata māparam {C}||
VaDhS 30.2  brāhmaṇo bhavaty agniḥ ||
VaDhS 30.3  agnir vai brāhmaṇa iti śruteḥ ||
VaDhS 30.4  tac ca katham ||
VaDhS 30.5  tatra sado brāhmaṇasya śarīraṃ vediḥ saṃkalpo yajñaḥ paśur ātmā raśanā buddhiḥ sado mukham āhavanīyaṃ nābhyām udaro 'gnir gārhapatyaḥ prāṇo 'dhvaryur apāno hotā vyāno brahmā samāna udgātātmendriyāṇi yajñapātrāṇi ya evaṃ vidvān indriyair indriyārthaṃ juhotīty api ca -- kāṭhake vijñāyate ||
VaDhS 30.6  athāpy udāharanti |
VaDhS 30.6  pāti trāti ca dātāram ātmānaṃ caiva kilbiṣāt {C}|
VaDhS 30.6  vedendhanasamṛddheṣu hutaṃ vipramukhāgniṣu {C}||
VaDhS 30.7  na skandate na vyathate nainam adhyāpatec ca yat {C}|
VaDhS 30.7  variṣṭham agnihotrāt tu brāhmaṇasya mukhe hutam {C}||
VaDhS 30.8  dhyānāgniḥ satyopacayanaṃ kṣāntyāhutiḥ sruvaṃ hrīḥ puroḍāśam ahiṃsā saṃtoṣo {C}|{BSPS saṃtoṣo}{F saṃtoṣaḥ}
VaDhS 30.8  yūpaḥ kṛcchraṃ bhūtebhyo 'bhayadākṣiṇyam iti kṛtvā kratu mānasaṃ yāti kṣayaṃ budhaḥ {C}||
VaDhS 30.9  jīryanti jīryataḥ keśā dantā jīryanti jīryataḥ {C}|
VaDhS 30.9  jīvanāśā dhanāśā ca jīryato 'pi na jīryati {C}||
VaDhS 30.10  yā dustyajā durmatibhir yā na jīryati jīryataḥ {C}|
VaDhS 30.10  yāsau prāṇāntiko vyādhis tāṃ tṛṣṇāṃ tyajataḥ sukhamiti {C} ||
VaDhS 30.11  namo 'stu mitrāvaruṇayor urvaśyātmajāya śatayātave vasiṣṭhāya vasiṣṭhāyeti ||


%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%


Vaikhānasa Dharmasūtra 

(= Vaikhānasa Smārtasūtra 8-10)

Digital text produced by Hiromichi Hikita, and collated by Yuuko Matsuda and Yasuke Ikari, March 1992, May 1996.
Converted to Unicode plain text without hyphens, sandhi resolution, and analytical markers by Timothy Lubin, October 2021.

Text: edited by W. Caland, Vaikhanasasmārtasūtram, the domestic rules of the Vaikhānasa school belonging to the Black Yajurveda, Calcutta 1927.

VaikhDhS 01.01.<112.1>  atha varṇāśramadharmaṃ
VaikhDhS 01.01.<112.1-2>  brāhmaṇakṣatriyavaiśyaśūdrāmukhabāhūrupādeṣu jātāś catvāro varṇā
VaikhDhS 01.01.<112.2-3>  yasmād brāhmaṇo asya mukham āsīd iti śrutiḥ
VaikhDhS 01.01.<112.3-4>  pūrveṣāṃ trayāṇāṃ niṣekādyāḥ saṃskārāvidhīyante
VaikhDhS 01.01.<112.4>  teṣāṃ dvijanmanāṃ vedādhikāraḥ
VaikhDhS 01.01.<112.4-5>  tasmād brāhmaṇasyādhyayanādhyāpanayajanayājanadānapratigrahāṇi ṣaṭ karmāṇi bhavanti
VaikhDhS 01.01.<112.6>  kṣattriyavaiśyayor yajanādhyayanadānāni
VaikhDhS 01.01.<112.6-7>  kṣatriyasya prajāpālayaduṣṭanigrahayuddhāḥ
VaikhDhS 01.01.<112.7>  vaiśyasya pāśupālyakusīdavāṇijyāni
VaikhDhS 01.01.<112.8>  śūdrasya dvijamanāṃ śuśrūṣā kṛṣiś caiva
VaikhDhS 01.01.<112.8-9>  brāhmaṇasyāśramāś catvāraḥ kṣatriyasyādyās trayo vaiśyasya dvāv eva
VaikhDhS 01.01.<112.9-10>  tadāśramiṇaś catvāro brahmacārī gṛhasthovānaprastho bhikṣur iti
VaikhDhS 01.02.<112.11-13>  upanīto brahmacārīmekhalopavītājinadaṇḍadhārī snātvā tarpaṇaṃ brahmayajñaṃ sāyaṃprātaḥ sandhyopāsanasamiddhomau cakurvan guroḥ pādāv upasaṃgṛhya nityābhivandī vratenādhyayanaṃ karoti
VaikhDhS 01.02.<112.13-15>  sthite gurau stheyād utthite pūrvam utthāyavrajantam anugacched āsīne śayāne ca niyukto nīcair anvāsanaśayane kuryād
VaikhDhS 01.02.<112.15-16>  anukto yat kiñcit karma nācarati
VaikhDhS 01.02.<112.16>  anukto 'pi svādhyāyanityakarmāṇy ācared
VaikhDhS 01.02.<112.16-113.3>  uṣṇāmbusnānadantadhāvanāñjanānulepanagandhapuṣpopānaṭchatradivāsvāparetaḥskandastrīdarśanasparśanamaithunānikāmakrodhalobhamohamadamātsaryahiṃsādīni varjayitvā sadāśuśrūṣur guroḥ priyahitakarmāṇi kurvīta
VaikhDhS 01.02.<113.3>  adveṣī vākcittānukūlaḥ priyaṃ satyaṃ vadaty
VaikhDhS 01.02.<113.3-4>  ārto 'py asatyāpriyaṃ nindaṃ nācakṣīta
VaikhDhS 01.02.<113.4-5>  madhumāṃsamatsyarasaśuktādyabhojyabhojanavarjī bhaikṣācaraṇaṃ kṛtvā guruṇānujñāto bhaikṣānnam aśnīyād
VaikhDhS 01.02.<113.5-6>  guruvṛddhadīkṣitānām ākhyāṃ na brūyād
VaikhDhS 01.02.<113.6-7>  gurvabhāve tatputre ca guruvat karmācarati
VaikhDhS 01.03.<113.8-9>  brahmacāriṇaś caturvidhā gāyatro brāhmaḥprājāpatyo naiṣṭhiketi
VaikhDhS 01.03.<113.9-10>  gāyatropanayanād ūrdhvaṃ trirātramakṣāralavaṇāśī gāyatrīm adhītyā sāvitravratasamāpter atra vratacārī
VaikhDhS 01.03.<113.10-14>  brāhmaḥ sāvitravratād ūrdhvamanabhiśastāpatitānāṃ gṛhasthānāṃ gṛheṣu bhaikṣācaraṇaṃ vedavratacaraṇaṃ ca kṛtvādvādaśa samā viṃśati samā vā gurukule sthitvā vedān vedau vedaṃ vā sūtrasahitamadhyayanaṃ kṛtvā gārhasthyānusaraṇaṃ kuryāt
VaikhDhS 01.03.<113.14-15>  prājāpatyaḥ snātvā nityakarmabrahmacaryaśīlo nārāyaṇaparāyaṇo vedavedāṅgārthān vicārya dārasaṃgrahaṇaṃ karoti
VaikhDhS 01.03.<113.15-16>  prājāpatye trisaṃvatsarād ūrdhvaṃ na tiṣṭhedity ṛṣayo vadanti
VaikhDhS 01.03.<113.16-114.3>  naiṣṭhikaḥ kāṣāyaṃ dhātuvastram ajinaṃvalkalaṃ vā paridhāya jaṭī śikhī vā mekhalī daṇḍī sūtrājinadhārībrahmacārī śucir akṣāralavaṇāśī yāvad ātmano viprayogas tāvad gurukule sthitvāniveditabhaikṣabhojī bhavati
VaikhDhS 01.04.<114.4-7>  dārān saṃgṛhya gṛhastho 'pisnānādiniyamācāro nityam aupāsanaṃ kṛtvā pākayajñayājī vaiśvadevahomānte gṛhāgataṃ guruṃsnātakaṃ ca pratyutthāyābhivandyāsanapādyācamanāni pradāyaghṛtadadhikṣīramiśraṃ madhuparkaṃ ca dattvānnādyair yathāśakti bhojayati
VaikhDhS 01.04.<114.7-10>  bhikṣūn brahmacāriṇo 'tithīn vedavidaḥ śrotriyān pitṛvyācāryaṛtvijmātulaśvaśurādīn abhyāgatān bālavṛddhān anāthārtādhvaśrāntāṃś ca yathārthaṃ pūjayaty
VaikhDhS 01.04.<114.10>  aśakto 'py agraṃ bhikṣāṃ vā sodakaṃ dattvāśeṣaṃ bhuñjīta
VaikhDhS 01.04.<114.11-13>  dayāsatyaśaucācārayutaḥsvādhyāyatarpaṇābhyām ṛṣīn yajñnabalihomajalapuṣpādyair devāñ chrāddhaiḥ putraiś ca pitṛṛnbalinā bhūtān annādyair manuṣyāṃś ca nityam arcayed
VaikhDhS 01.04.<114.13>  ṛṇatrayeṇa mukto 'nṛṇo bhavati
VaikhDhS 01.05.<114.14>  gṛhasthāś caturvidhā vārtāvṛttiḥ śālīnavṛttiryāyāvaro ghorācārikaś ceti
VaikhDhS 01.05.<114.14>  vārtāvṛttiḥ kṛṣigorakṣyavāṇijyopajīvī
VaikhDhS 01.05.<114.14-15>  śālīnavṛttir niyamair yutaḥ pākayajñairṣṭvāgnīn adhāya pakṣe pakṣe darśapūrṇamāsayājī caturṣu caturṣu māseṣu cāturmāsyayājīṣaṭsu ṣaṭsu māseṣu paśubandhayājī pratisaṃvatsaraṃ somayājī ca
VaikhDhS 01.05.<115.1-3>  yāyāvaro haviryajñaiḥ somayajñaiś ca yajateyājayaty adhīte 'dhyāpayati dadāti pratigṛhṇāti ṣaṭkarmanirato nityamagniparicaraṇam atithibhyo 'bhyāgatebhyo 'nnādyaṃ ca kurute
VaikhDhS 01.05.<115.3-7>  ghorācāriko niyamair yukto yajate nayājayaty adhīte nādhyāpayati dadāti na pratigṛhṇāty uñcchavṛttim upajīvatinārāyaṇaparāyaṇaḥ sāyaṃprātar agnihotraṃ hutvā mārgaśīrṣajyeṣṭhamāsayor asidhārāvrataṃvanauṣadhibhir agniparicaraṇaṃ karoti
VaikhDhS 01.06.<115.8-11>  gṛhasthaḥ sapatnīkaḥ pañcāgnibhis tretāgnibhirvā gṛhād vanāśramaṃ yāsyann āhitāgnir anāhitāgniś cāupāsanam araṇyāmāropya gṛhe mathitvā śrāmaṇakīyavidhānenādhāyāghāraṃ hutvāśrāmaṇakāgnim ādāya tṛtīyam āśramaṃ gacchet
VaikhDhS 01.06.<115.11-12>  pūrvavad agnyālayaprokṣaṇollekhanādikarma kuryāt
VaikhDhS 01.06.<115.12-14>  tṛtīyām api vediṃ parimṛjya ṣaḍaṅgulāgnerdarbhair grathite 'dhas tridhākṛtaṃ rajjuvat- mūle baddhaṃ ṣaṭtriṃśad aṅgulapramāṇaṃparistaraṇakūrcaṃ kṛtvā madhyavedyāṃ paristṛṇāti śrāmaṇakaṃ
VaikhDhS 01.06.<115.15-17>  śrāmaṇakayajñaṃ yajñadaivaviśvān devānity antam āvāhyājyaṃ nirūpya śrāmanakāya svāhā śrāmaṇakayajñāya svāhāyajñadaivataviśvebhyo devebhyaḥ svāhety antaṃ hutvā caruṃ juhuyād ity ādhāraviśeṣaḥ
VaikhDhS 01.06.<115.18-116.1>  śrāmaṇakāgneś cordhvavedirdvātriṃśadaṅgulyāyatā caturaṅgulivistāronnatā
VaikhDhS 01.06.<116.1-2>  madhyamā tatparigatā pañcāṅgulivistārācaturaṅgulotsedhā
VaikhDhS 01.06.<116.2>  adhastād ūrdhvavedivistāronnatā tṛtīyā vedir
VaikhDhS 01.06.<116.2-5>  dvādaśāṅgulaṃ madhye nimnaṃ trivedisahitaṃkuṇḍaṃ kṛtvādhāya vanastho nityam aupāsanavat sāyaṃprātarāhutīr hutvā mahāvyāhṛtibhiḥ śrāmaṇakāgniṃ juhuyād
VaikhDhS 01.06.<116.5>  patnīko dārair agnibhir vinā vanaṃ gacchet
VaikhDhS 01.07.<116.6>  vānaprasthāḥ sapatnīkāpatnīkāś ceti
VaikhDhS 01.07.<116.6-7>  sapatnīkāś caturvidhā
VaikhDhS 01.07.<116.7>  audumbaro vairiñco vālakhilyaḥ phenapaś ceti
VaikhDhS 01.07.<116.7-11>  audumbaro 'kṛṣṭaphalāvāpyauṣadhibhojī mūlaphalāśī vāaṇahiṅgulaśunamadhumatsyamāṃsapūtyannadhānyāmlaparasparśanaparapākavarjī devaṛṣipitṛmanuṣyapūjī vanacaro grāmabahiṣkṛtaḥsāyaṃprātaragnihotraṃ hutvā śrāmaṇakāgnihomaṃ vaiśvadevahomaṃ kurvaṃs tapaḥ samācarati
VaikhDhS 01.07.<116.12>  śrāmaṇakāgnim ekam evādhāya juhotīty eke
VaikhDhS 01.07.<116.12-15>  vairiñcaḥ prātryāṃ diśaṃ prekṣate tāṃ diśaṃgatvā tatra priyaṅguyavaśyāmākanīvārādibhir labdhaiḥ svakīyān atithīṃś capoṣayitvāgnihotraśrāmaṇakavaiśvadevahomī nārāyaṇaparāyaṇas tapaḥśīlobhavati
VaikhDhS 01.07.<116.15-17>  vālakhilyo jaṭādharaścīravalkalavasano 'rkāgniḥ kārttikyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ puṣkalaṃ bhuktam utsṛjyānyathā śeṣān māsān upajīvyatapaḥ kuryāt
VaikhDhS 01.07.<116.18>  asya sūryaivāgnir bhavatīty āmananti
VaikhDhS 01.07.<116.18-117.2>  phenapa uddaṇḍaka unmattako nirodhakaḥ śīrṇapatitapattrāhārī cāndrāyaṇavrataṃ caran pṛthivīśāyīnārāyaṇaṃ dhyāyan mokṣam eva prārthayate
VaikhDhS 01.08.<117.3>  apatnīkā bahuvidhāḥ
VaikhDhS 01.08.<117.3-11>  kālāśikā uddaṇḍasaṃvṛttā aśmakuṭṭā udagraphalino dantolūkhalikā uñchavṛttikāḥ saṃdarśanavṛttikāḥ kapotavṛttikāmṛgacārikā hastādāyinaḥ śailaphalakādino 'rkadagdhāśino bailvāśinaḥkusumāśinaḥ pāṇḍupattrāśinaḥ kālāntarabhojina ekakālikāś catuṣkālikāḥkaṇṭakaśāyino vīrāsanaśāyinaḥ pañcāgnimadhyaśāyino dhūmāśinaḥpāṣāṇaśāyino 'bhyavagāhina udakumbhavāsino mauninaś cāvākśirasaḥ sūryapratimukhā ūrdhvabāhukā ekapādasthitāś ceti vividhācārā bhavantīti vijñāyate
VaikhDhS 01.09.<117.12-13>  atha bhikṣukā mokṣārthinaḥ kuṭīcakābahūdakā haṃsāḥ paramahaṃsāś ceti caturvidhā bhavanti
VaikhDhS 01.09.<117.13-15>  tatra kuṭīcakā gautamabhāradvājayājñavalkyahārītaprabhṛtīnām āśrameṣv aṣṭau grāsāṃṣ caranto yogamārgatattvajñā mokṣam evaprārthayante
VaikhDhS 01.09.<117.15-118.1>  bahūdakās tridaṇḍakamaṇḍalukāṣāyadhātuvastragrahaṇaveṣadhāriṇo brahmarṣigṛheṣu cānyeṣu sādhuvṛtteṣu māṃsalavaṇaparyuṣitānnaṃ varjayantaḥ saptāgāreṣubhaikṣaṃ kṛtvā mokṣam eva prārthayante
VaikhDhS 01.09.<118.1-4>  haṃsā nāma grāme caikarātraṃ nagarepañcarātraṃ vasantas tadupari na vasanto gomūtragomayāhāriṇo vā māsopvāsino vānityacāndrāyaṇavratino nityam utthānam eva prārthayante
VaikhDhS 01.09.<118.4-5>  paramahaṃsā nāma vṛkṣaikamūle śūnyāgāreśmaśāne ā vāsinaḥ sāmbarā digambarā vā
VaikhDhS 01.09.<118.5-6>  na teṣāṃ dharmādharmau satyānṛteśuddhyaśuddhyādi dvaitaṃ
VaikhDhS 01.09.<118.6-7>  sarvasamāḥ sarvātmanaḥ samaloṣṭakāñcanāḥsarvavarṇeṣu bhaikṣācaraṇaṃ kurvanti
VaikhDhS 01.09.<118.7-8>  brāhmaṇānāṃ cāturāśramyaṃ kṣatriyāṇāṃtrayāśramyaṃ vaiśyānāṃ dvyāśramyaṃ vihitaṃ
VaikhDhS 01.09.<118.8-9>  tatphalaṃ hi sakāmaṃ niṣkāmaṃ ceti dvividhaṃbhavati
VaikhDhS 01.09.<118.9-11>  sakāmaṃ nāmeha saṃsāre 'bhivṛddhiṃ jñātvā putralābhādyabhikāṅkṣaṇam anyat svargādiphalakāṅkṣaṇaṃ vā
VaikhDhS 01.09.<118.11-12>  niṣkāmaṃ nāma kiñcid anabhikāṅkṣya yathāvihitānuṣṭhānam iti
VaikhDhS 01.09.<118.12-13>  tatra niṣkāmam dvividhaṃ bhavati pravṛttirnivṛttiś ceti
VaikhDhS 01.09.<118.13-15>  pravṛttir nāma saṃsāram anādṛtyasaṅkhyajñānaṃ samāśritya prāṇāyāmāsanapratyāhāradhāraṇāyukto vāyujayaṃ kṛtvāṇimādyaiśvaryaprāpaṇaṃ
VaikhDhS 01.09.<118.15-119.1>  tat punar api tapaḥkṣayāj janmaprāpakatvādvyādhibāhulyāc ca nādriyante paramarṣayo
VaikhDhS 01.09.<119.1-6>  nivṛttir nāma lokānām anityatvaṃ jñātvā paramātmano 'nyan na kiñcid astīti saṃsāram anādṛtya cchitvā bhāryāmayaṃ pāśaṃjitendriyo bhūtvā śarīraṃ vihāya kṣetrajñaparamātmanor yogaṃ kṛtvātīndriyaṃsarvajagadbījam aśeṣaviśeṣaṃ nityānandam amṛtarasapānavat sarvadā tṛptikaraṃ paraṃ jyotiḥpraveśakam iti vijñāyate
VaikhDhS 01.10.<119.7-8>  nivṛttyācārabhedād dhi yoginas trividhābhavanti sāraṅgā ekārthyā visaragāś ceti
VaikhDhS 01.10.<119.8-9>  anirodhakā nirodhakā mārgagā vimārgagāśceti caturvidhā sāraṅgā
VaikhDhS 01.10.<119.9-10>  dūragā adūragā bhrūmadhyagā asaṃbhaktāḥsaṃbhaktāś cety ekārthyāḥ pañcadhā bhavanti
VaikhDhS 01.10.<119.10-11>  na saṅkhyāvanto visaragāḥ
VaikhDhS 01.10.<119.11-13>  tatra sāraṅgāḥ sāraṃ kṣetrajñas taṃgacchantīti sāraṅgās teṣv anirodhakā ahaṃ viṣṇur iti dhyātvā ye caranti teṣāṃprāṇāyāmādayo na santi
VaikhDhS 01.10.<119.13-14>  ye tu nirodhakās teṣāṃ prāṇāyāmapratyāhāradhāraṇādayaḥ ṣoḍaśa kalāḥ santi
VaikhDhS 01.10.<119.14-15>  ye mārgagās teṣāṃ ṣaḍ eva prāṇāyāmādayo
VaikhDhS 01.10.<119.15-17>  ye vimārgās teṣāṃ yamaniyamāsanaprāṇāyāmapratyāhāradhāraṇādhyānasamādhayaścety aṣṭāṅgān kalpayanto dhyeyam apy anyathā kurvanti
VaikhDhS 01.11.<120.1>  athaikārṣyā
VaikhDhS 01.11.<120.1>  eka eva ṛṣir yeṣāṃ te ekarṣyāḥ
VaikhDhS 01.11.<120.1-2>  teṣu ye dūragās teṣām ayaṃ mārgaḥ
VaikhDhS 01.11.<120.2-5>  piṅgalayā nāḍikayādityamaṇḍalam anupraviśyatatrasthena puruṣeṇa saṃyujya tataś candramaṇḍalaṃ tatrasthena puruṣeṇa tatovidyutaṃ tatrasthena puruṣeṇa punaḥ krameṇa vaikuṇṭhasāyujyaṃ yanti
VaikhDhS 01.11.<120.5>  ye 'dūragās teṣām ayaṃ dharmaḥ
VaikhDhS 01.11.<120.5-7>  kṣetrajñaparamātmanor yogaṃ kṣetrajñadvāreṇakārayitvā tatraiva samastavināśaṃ dhyātvākāśavat sattāmātro 'ham iti dhyāyanti
VaikhDhS 01.11.<120.7-10>  bhrūmadhyagāḥ kṣetrajñaparamātmanor yoge sattvarūpāgnidvāreṇa bhrūmadhyaṃ nītvā pañcabhyo 'ṅgṣṭādibhyaḥsthānebhyākarṣaṇaṃ punaḥ piṅgalādvāreṇa niṣkramaṇaṃ pralayāntaṃkṣetrajñayogāntaṃ vā kurvanti
VaikhDhS 01.11.<120.10-11>  asaṃbhaktā nāma manasā dhyānaṃ kurvanti
VaikhDhS 01.11.<120.11>  tatpratipādanāgamaṃ śrotreṇa śṛṇvanti
VaikhDhS 01.11.<120.11-12>  cakṣuṣā devatākāraṃ paśyanti
VaikhDhS 01.11.<120.12>  ghrāṇena gandham anubhavanti
VaikhDhS 01.11.<120.12-13>  pāṇinā devatāṃ namaskurvanti
VaikhDhS 01.11.<120.13-14>  saṃbhaktā nāma brāhmaṇaḥ sarvavyāpakatvādyuktam ayuktaṃ yo 'sau paramātmā tat sa vyāpyākāśavat tiṣṭhati
VaikhDhS 01.11.<120.15>  tasmād brahmaṇo 'nyan na kutracid ātmānaṃpratipadyate 'sau
VaikhDhS 01.11.<120.16>  bhrūmadhyagatasyāpi saṃśayān niṣpramāṇamevety uktaṃ
VaikhDhS 01.11.<120.16-121.1>  tasmād brahmavyatiriktam anyan nopapadyate
VaikhDhS 01.11.<121.1-2>  vividhasaraṇād vividhadarśanātkupathagāmitvād visaragāḥ
VaikhDhS 01.11.<121.2-3>  purā prajāpatir upadeśagūhanārthaṃvisaragapakṣaṃ dṛṣṭavān
VaikhDhS 01.11.<121.3>  taṃ dṛṣṭvā munayo 'pi mohaṃ jagmuḥ kiṃ punar manuṣyāḥ
VaikhDhS 01.11.<121.4>  visaragapaśūnām ahaṃkārayuktānāṃ janmāntareṣumuktir nāsmiñ janmani
VaikhDhS 01.11.<121.5>  tasmād visaragapakṣo nānuṣṭheyo
VaikhDhS 01.11.<121.5-8>  kecid visargāḥ kāyakleśāt kecin mantrajapātkecid yena kenacid dhyānena kecid yena kenacid akṣareṇa kecid vāyujayād anye paramātmanākṣetrajaṃ saṃyojya dhyāyanty ete paramātmasaṃyogam eva necchanti
VaikhDhS 01.11.<121.8-9>  hṛdisthaiva puruṣeti vadanti
VaikhDhS 01.11.<121.9-10>  kecin na kiñcid dhyānam itiyathoktānuṣṭhānaṃ yogam iti jñātvā muktim icchanti
VaikhDhS 01.11.<121.10-11>  teṣāṃ visaragapaśūnāṃantareṣu muktir nāsmiñ janmani
VaikhDhS 01.11.<121.11-12>  tasminn eva janmani mokṣakāṅkṣiṇāvisaragapakṣo nānuṣṭheyaḥ
VaikhDhS 01.11.<121.12-15>  saguṇe brahmaṇi buddhiṃ niveśyapaścān nirguṇaṃ brahmāśritya yatnaṃ kuryād iti vijñāyate
VaikhDhS 02.01.<122.1>  atha vanasthasya śrāmaṇakavidhānaṃ
VaikhDhS 02.01.<122.1-3>  gṛhasthaḥ somayājī putraṃ pautraṃ ca dṛṣṭvā tatputrādīn gṛhe saṃsthāpya mauṇḍyaṃ kṛtvā prājāpatyaṃ kṛcchraṃ caret
VaikhDhS 02.01.<122.3-4>  vasante śuklapakṣe puṇyakṣetre patnyā sārdhaṃvanāśramaṃ yāti
VaikhDhS 02.01.<122.4-5>  pūrvasmin divase kṛtasnānaḥ saṃkalpyakuśodakaṃ pītvopavāsaṃ kuryād
VaikhDhS 02.01.<122.5-6>  aupāsanahomaṃ hutvāgnimayaṃ te yonir ityaraṇyām āropayed
VaikhDhS 02.01.<122.6-8>  darśapūrṇamāsavidhānena darbhādīn saṃgṛhyapūrvavat paristaraṇakūrcān paridhīn samidhoveṇudaṇḍopavītakamaṇḍaluvalkalādīn saṃbharati
VaikhDhS 02.01.<122.8>  pūrvoktavidhināgnikuṇḍaṃ kuryād
VaikhDhS 02.01.<122.8-10>  aparasmin divase vaiśvānarasūktenāgniṃmathitvā prajvālyāgnāyāhy| upāvarohety agniṃ nidhāya pūrvavat- śrāmaṇakāgnyāghāraṃ juhoti
VaikhDhS 02.01.<122.10-13>  praṇamyāgniṃ pariṣicyāgne prāyaścitte tvam iti pañcaprāyaścittaṃ hutvāpohiraṇyāvamānair ātmānaṃ prokṣyabrahmadaivatyaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ pañcavāruṇaṃ ca pradhānān vyāhṛtyantaṃ yajet
VaikhDhS 02.02<122.14-17>  agneḥ pratīcyāṃ dvau kuśau pūrvāgrau nyasyordhve 'śmānaṃ nidhāya tat savitur vareṇyam iti dakṣiṇapādāṅguṣṭhāgreṇāśmānam adhitiṣṭhet tejovatsava iti valkalam ajinaṃ cīraṃ vā paridhāya pūrvavan mekhalādīṃs trīṇy upavītāny uttarīyaṃ kṛṣṇājinaṃ cādadāty {on the reading of the mantra, cf. Cal p122  n4}
VaikhDhS 02.02<122.17-123.1>  ācamya svasti devety agniṃ pradakṣiṇaṃpraṇāmaṃ ca kṛtvāsīta
VaikhDhS 02.02<123.1-3>  śaṃ no vedīr iti svamūrdhni prokṣya jayān abhyātānān rāṣṭrabhṛto vyāhṛtīś ca hutvājyaśeṣaṃ prāṇāyāmena prāśnīyād 
VaikhDhS 02.02<123.3-5>  yoge yoga iti dvir ācamya śatam in nu śaradetipraṇāmam āgantrā samagan mahīti pradakṣiṇaṃ cādityasya kurvīta
VaikhDhS 02.02<123.5-6>  rāṣṭrabhṛd asīty ūrdhvāgraṃ kūrcaṃ gṛhṇīyāt
VaikhDhS 02.02<123.6-10>  oṃ bhūs tat savitur oṃ bhuvo bhargo devasyoṃ suvar dhiyo yo na iti paccho vyastām oṃ bhūr bhuvas tat savitur oṃ suvardhiyo yo na ity ardharcām oṃ bhūr bhuvaḥ suvas tat savitur iti samastāṃ casāvitrīṃ japtvā vanāśramaṃ praviśya brahmacaryavrataṃ saṃkalpayet
VaikhDhS 02.03<123-11>  tatpatnī ca tathā brahmacāriṇī syāt
VaikhDhS 02.03<123.11-14>  svayam evāgniṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtyājyena prājāpatyaṃ dhātādīn mindāhutī vicchinnam aindraṃ vaiśvadevaṃ vaiśṇavaṃ bāhyaṃ viṣṇornukādīn prājāpatyasūktaṃ tadvratabandhaṃ ca punaḥ pradhānān hutvāprājāpatyavrataṃ badhnāti
VaikhDhS 02.03<123.14-16>  sthitvā devasya tvā yo me daṇḍa iti dvābhyāṃ pañcasaptanavānyatamaiḥ parvabhir yuktaṃ keśāntāyataṃvāpy avakraṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ dvidaṇḍam ādadāti
VaikhDhS 02.03<123.16-17>  yena devā iti kamaṇḍalumṛdgrahiṇyau pūrvavadupānaṭchatre ca gṛhṇāty

VaikhDhS 03.12.<142.11-12>  viprād vaiśyāyām ambaṣṭhaḥkakṣyājīvyāgneyanartako dhvajaviśrāvī śalyacikitsī
VaikhDhS 03.12.<142.13>  jarāt kumbhakāraḥ kulālavṛttir nāpito nābherūrdhvavaptā ca
VaikhDhS 03.12.<142.14-15>  kṣatriyād vaiśyāyāṃ madguḥ śreṣṭhitvaṃ prāpto mahānarmākhyaś ca vaiśyavṛttiḥ kṣātram karma nācarati
VaikhDhS 03.12.<142.15>  gūḍhād āśviko 'śvakrayavikrayī syāt
VaikhDhS 03.13.<143.1-2>  viprāc chūdrāyāṃ pāraśavo bhadrakālīpūjanacitrakarmāṅgavidyātūryaghoṣaṇamardanavṛttir
VaikhDhS 03.13.<143.2-3>  jārotpanno niṣādo vyāḍādimṛgahiṃsākārī
VaikhDhS 03.13.<143.3>  rājanyataḥ śūdrāyām ugraḥ sudaṇḍyadaṇḍanakṛtyo
VaikhDhS 03.13.<143.3-4>  jārāc chūlikaḥ śūlārohaṇādiyātanākṛtyo
VaikhDhS 03.13.<143.4-5>  vaiśyataḥ śūdrāyāṃ cūcukaḥ kramukatāmbūlaśarkarādikrayavikrayī
VaikhDhS 03.13.<143.5-6>  gūḍhāt kaṭakāraḥ kaṭakārī ceti
VaikhDhS 03.13.<143.6-7>  tato 'nulomād anulomāyāṃ jātaś cānulomaḥpitur mātur vā jātaṃ vṛttiṃ bhajeta
VaikhDhS 03.13.<143.7-9>  kṣatriyād viprakanyāyāṃ mantravat- jātaḥ sūtaḥpratilomeṣu mukhyo 'yaṃ mantrahīnopanīto dvijadharmahīno
VaikhDhS 03.13.<143.9>  asya vṛttir dharmānubodhanaṃ rājño 'nnasaṃskāraś ca
VaikhDhS 03.13.<143.10-11>  jāreṇa mantrahīnajo rathakārodvijatvavihīnaḥ śūdrakṛtyo 'śvānāṃ poṣaṇadamanādiparicaryājīvī
VaikhDhS 03.13.<143.11-13>  vaiśyād brāhmaṇyāṃ māgadhaḥ śūdrair apyabhojyān no 'spṛśyaḥ sarvavandī praśaṃsākīrtanagānapreṣaṇavṛttir
VaikhDhS 03.13.<143.13>  gūḍhāc cakrī lavaṇatailavikretā syāt
VaikhDhS 03.14.<143.14-15>  vaiśyān nṛpāyām āyogavas tantuvāyaḥ paṭakartā vastrakāṃsyoapjīvī
VaikhDhS 03.14.<143.15>  gūḍhācārāt pulindo 'raṇyavṛttirduṣṭamṛgasattvaghātī
VaikhDhS 03.14.<143.16-17>  śūdrāt kṣatriyāyāṃ pulkasaḥ kṛtakāṃ vāārkṣāṃ vā surāṃ hutvā pācako vikrīnīta
VaikhDhS 03.14.<143.17>  coravṛttād velavo janbhananartanagānakṛtyaḥ
VaikhDhS 03.14.<144.1-2>  śūdrād vaiśyāyāṃ vaidehakaḥ śūdrāspṛśyastair apy abhojyānno vanyavṛttir ajamahiṣagopālas tadrasān vikrayī
VaikhDhS 03.14.<144.2-3>  cauryāc cakriko lavaṇatailapiṇyākajīvī
VaikhDhS 03.14.<144.3-6>  śūdrād brāhmaṇyāṃ caṇḍālaḥsīsakālāyasābharaṇo vardhrābandhakaṇṭhaḥ kakṣerīyukto yatas tataś caran sarvakarmabahiṣkṛtaḥ pūrvāhṇe grāmādau vīthyām anyatrāpi malāny apakṛṣya bahir apohayati
VaikhDhS 03.14.<144.6>  grāmād bahir dūre svajātīyair nivaset
VaikhDhS 03.14.<144.6-7>  madhyāhnāt paraṃ grāme na viśaty| ayaṃ
VaikhDhS 03.14.<144.7>  viśec ced rājñā vadhyo
VaikhDhS 03.14.<144.7-8>  anyathā bhrūṇahatyām avāpnoty
VaikhDhS 03.14.<144.8>  antarālvratyāś ca
VaikhDhS 03.14.<144.8-10>  cūcukād viprāyāṃ takṣako 'spṛśyojhallarīhasto dārukāraḥ suvarṇakāro 'yaskāraḥ kāṃsyakāro vā
VaikhDhS 03.14.<144.10>  kṣatriyāyāṃ matsyabandhur matsyabandhī
VaikhDhS 03.14.<144.10-11>  vaiśyāyāṃ sāmudraḥ samudrapaṇyajīvīmatsyaghātī ca syāt
VaikhDhS 03.15.<144.12-13>  ambaṣṭhād viprāyāṃ nāvikaḥsamudrapaṇyamatsyajīvī samudralaṅghanāṃ nāvaṃ plāvayati
VaikhDhS 03.15.<144.13-14>  kṣatriyāyām adhonāpito nābher adho romavaptā
VaikhDhS 03.15.<144.14>  madgor viprāyāṃ veṇuko veṇuvīṇāvādī
VaikhDhS 03.15.<144.14-15>  kṣatriyāyāṃ karmakaraḥ karmakārī
VaikhDhS 03.15.<144.15>  vaidehakād viprāyāṃ carmakāraś carmajīvī
VaikhDhS 03.15.<144.16>  nṛpāyāṃ sūcikaḥ sūcīvedhanakṛtyavān
VaikhDhS 03.15.<144.16-17>  āyogavād viprāyāṃ tāmras tāmrajīvī
VaikhDhS 03.15.<144.17>  nṛpāyāṃ khanakaḥ khananajīvī
VaikhDhS 03.15.<144.17-18>  khananān nṛpāyām udbandhakaḥ śūdrāspṛśyo vastranirṇejakaḥ
VaikhDhS 03.15.<144.18-19>  pulkasād viprāyāṃ rajako vastrāṇāṃrajonirṇejakaś 
VaikhDhS 03.15.<144.19-145.2>  caṇḍālād viprāyāṃ śvapacaḥ caṇḍālavaccihnayukto nityanindyaḥ sarvakarmabahiṣkāryo nagaryādau malāpohakaḥ śmaśāne vasan heyapātragrāhī pretam abandhukaṃ visṛjeta
VaikhDhS 03.15.<145.3-4>  vadhyān hatvā tadvastrādigrāhīparādhīnāhāro bhinnapātrabhojī śvamāṃsabhakṣī carmavāravāṇavāṇijyakārī syāt
VaikhDhS 03.15.<145.4-5>  tasmān nikṛṣṭe sute samutpanne patito naṣṭoghorān narakān vrajati
VaikhDhS 03.15.<145.5-6>  satputro narakebhyas trāyakaḥ pitṛṛnpāvayitvā | śubhāṃl lokān nayati
VaikhDhS 03.15.<145.6-8>  tasmād brāhmaṇādyāḥ savarṇāyāṃ vidhivat putram utpādayeyur iti vikhanāḥ
